Data model: [§64](../data-model/README.md#64-sprint-4-token-art-c-art).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Glyphs `src/client/common/features/entities/creature-art/` | `index.ts`: `CREATURE_ART` = type → `{viewBox, path}` (14, game-icons.net, glyph path only); `credits.ts`: type → `{icon, author, url}`, CC BY 3.0; `ATTRIBUTION.md` the same 14 rows. Path strings only (licence test) | FR-ENT-28, -29 |
| Draw (`layers/TokensLayer.tsx`, `shape.ts`) | No image + a type → the glyph (65 % of the footprint, `creatureGlyph` white) on `creature.<type>` in a disc (round), rounded square (square) or bare (none, `artBackdropOf`); `"initials"` / null → initials as before; an image wins. `map-token` attr `art` = `image` \| `<type>` \| `initials` (`tokenArtOf`); glyph node `map-token-art`, attr `creature` | FR-ENT-28, FR-MAP-35 |
| Canvas colours (`scenes-map/theme.ts`) | `colors.creature[<type>]` read from `--creature-<type>`, falling back to visual-direction's proposed values (`CREATURE_COLORS`); `colors.creatureGlyph` from `--creature-glyph`, else `#ffffff` | OQ-ENT-30 |
| DM token menu (registry line `entTokenMenu`, order 26, after Shape) | **Art ▸ Creature type (‹Type›)** (the definition's current type via `useDefinitions`; disabled without one) / **Initials**: each sends `map.updateToken {tokenId, creatureType}` on select; checked = the token's `creatureType` (type → Creature type, `"initials"` → Initials, null → neither). Test IDs `ent-token-menu-art`, `ent-token-menu-art-type` (+ `data-value` = the type), `ent-token-menu-art-initials` | FR-ENT-28 |
| Creature form Type (`dm/features/combat-tracker/creature-form.tsx`) | Select `cbt-form-type`: None + the 14 types; Derive / Edit prefill from the source's `statBlock.type`; None omits `type` from the whole `PUT` / `POST` stat block | FR-CBT-43, FR-ENT-26 |
| Credits (`common/features/compendium/credits.tsx`) | Footer **Credits** in the Compendium list view (both clients, `cmp-credits`, always shown) and the DM Creatures window (`ent-list-credits`) → About dialog `cmp-about-dialog`: each imported source (`cmp-about-source` + `data-record-id` = key, `cmp-about-text`) then **Token art** (`cmp-about-token-art`, 14 × `cmp-about-token-art-row` + `data-value` = type). Sources read on open: one `GET /api/compendium/search` page (empty `q`), then one `GET /entries/:id` per non-homebrew source label. The entry view's **About** opens the same dialog with its own source | FR-CMP-19, FR-ENT-29 |
| Bundle (measured, in-memory Vite build, gzip -9 of exactly these modules) | Glyphs 13.1 KB gz in the lazy `canvas-layers` chunk (both clients; raw 27.4 KB); credits 1.9 KB + `art.ts` 0.3 KB in shared chunks; the DM menu 0.7 KB in the DM entry. 16.0 KB gz upper bound in all | §12 (≈ 12.7 KB expected) |

## 82. Sprint 5: party core (P0)

From [party-board](../specs/party-board.md#66-sprint-5-loot-tab-present-uploads) FR-PARTY-31–35, -40 and [assets-pipeline](../specs/assets-pipeline.md#61-sprint-5-image-attach-control-and-attachments) FR-ASSET-24–26 (DM gate #256; seams S1, S5, S11, S14; rulings P #23, #26). **Built by `build/party-s5-core`**: server only (popup and DM controls: P2; attach control: P1). Data side: [data model §65](../data-model/README.md#65-sprint-5-party-core-p0).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Record type `handout-present` (`src/server/features/party-board/records.ts`) | `{id, campaignId, handoutId, playerId, presentedAt}`; campaign scope, **not lazy** (in every snapshot). Visibility `presentVisibility` (`@shared/party-board/visibility`): DM `full`; a player `full` iff `playerId` is theirs, else `hidden` (≤ 1 row per player frame). The provider reads only the viewer's row for a player |
| `party.presentHandout {handoutId, to}` (DM) | Strict; `to = {mode: all} \| {mode: players, playerIds}` (1–20 unique). Handout of the campaign, else `not_found`. Targets: `all` = every player who can see it; `players` = each a player of the campaign (`not_found`) who can see it, else `conflict` "‹name› can't see this handout — share it first". No target → `conflict` "Share it first". Per target: their current row (any handout) deleted, a new row inserted. Result `{presentIds}` (target order). No notification |
| `party.closePresent {presentId} \| {handoutId}` (DM, player) | Strict, exactly one key. `presentId`: a player their own row only (another player's → `not_found`), the DM any row of the campaign. `handoutId`: DM only (player → `forbidden`), every row of it. Nothing left → `ack`, no event |
| `party.shareHandout {…, present?}` (DM) | `present: true` → after the share, same tx, `presentHandout` for the **gainers only** (FR-PARTY-06's G; review #23). G empty / unchanged share → no present, no event. `mode: none` + `present` → `invalid`. Event order: `HandoutShared`, `NotificationCreated` (FR-PARTY-06), `HandoutPresented`. The losers' rows of the handout ride on `HandoutShared` as `handout-present` deletes (FR-PARTY-35) |
| `party.deleteHandout` | Every row of the handout deleted, as `handout-present` deletes on `HandoutDeleted` |
| `HandoutPresented {handoutId, playerIds}` | Logged. Changes: per target the old row's delete, the new row's upsert. A target receives only their own rows; other players at most an empty-change frame |
| `HandoutPresentClosed {handoutId, playerIds, by: dm \| player}` | Logged. Changes: `handout-present` → null per row (DM; each closed player their own delete) |
| `POST /api/assets` `listed` field | Optional multipart `listed` = `"true"` \| `"false"` (absent = listed), else 400 `invalid` (path `listed`). Parsed in the assets feature route (`routes.ts`); no `app.ts` change (S11). DM only as before (player token → 401) |
| `assets.ingest` `listed?` | false = attachment: row `listed = false`; `AssetIngested {…, listed: false}` logged with **no** record change. Same bytes later with `listed: true` → `existing` + `AssetListed {assetId}` (logged; `asset` upsert, DM only). `listed: false` never unlists. Record type `asset` serves listed rows only (snapshot) |
| `AssetListed {assetId}` (`src/shared/events/assets-pipeline.ts`) | Logged, not undoable; change `asset` upsert (before null) |
| Fixtures | `ingestStep(file, {listed: false})` (ruling #26; a string option is still the name); the attachment's id via `r.id(key, "asset")` (the loader records `AssetIngested` ids since C5 core, [§86](#86-sprint-5-compendium-entry-images-c5-core); P0's `fixtureAttachmentId` removed). Units `handout-attachment`, `handout-present-1`; bundle `party-s5`; both units in `session-sim-9` |
| CON-44 | `handout-present` DM 1,459 B at 6 rows, 259 B per player, 4,819 B at 20 rows (bound 5,100); attachments 0 B. Ceilings DM 1.5 MiB, players 512 KiB hold |

## 83. Sprint 5: combat core (R0)

From [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#9-api--events) (FR-CBT-88, -91, -94, -97–100; [ADR-0053](../architecture/adr/0053-apply-hit.md); #252) and [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#9-api--events) (FR-MAP-65, -66). **Built by `fix/s5-combat-core`**. Data side: [data model §66](../data-model/README.md#66-sprint-5-combat-core-r0).

**Intents** (strict zod, `@shared/combat-tracker/schemas`)

| Intent | Who | Validation | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cbt.applyHit {cardId, toHitTotal (−20…99), targets [{targetId, hit, multiplier}] (1–20, unique)}` *(new)* | DM | encounter ended / card not pending / already ruled → `conflict` ("Hit already applied"); card without `toHit`, no `hit: true`, duplicate or > 20 targets → `invalid`; unknown or removed target → `not_found` | `{rulingId}` | FR-CBT-88 |
| `cbt.setSide {combatantId, side: ally \| enemy, secret?}` *(new)* | DM | no started encounter → `conflict` "No encounter running"; unknown, other campaign or not in the started encounter → `not_found`; removed allowed; same side and (secret, or already shown to players) → no-op ack | — | FR-CBT-99 |
| `cbt.applyCard` + `damageRollId: string \| null` | DM | ≠ the card's damage `rollId` (null = none) → `conflict` "Damage just arrived — check and Apply again", nothing written; **required since R1** ([§88](#88-sprint-5-late-damage-r1)) | as before | FR-CBT-94 |
| `cbt.rollDamage` result + `unused?: true` | DM, player | written by R1 ([§88](#88-sprint-5-late-damage-r1)) | as before | FR-CBT-92 |
| REST encounter bodies: entries + `side?` | DM | `ally` \| `enemy`, absent = `enemy` | as before | FR-CBT-98 |

**Record types**

| Type | Player visibility | Snapshot | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hit-ruling` *(new)* | `audience: dm` | the started encounter's rulings whose card is pending, card order; live: Apply, Dismiss and End list its delete, Undo's reopen its upsert (the DB row stays) | FR-CBT-88, -91 |
| `combatant` + `side`, `publicSide` | redactions gain `side = publicSideOf(c)`; the true `side` and `publicSide` never reach players | as before | FR-CBT-100 |
| `encounter-entry` + `side` | DM only (as before) | as before | FR-CBT-98 |
| `token` + `trail` | DM: every token's; players: on player tokens only (`ownerPlayerId ≠ null`), key absent on every other token | as before | FR-MAP-66 |

**Domain events** (`src/shared/events/combat-tracker.ts`)

| Event | Changes | Player sees | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|
| `HitApplied {cardId, encounterId, rulingId, entryIds}` *(new)* | `hit-ruling` create; one `game-log-entry` per target (`cbt.attack`, `awaitingDamage`, `public` per FR-CBT-36) | the lines they may see ("… = 15 — DM: hit."); never the ruling; no card upsert | no |
| `CombatantSideChanged {encounterId, combatantId, side, secret}` *(new)* | `combatant`; one `cbt.side` `game-log-entry` | the upsert iff their side changed (an unchanged redaction is never sent); the line iff `public`; **no frame at all** otherwise (`quietWhenUnchanged`, R4 walkthrough fix) | no |
| `AttackApplied` (the apply's last) / `CombatEntryUndone` (the undo's last) / `NotificationDismissed` (`note.dismiss`, and End's dismissals) | + the card's `hit-ruling` delete / upsert / delete (dismissal: the kind's `onClose`, §26) | nothing (DM only) | as before |
| `TokenMoved` / `TokenRevealed` | the one `token` upsert is read **after** the token-changed hooks, so it carries the appended / reset `trail` | per token visibility; `trail` on player tokens only | no |
| `TurnChanged` (Next, Prev, Start round 1), `EncounterEnded` | + one `token` upsert per cleared trail (`clearTrails`) | as above | no |

**Helpers and hooks**

- scenes-map server (`src/server/features/scenes-map/trail.ts`): `appendTrail(env, token, from, to)` (nothing while `hidden` or for a move that stays put; starts at `from`; keeps the newest 21 cells), `resetTrail(env, token)`, `clearTrails(env, sceneId) → RecordChange[]`. Server code only.
- Combat's token-changed hook (`combat-tracker.combatant-copies`) runs the trail step first: a move of any token on the started encounter's scene (bound or not, any mover) appends; a reveal resets. A guard-refused move never reaches it.
- Log-entry kind `cbt.side` (`@shared/combat-tracker/side-kind.ts`, appended to `src/shared/dice-roll-log/kinds.ts`): players "Goblin 2 is now an ally"; DM "DM — Goblin 2 is now an ally" (+ " (secret)" from `dmPayload`).

## 84. Sprint 5: kept-template right-click menu (K1)

Built by `fix/tpl-menu` ([FR-SCN-48–51](../specs/scene-tools.md#6-functional-requirements), [FR-MAP-71](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements); #250 [1], #256). **No wire change**: the menu sends the existing intents of [§68](#68-sprint-4-map-tools-core-mc) / FR-SCN-26; no new event, record, signal or field. Data side: [data model §67](../data-model/README.md#67-sprint-5-kept-template-right-click-menu-k1).

| Client action | Sends | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Right-click (button-2 press + release < 4 px) on a kept template this client may edit, under any tool | nothing (the menu opens at the pointer; no `map.laser` frame) | FR-SCN-48, FR-MAP-71 (4) |
| Right-*drag* (≥ 4 px) starting on such a template | `map.laser` as today, its first point = the press point (gated by `signalsLive`) | FR-MAP-71 (4) |
| Player right press on a token with no own template under it | nothing (no laser, FR-MAP-24) | FR-MAP-71 (2) |
| Menu **Delete** | `scn.deleteTemplate {templateId}` (no confirm, not optimistic); `forbidden` / `not_found` → Toast ("Template already removed" for `not_found`) | FR-SCN-50 |
| Menu **Edit…** | nothing: the AoE tool becomes active with the template selected (panel, handles) | FR-SCN-48 |
| Menu **Hide** / **Reveal** (DM) | `scn.updateTemplate {templateId, hidden}` | FR-SCN-48, -26 |

- **Routing** (`scenes-map/right-press.ts`, pure `rightPress`): door / prop node menus (`cancelBubble`) → not live: nothing → token (DM: token menu on release; player: a layer menu hit there, else nothing) → the drawing tool's `pointer.menu` → first layer `contextMenu` hit (deferred: < 4 px opens, ≥ 4 px lasers) → laser. The menu needs `live`; the laser `signalsLive`.
- **Hit test** `templateAt` (`scene-tools/templates.ts`): topmost editable kept template whose true outline (`templateArea`, uncut) contains the point or lies within 6 screen px; hidden ones only for the DM with DM-only items on; none while the DM's Templates switch is off; a blind player only their own.
- **DOM:** `scn-template-ctx` (`data-record-id` = template id), items `scn-template-ctx-delete`, `-edit`, `-hide` (`data-hidden` `true` \| `false`; label Reveal when `true`). Items disabled while not live; the menu closes when its template leaves what this client may edit on the shown scene (deleted, cleared, hidden for this player, scene switch, snapshot), on Esc or a click outside.
- **Visibility / leaks:** unchanged (the intents' rules, FR-SCN-26): a player's Delete of another player's template → `forbidden`, nothing to anyone; of a hidden one → `not_found`. Tested: `FR-SCN-50 delete for everyone` (harness DM, `player-1`, `player-2`, reconnect convergence + leak scan).
- **CON-44:** no snapshot growth (no new record or field).

## 85. Sprint 5: image attach control (P1)

From [assets-pipeline](../specs/assets-pipeline.md#61-sprint-5-image-attach-control-and-attachments) FR-ASSET-20–23, -27 and [party-board](../specs/party-board.md#66-sprint-5-loot-tab-present-uploads) FR-PARTY-39 (DM gate #256; seams S4, S5; rulings P #17, #18). **Built by `build/image-attach`**: client only; no record type, event, intent or schema change (the `listed` field and attachments: [§82](#82-sprint-5-party-core-p0)). No data-model change.

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| `ImageAttach` (`@ui/features/assets-pipeline/attach/ImageAttach.tsx`) | Props `max`, `count`, `onAdd(assetId)`, `owner`, `pasteTarget?`, `dropTarget?` (`RefObject<HTMLElement>`, may sit inside a portal: native listeners on the ref's element, re-attached when it changes), `library?` (default true), `disabled?`. Free = `max − count − queued − in flight`. `onAdd` once per finished image, in order, straight from the upload response; the same id may repeat (dedupe is the caller's). Renders nothing without an uploader context |
| Queue (`attach/queue.ts`, DOM-free) | One `POST /api/assets` at a time, multipart `listed=false` (+ `name` for pastes). Pre-check by MIME (PNG / JPEG / WebP / SVG, else "Unsupported file type"; > 25 MB "File over 25 MB"); beyond the free slots "Only ‹n› more images" (first n sent) / "No more images (‹max›)". Progress "Uploading ‹i› of ‹n› · ‹pct› %", then "Processing…". Per-file error lines `‹name›: ‹message›`, cleared by the next add; the queue continues. 401 drops the rest |
| Paste / drop (`attach/paste.ts`, `attach/handlers.ts`) | Paste: no plain text, or a single `http(s)://` token, + ≥ 1 `image/*` file → `preventDefault`, images queued as "Pasted image ‹yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm›" (local); other text → the browser's paste, no upload; no slot / disabled → "No more images (‹max›)". Drop: drags carrying `Files` outline the target (the picker's dashed outline, `data-attach-dragging`) and queue on drop; other drags ignored |
| Uploader context (`@ui/features/assets-pipeline/uploader.tsx`) | `AttachUploaderProvider` / `useAttachUploader()`: `{upload(file, {listed, name?}, handlers)}`. Provided **only** by the DM client root (`DmAttachUploaderProvider`, `@dm/features/assets-pipeline/uploader.tsx`: DM secret; 401 → secret reset). The player client mounts none: no control, picker Upload disabled |
| Picker + upload (`@ui/features/assets-pipeline/AssetPicker.tsx`, `upload.ts`) | Moved from `@dm`; the picker uploads through the context with `listed=true` (its own uploads stay in the library). `uploadAsset(file, secret, {listed, name?}, handlers)` |
| Test IDs | `asset-attach` (+ `data-owner`, `data-remaining`), `asset-attach-upload`, `asset-attach-file-input`, `asset-attach-library`, `asset-attach-hint` / `asset-attach-full`, `asset-attach-progress`, `asset-attach-error` + `asset-attach-error-line`. `asset.test`: `asset-test-attached-id` (+ `data-record-id`), `asset-test-dialog-open`, `asset-test-dialog`, `asset-test-dialog-text`, `asset-test-dialog-drop`. Handout editor: `party-editor-duplicate` |
| Callers | Handout editor: `max` 10, `owner` `party-editor`, paste and drop target = the whole editor form, `disabled` while a save is in flight; an id already in the strip → not added, "Already in this handout". `asset.test` (test mode): one control (`owner` `asset-test`) + one in a Dialog (`asset-test-dialog`, targets inside the portal) |
| CON-44 | No record, no snapshot bytes (attachments are 0 B, §82) |

## 86. Sprint 5: compendium entry images (C5 core)

From [compendium](../specs/compendium.md#9-api--events) FR-CMP-45–49, -53 and [assets-pipeline](../specs/assets-pipeline.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-ASSET-35–38 ([ADR-0054](../architecture/adr/0054-compendium-rest-asset-ids.md), amending ADR-0023 "Secrecy"; DM gate #256). **Built by `fix/cmpimg-core`**: server only (form slots, gallery and lightbox are C5 UI). No new route, event, intent, record type or registry. Data side: [data model §68](../data-model/README.md#68-sprint-5-compendium-entry-images-c5-core).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| zod (`@shared/compendium/schemas`) | `IMAGE_SLOTS_MAX = 4`; `EntryImages` = `AssetId[]`, ≤ 4 ("At most 4 images"), unique ("Same image twice"). `CompendiumEntry.imageAssetIds: EntryImages` (an entry with a `source` must have `[]`). `PutEntryBody.imageAssetIds = EntryImages.default([])` (`@shared/compendium/homebrew`) |
| `POST` / `PUT /api/compendium/entries[/:id]` | Body gains `imageAssetIds` (slot order, first = main; whole replace). > 4 or a duplicate → `400 invalid` (issue message as above). In the command's transaction, before any write, each ID through `assets.require` in the entry's campaign: unknown or another campaign's → `404 not_found` "Image not found" (the ID is not echoed), nothing written, no event. Listed assets and attachments both accepted. `CompendiumEntrySaved {entryId, kind, shared}` unchanged (no IDs) |
| `GET /api/compendium/entries/:id` | `entry.imageAssetIds` only after `entryVisibleTo` passes (a hidden entry → `404`, also with `If-None-Match`; never `304`). IDs without an asset row in the entry's campaign are dropped (FR-ASSET-37). `ETag` = opaque SHA-256 over (`updatedAt`, source `version`, monster definition `updatedAt`, delivered IDs): a dropped image changes it; no ID is readable from it |
| Search, `sameAs` | Summaries unchanged: no image fields (OQ-CMP-42) |
| Players (FR-ASSET-36) | A player's compendium REST body may carry an asset ID only for an entry `entryVisibleTo` lets them read; never in an error body, `304` or `ETag`. Entry images never travel on the WS (no record, no event field; attachments have no `asset` record) |
| Fixture loader (`src/server/features/fixtures-adapters/loader.ts`) | Records an attachment's `AssetIngested.assetId` (no record change) under type `asset`: `r.id(key, "asset", i)` and the manifest `records` count attachments and listed ingests in order |
| Fixtures | Unit `cmp-images` (3 attachments; "Moonlit Lantern" shared, "CMP-IMG-SECRET" DM only); bundle `cmp-images`; unit in `session-sim-9` |
| Tests | `images.test.ts` (FR-CMP-45 over REST, -46, -47, -48 ×2, -49, `cmp.putEntry:before-commit`), `image-leak.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-36 leak property over REST`: 60 seeded steps, both players), `snapshot-size.test.ts` `FR-CMP-53` (+0 B every viewer) |

## 87. Sprint 5: handout present client (P2)

From [party-board](../specs/party-board.md#66-sprint-5-loot-tab-present-uploads) FR-PARTY-36–38 (DM gate #256; seams S4; ruling P #23). **Built by `build/party-present`**: client only; uses §82's record type and intents unchanged (no new intent, record, event or snapshot byte). Data side: none.

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Player popup (`player/features/party-board/present-popup.tsx`) | Mounted by one line in the player play page (`PlayerPlayPage.tsx`, inside the sync and shell providers; seams S4). Shows while the store holds the viewer's own `handout-present` row (live upsert or snapshot) and this page hasn't closed it: a modal Dialog built on the Radix primitives in the feature file (its own layer; the shared `DialogContent` has a fixed z-50 overlay under the toasts; orchestrator ruling) at z `PRESENT_POPUP_Z` = 1 000 000 000 (over windows, chrome, dialogs and every toast incl. sonner's 999 999 999; under the dice layer `DICE_LAYER_Z`). Content = `handout` (whole type) + `handout-body` by id via `useLazyRecords` (FR-PARTY-10): skeleton, reject → Retry, a load without the id → closes silently and sends the close. Title; images one at a time (`display`, ≤ 90 vw × 75 vh; > 1 → Prev / Next + "‹n› / ‹N›"); body through `DiceText`. Opening marks it seen (FR-PARTY-13) and calls `hideToast` for every `handout-shared` notification of the same handout (FR-NOTE-26). A new row (another id) swaps the content; the row's delete closes it |
| Player close | Close (focused on open), Esc, click outside, **Open in Party** (opens `party.handout` on it first) → hidden at once for this snapshot generation (`lazy.generation`) and `party.closePresent {presentId}` sent; a failed or lost send leaves the row → it shows again after the next snapshot (FR-PARTY-37) |
| DM Share… (FR-PARTY-38 a) | Checkbox "Also open it on the screens of players who newly get it" (unticked on open; hidden for Not shared; disabled while the draft has no gainer) + "Opens on: ‹names›" / "Nobody new" (`shareGainers` against the stored share). Ticked with ≥ 1 gainer → `party.shareHandout {…, present: true}`; otherwise `present` is omitted |
| DM Open on screens… (b) | Row menu and viewer, shared handouts only. Dialog: Everyone it's shared with (default) → `to {mode: all}`; Chosen players → a checkbox per player who can see it (name, online dot) → `to {mode: players, playerIds}` (candidate order). Open disabled while Chosen has none ticked, nobody can see it, not `live` or in flight. Reject → inline; lost → Toast "Connection lost — check the handout before trying again" |
| DM On screen (c, d) | From the DM's `handout-present` rows: Badge "On screen: ‹n›" on the row and in the viewer while n > 0 (Tooltip: names in `presentedAt` order); viewer **Close on all screens** → `party.closePresent {handoutId}` |
| Test IDs | DM `party-share-present`, `party-share-present-names`, `party-present-open` (+ `data-record-id`), `party-present-dialog`, `party-present-mode` (+ `data-value` `all \| players`), `party-present-player` (+ `data-record-id`), `party-present-submit`, `party-present-error`, `party-present-count` (+ `data-value` n), `party-present-close-all`; player `party-present-popup` (+ `data-record-id` handout id, `data-present-id`), `party-present-title`, `party-present-image` (+ `data-record-id` asset id), `party-present-prev`, `party-present-next`, `party-present-page`, `party-present-body`, `party-present-loading`, `party-present-retry`, `party-present-close`, `party-present-open-board` |

## 88. Sprint 5: late damage (R1)

From [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#62-sprint-5-late-damage) (FR-CBT-87–94; [ADR-0053](../architecture/adr/0053-apply-hit.md)) and [effect-modifiers](../specs/effect-modifiers.md#6-functional-requirements) (FR-MOD-63). **Built by `build/late-damage`** on R0's core ([§83](#83-sprint-5-combat-core-r0)). No new intent, event, record type or table. Data side: [data model §68](../data-model/README.md#69-sprint-5-late-damage-r1).

**Intents** (strict zod, `@shared/combat-tracker/schemas`)

| Intent | Who | Validation | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cbt.applyCard` `damageRollId` | DM | **required** (`string \| null`, every caller); ≠ the card's damage `rollId` → `conflict` "Damage just arrived — check and Apply again", nothing written | as before | FR-CBT-94 |
| `cbt.applyCard` on a ruled card | DM | `toHitTotal` sent → `invalid`; a target outside the ruled hits, or `hit: false` → `invalid` "Not a hit target" (path `targets.i`). Applies in ruling order: each ruled hit sent → a damage-only `cbt.attack` line (`toHit: null`) + `hp_change` with the ruling's to-hit facts (`toHitTotal`, `toHitRolled`, `natural`, `hitComputed`); each ruled miss still active in the encounter → a `raw 0` `hp_change` + a **DM-only** miss line (`public: false`; players read its miss on the Apply hit line); a ruled hit not sent → resolution `{hit: true, applied: 0, dropped: true}`, no row. The ruling's delete rides on the last `AttackApplied` | as before | FR-CBT-91 |
| `cbt.rollDamage`, no to-hit-only card of the row | DM, player | the latest pending card of that row has to-hit and damage, and the sender owns it (its player on a player card, the DM on a DM card) → **no card**: the roll entry as before (player public, DM DM-only), no offers rolled (`subject.card` false), the card's `unusedRolls` += `{rollId, total}` (newest 3; total = base with mods + extra parts) via `updateNotification` (text unchanged). Anyone else → a new damage-only card (FR-CBT-71) | `{cardId, rollIds, attached: false, unused: true}` | FR-CBT-92 |

**Events** (no new type)

| Event | Changes | Player sees | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DamageRolled {cardId, rollIds, attached: false}` (unused: `cardId` = the completed card) + `NotificationUpdated` | roll line; card (`unusedRolls`) | owner: own card upsert + roll line; others: the roll line if public; never another owner's `unusedRolls` | no |

**Fixtures:** unit `late-hit-ruled` (as `player-1`: Mira's Light crossbow to-hit, d20 9 + 4 + Bless 2 = 15; DM Apply hit Goblin 2) in bundles `late-damage` and `session-sim-9`. `fixtureSeededId(key, draw)` (`src/server/features/combat-tracker/fixtures.ts`) names a seeded ID no record change carries (a roll, a sheet attack row); `combat-hit` passes its damage `rollId` with it.

**DM client** (`src/client/dm/features/combat-tracker/`): `late-fill.ts` (popup step from the `hit-ruling` record, "Apply hit" rule, per-field fill / focus / blur, late note, unused text); `popup-store.ts` keeps per-card drafts (fields, rows, multipliers, popup-part values, drops) until reload and the cards this client saw resolved (late note); Pending row text and `data-step` from `pending.ts`. Test IDs per CBT §7 sprint 5; `cbt-init-card` carries `data-step`.

**CON-44:** no record, field or snapshot byte beyond R0's measured rows (`unusedRolls` at 3 per card already in the heavy harness).

## 89. Sprint 5: window layout (W1)

From [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md) FR-SHELL-45–54, [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md) FR-DICE-38, -39, [notifications](../specs/notifications.md) FR-NOTE-31 (decided #256; seams S2, S3). **Built by `fix/window-layout`**: browser only, no record type, intent, event, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Supersedes §18's "Open placement" and "Default layout" bullets and the keep-out rows of §69 / §75 where they differ. Fixture `win-layout`, bundle `windows-s5` (not in `session-sim-9`).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Auto placement (`placement.ts` `placeWindow(def, vp, {windows, hard, soft, tokens, need, lastResort}) → {rect, clear}`) | Candidates: the fitted default rect; spots in the default column and the left / right edge column at rows from the usable area's, every open window's and every keep-out region's edges (± 12), each at full size, at `minOpenH`, shrunk to the clear height (≥ min) and at min height; a candidate over PC token centres also yields itself shrunk to clear them (narrower keeping its left or right edge, shorter keeping its top or bottom, ≥ min size; W1 re-test), so (2) wins over (6) and cover stays only when no size avoids it (#245). Rank (lexicographic): (1) clear of every open window and hard keep-out with a 12 px gap (else: no protected window, no title bar, least protected area, least overlap); (2) no PC token centre (`pc` = the token has an owner in this client's store); (1b) `minOpenH` windows clear at ≥ `minOpenH` (built after (2), so a slightly shorter window beats one over a PC: walkthrough W1; open issue for the spec's order); (3) edge column before inward; (4) clear of soft regions; (5) least token area; (6) full size (≥ `minOpenH` counts; else `minOpenH` windows take the tallest); (7) default column; (8) nearest to the fitted default rect, then taller, higher, left, least soft overlap. ≤ 5 ms for 10 windows × 200 tokens (asserted) | FR-SHELL-45 |
| Inward columns | Only when no edge candidate passes (1), (1b) and (2): x = every window's / keep-out's right edge + 12 and left edge − 12 − w, clamped right of the rail | FR-SHELL-46 |
| Screen full (`manager.ts`) | No clear candidate → the lowest-z open launcher window (type neither `defaultOpen` nor `fixedDefault`) is minimised (written; `ui-window` `data-auto-minimised="true"` until restored or closed, client memory) and placement runs again; nothing left to park → `lastResort` (min-size candidates too). A user restore reopens at the stored rect. Protected = open `defaultOpen` types: never parked, never covered while avoidable | FR-SHELL-47 |
| Keep-outs (`keep-outs.ts`) | `keepOuts(client, vp) → {id, rect, kind}[]`: `toasts` = `toastKeepOut(client, vp)` (players hard, DM soft) + rects from `reportKeepOut(id, rect \| null)` (hard; sprint 5: scenes-map's `map.toolbar`, W3). `useKeepOuts(client)`, `hardRects`, `keepOutsAttr`. Hard: blocked like a window, re-placed on a feature open / appearance / settle, caps growth. `fitObstacles(windowRects, regions)` (`free-area.ts`) adds only `toasts` (tool bars never refit the camera); `useFreeArea()` returns them as `toastFreeAreas` beside the window-only `area` / `areas`; the camera fits into `toastFreeAreas` since W2 (FR-MAP-59, §93). `ui-play-page` `data-keep-outs` = "id:x,y,w,h;…" | FR-SHELL-48 |
| Toast boxes (`toast-region.ts`) | Both 356 × 238 (1 toast 40 + 1 popup 190 + 8): players (vw − 372, vh − 310), DM ((vw − 356) / 2, 100); `ROLL_POPUP_W` 320, `ROLL_POPUP_H` 190 (seams S2; D11 edits popup internals only). Regions, stack order, `dmStackAt`, FR-NOTE-32 and `avoidTokens` unchanged (#256, OQ-SHELL-32 C). `ShellToaster` mounts `ui-toast-corner` (`data-rect` = "x,y,w,h", no input) | FR-SHELL-49 |
| `defineWindow({… defaultSize: Size \| (vp) => Size, previousDefaults?: Size[]})` | `defaultSizeAt(def, vp)`, `knownDefaultSizes(def)` (`registry.ts`); classes wide = vw ≥ 1440, tall = vh ≥ 900 (`isWide`, `isTall`, `CLASS_VIEWPORTS`); the registry checks default ≥ min in every class. `fittedDefaultRect(def, vp, hard)`: a top-anchored rect over a hard region below its top ends 12 px above it (≥ min; player sheet 320×338 / ×418 at 1280×720 / ×800) | FR-SHELL-51 |
| Named default layouts | Token-free: exactly play-shell §7's table at 1280×720 / 1280×800 / 1440×900 / 1600×900 (registry-driven tests, both clients). Map-first: DM 57.1 % at 1440×900 (≥ 55), player 56.9 % (≥ 55); at 1280×720 ≥ 60 / 65 % | FR-SHELL-50, -54 |
| Growth cap (`geometry.ts` `grownRect(r, needed, up, vp, caps)`, `windowRects(items, vp, hard)`) | A growing window stops 12 px before the usable area's edge and before the first other window / hard keep-out whose x-range overlaps it and whose top is below its top (DM Initiative bottom 644 / 724 / 572 / 572; player Initiative 12 above the Game log). `renderedWindowRects(…, hard)` and `ShellWindow` (`useGrowthCaps`) return the capped rect | FR-SHELL-52 |
| Old default sizes (`restoreLayout`) | On applying an entry, a window at one of its type's known default sizes (any class or `previousDefaults`, no `fixedH`) takes this viewport's default: kept in place if inside the work area and clear of the other open windows and hard regions, else auto-placed; written with `area` (`Restored.migrated` → the store writes the entry) | FR-SHELL-53 |
| `dice.log` (both registries) | DM 1150×240 wide + tall, 690 / 160 below; player 850×280, 510 / 160; min 300×160; `previousDefaults` DM [460×240], player [340×280]. `GameLog.tsx`: expression input ≤ 320 px, label ≤ 240 px, lines full width; the log reaches 6 px into the body's vertical padding with 4 px gaps, so 160 high shows ≥ 3 entries (≥ 2 with Physical dice on; e2e) | FR-DICE-38, -39 |
| Player `cbt.initiative` | `defaultPosition` `top-left`, `dx` 56 (token-free 68, 60, 280×400); at Start auto-placed in the left column above the Game log, y by token cover | FR-SHELL-50 |
| `note.history` | Placement only: 480 high where a clear slot allows, else the tallest clear slot (412 at 1280×720; player 472 at 1440×900 above the 280-high log); never over the Game log | FR-NOTE-31 |
| Placement tokens (`ShellContext`) | `tokenScreenBoxes("all")` boxes carry the token `id`; `pc` from this client's token records (`ownerPlayerId`) | FR-SHELL-45 (2) |

## 90. Sprint 5: dice landing (D8)

From [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md) FR-DICEX-49, -50 and [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md) FR-DICE-40 ([ADR-0051](../architecture/adr/0051-dice-landing-line.md); DM gate #256: OQ-DICEX-38 A, OQ-DICE-30 A). **Built by `build/dice-landing`**: no record type, intent, event, signal or wire field; the server never waits ([ADR-0001](../architecture/adr/0001-tech-stack.md), ADR-0044). Data side: [data model §70](../data-model/README.md#70-sprint-5-dice-landing-d8).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Holding (`dice-extras/landing.ts`, initial chunk) | Client memory `holding: Map<entryId, deadline>`. Filled **only** by the runtime's live-frame handler (`ThrowLayer.tsx`, after the popup and the throw exist): the frame's new `roll` entries (FR-DICEX-11 source), none with display `off` or a hidden tab, never a manual-only entry (every payload item `manual`). Snapshots, resyncs, reconnects, undo upserts and rows below the window floor never add one. Deadline = arrival + 6 s (`LANDING_MAX_MS` = `POPUP_ROLLING_MAX_MS`) |
| Landing | An entry leaves when none of its presentations on this client moves: a throw run in `moving` (an interrupted run snaps to `rest`), a popup part in `moving` (popup not `closing`: hand-off, miniature, chip fade-in), a throw waiting for the 3D chunk (`landing.wait` / `unwait`); swept on every change of either store. Else at the deadline. A resync changes none of these, so a held entry stays held; a reload starts empty |
| Hooks (`@ui`) | `useLanded(entryId)` (= not held), `useAllLanded(ids)`, `useHolding()`, `useLandingFade(landed)` (at the landing each `[data-landing]` child fades in over 150 ms, Web Animations API; no keyframes) |
| Log line (`GameLog.tsx`, FR-DICE-40) | Held: `heldSegments` (`dice-roll-log/logic.ts`, render only): `die` chips blank (`DieShape` `blank`: shape in the look, no number), total "…", "(adv)" / "(dis)" marker "(…)", natural 20 / 1 marker left out until the landing; Tooltip without the dice; `aria-busy="true"`. `formatLine`, `lineText`, copy and the stored entry unchanged; the "N new" count and the launcher dot don't wait |
| Tray result (FR-DICEX-10) | Held: `FlatDie` blank, total "…", the line text through `heldSegments` |
| Popup (FR-DICEX-40) | Each row's total waits for its own entry ("Rolling…"); chips blank till then. `data-state` and the 6 s timer stay the popup's own (an Off viewer: totals at once, the card still fades its chips) |
| Label reset (FR-DICEX-50, `tray-logic.ts`) | `editStartsFresh(state, kind)`: an edit (palette die, expression or constant input) in `result` or over a non-`free` request starts fresh (`editRequest`: expression only, category `other`, no label, no actor); while composing it keeps the staged request. The expression and constant inputs show in `result` too. The tray header names the actor only when one is picked ("Other" for a fresh request). "Roll again" re-sends the staged request; a stage (notation, roll bar, Alt+click) replaces it |
| Test IDs | `dice-log-entry` + `data-landed`; `dice-log-die` and `dicex-die` drop `data-value` while held; `dicex-tray-total` + `data-landed`; `dicex-popup` + `data-landed` (every entry); `dicex-popup-row` + `data-landed` |
| Bundle | dice-extras in each client's initial chunks **13.0 KB gz** (cap 15); `RollPopup` **4.8 KB gz** (cap 8) |
| CON-44 | 0 B: no record, field or frame added. Bundle `dice-polish` DM 21,098 B, P1 13,882 B, P2 10,684 B |

## 91. Sprint 5: player colours core (K0)

From [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#9-api--events) FR-CAMP-23–26, -28, -29, [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#9-api--events) FR-NOTE-38 and [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICE-61 ([ADR-0055](../architecture/adr/0055-player-colour.md); DM gate #256). **Built by `fix/colours-core`**: server, zod, the client helper and theme tokens; the picker is K2, map / log / notification colouring K3 / K4. **No signal change** (0 B: the colour derives from the server-stamped `from`). Data side: [data model §71](../data-model/README.md#71-sprint-5-player-colours-core-k0).

**Intent** (strict zod `SetColorInput`, `@shared/campaign-management/schemas`; command in `src/server/features/campaign-management/colors.ts`)

| Intent | Who | Validation | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `camp.setColor {playerId: Id \| null, color}` *(new; the first CAMP WS intent)* | DM, player | player: `playerId` ≠ own (another player or `null`) → `forbidden`; DM: not a player of the connection's campaign → `not_found`; `color` not one of the 8 keys, or extra keys → `invalid`; same as the current colour → `ack`, no write, no event; taken by the DM colour or another player of the campaign → `conflict` "Taken by ‹name›" (the player's name or "DM"), checked in the transaction (racing picks: one `ack`, one `conflict`) | `{}` | FR-CAMP-25, -26 |

**Record types** (scope, visibility and snapshot as before: every connection of the campaign)

| Type | Change | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `campaign` | + `dmColor` (palette key) | FR-CAMP-24, -29 |
| `player` | + `color` (palette key, never null: the boot backfill runs before the server listens); still no `linkToken` | FR-CAMP-23, -29 |
| `notification` | + `byRole` (`dm` \| `player` \| null), `byPlayerId` (ID \| null): who caused it; null on older rows and system rows; never in a helper argument or intent (`note.testSend {byPlayerId}` → `invalid`) | FR-NOTE-38 |

- The wire zod reads `color` / `dmColor` as a non-empty string: a client meeting a key it doesn't know (newer server, edited DB) falls back to the role accent instead of failing the frame.

**Domain events** (`src/shared/events/campaign-management.ts`)

| Event | Changes | Player sees | Logged | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PlayerColorSet {playerId: Id \| null, color}` *(new)* | one `player` upsert, or one `campaign` upsert when `playerId` is null (DM colour) | the upsert (every connection of the campaign) | yes | no |
| `PlayerAdded` | its `player` upsert carries the default `color` (payload unchanged) | as before | yes | no |
| `CampaignCreated` / `CampaignRenamed` | the `campaign` upsert carries `dmColor` | as before | yes | no |
| `NotificationCreated` | the `notification` upsert carries `byRole` / `byPlayerId` | per FR-NOTE-06 (unchanged) | yes | no |

**Helpers, hooks and seams**

- Start hook `campaign-management.color-backfill` (`backfillPlayerColors`): fills null `player.color` before the server listens; one transaction, no event, no log row.
- `notify` (`src/server/features/notifications/helpers.ts`) stamps the causer from `env.actor`; a **system** actor stamps null / null and closes with null closer fields (`resolveNotification`, dismiss). `gameLog.append` (`src/server/features/dice-roll-log/game-log.ts`) **throws** under a system actor (the command fails `internal`, nothing written).
- Client: `colorOf(store, {role, playerId?})`, `colorKeyOf`, `colorHex`, `useColorOf()` (`@ui/features/campaign-management/colors`); tokens `--player-<key>` in `src/client/common/styles.css`. Palette, `defaultColor` and the contrast / CIEDE2000 checks: `@shared/campaign-management/colors`.
- Tests: `@shared/campaign-management/colors.test.ts` (FR-CAMP-23 default colour, FR-CAMP-28 palette contrast ×3), server `campaign-management/colors.test.ts` (FR-CAMP-23 default ×2, backfill ×2, backfill rerun; FR-CAMP-24; FR-CAMP-25 permissions, crash; FR-CAMP-26), `stamp.test.ts` (FR-NOTE-38 ×3, FR-DICE-61 system actor), `colours-sync.test.ts` (FR-CAMP-29 ×2 over WS with reconnect, restart and leak scan; FR-NOTE-38 visibility), client `colors.test.tsx` (FR-CAMP-28 colorOf ×4), `db/colours-schema.test.ts` (push), `ws/snapshot-size.test.ts` (CON-44 colours).

## 97. Sprint 5: Fit keeps every token visible (W2)

From [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md) FR-MAP-59, -61 (DM gate #256: OQ-MAP-49 / OQ-SHELL-39 A; OQ-SHELL-40 A). **Built by `fix/map-fit-visible`**. Client only: no record, intent, signal, frame or Konva layer.

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Fit pipeline (`camera.ts` `fitScene(tokens, grid, map, view, {areas, obstacles, windowsOnly})`) | Step 1 = FR-MAP-58 → -44 → -46 in the toast-free candidates (`useFreeArea().toastFreeAreas`); none → the window-only `areas`; the free area is the **largest candidate even below FR-SHELL-34's 25 % share** (the DM's toast box or a resized layout puts it there, and the whole work area would put PCs under windows); no candidate at all → the work area right of the rail. Then `fitVisible`. Runs on scene open, Fit, appearance and resize refit | FR-MAP-59 |
| `fitVisible(tokens, obstacles, area, from, cell)` | `tokens` = every content token's footprint (map px; DM incl. hidden), `obstacles` = rendered open windows + this client's toast box (`fitObstacles`), `area` = `visibleArea(view)` (work area right of the rail, inset 16). Step 1 clear → unchanged, `all`. Else integer cells from step 1's (floor) down to `FIT_HARD_MIN_CELL_PX` 24 in 4-px steps; at the first with an allowed pan also + 3, + 2, + 1 below the previous step, the largest allowed wins. Pans: each (token, obstacle) pair forbids an open rectangle; candidates = x-values {range bounds, step 1's x clamped, forbidden edges} × y-values likewise, swept over x; the allowed one nearest step 1's pan (Manhattan, then higher, then left), → `all`. `all` always keeps the map's top edge at or below the top bar (FR-MAP-44 / V7: pans above it are out of range; a step 1 above it searches). Nothing at 24 px, or > 30 tokens or > 6 obstacles in the area → step 1 (PCs first), `pcs`. Pure, deterministic; worst case (30 tokens, 6 bars, 100 → 24 px) best of 5 ≈ 10 ms (gate 25 ms, `camera.bench.ts`) | FR-MAP-59 |
| `map-canvas` attrs | `data-fit-visible` = `all` \| `pcs` (last fit); `data-cell-px` integer when FR-MAP-59 moved the camera; `data-fit-floor` false then (the floor no longer set the scale) | FR-MAP-59 |
| Appearance refit (`createAppearanceRefit(refit, frames?)`) | An appearance schedules the refit for the next animation frame; each new token list or window-rect set the canvas renders before it fires moves it one frame later (≤ 10 times), so tokens the causing frame (Start) creates or reveals are counted. Only while `fitted` | FR-MAP-61 |
| Named outcomes (registry-driven `fit-visible.test.ts`, `session-sim-8` Goblin Ambush incl. Brakka) | DM Game log + Initiative: `pcs` 1280×720, `all` 25 / 26 / 31 px at 1280×800 / 1440×900 / 1600×900; + Scenes: `all` 25 px at 1280×800 only. Players sheet + log (+ Initiative): `all` at all four sizes (33 / 34 / 42 / 48 px with Initiative) | FR-MAP-59 |
| CON-44 | 0 B | — |

## 98. Sprint 5: colour picker (K2)

*(Superseded by [§119](#119-sprint-6-colour-picker-and-dice-section-pf2), build/colour-picker-2: `ColorPopover.tsx`, the player header dot `camp-my-color` and `data-taken-by` retired.)*

From [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#7-uiux-notes) FR-CAMP-27 ([ADR-0055](../architecture/adr/0055-player-colour.md); DM gate #256). **Built by `build/colour-picker`**: client only; sends K0's `camp.setColor` ([§91](#91-sprint-5-player-colours-core-k0)). No new route, intent, event, record type, registry or snapshot byte. No data-model change.

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| `ColorPopover` (`@ui/features/campaign-management/ColorPopover.tsx`) | Trigger: a round 12 / 16 px Button filled with `colorOf` of the target, Tooltip = its `tip`; `data-value` = the target's stored key. Popover: 8 round 24 px swatches, 4 × 2, palette order. `colorSwatches(holders, target)` (pure): the target's own key checked and never taken (also when a repeated default shares it); any other key held by the DM (target ≠ DM) or another player → taken, disabled, label "‹Key› — taken by ‹name›" ("DM" for the DM); free → "‹Key›". Label = `aria-label` and Tooltip |
| Send | A click closes the Popover and sends `camp.setColor {playerId: target, color}` (`target` null = the DM colour). Not optimistic: trigger and swatches read the `player` / `campaign` records, so a `reject` (Toast with the server's message, scenes-map's `toastReject`; `disconnected` / `offline` → the banner only) leaves the server's colour |
| Not live | Every swatch disabled; the trigger still opens the Popover; marks keep the last colours |
| Player (`@player/features/campaign-management/screens.tsx` `JoinedHeader`) | "Playing as ● **Player 1** in …": the dot before the name, target = `you.playerId`, Tooltip "Your colour" (no play-shell frame edit) |
| DM (`@dm/features/campaign-management/PlayersPanel.tsx`, `camp.players`) | "Your colour (DM)" + swatch (target null) as one 16 px line above the table, inside the scrolling body (body / footer gaps 4 / 6 px, so two players with two character lines each still fit 400×220 unscrolled at 1440×900, the P3 glitch-5 guard); each player row starts with that player's swatch (Tooltip "‹name›'s colour"). Window size unchanged (400×220) |
| Test IDs | `camp-my-color` (player dot, `data-value`), `camp-color-dm` (`data-value`), `camp-player-color` (+ `data-record-id` = player id, `data-value`), `camp-color-popover`, `camp-color-swatch` (+ `data-value` = key, `data-taken-by` = player id \| `dm` \| absent, `data-state` = `checked` \| `unchecked`) |
| Fixtures | Bundle `player-colours` and unit `pc-colours` (K0); `session-sim-9` already holds `pc-colours`; nothing new |
| Tests | `ColorPopover.test.tsx` (FR-CAMP-27 picker ×7: swatch model per target, 9th player, static render taken / checked / not live, send, reject keeps the server's colour), player `screens.test.tsx` (header dot); e2e `colour-picker.e2e.ts` (`player-colours`: CAMP §13 K1, K2, K10, K11) |
| CON-44 | 0 B (no record) |

## 99. Sprint 5: compressed + cached built client (D12)

From [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-52 (§6.1 table H; BL-21; OQ-DICEX-40 A, -41 A, #256) and [ADR-0052](../architecture/adr/0052-static-serving.md). **Built by `fix/static-compress`** (seams S11: `src/server/app.ts`, `vite.config.ts`). HTTP headers on the built client only; no intent, event, record, signal or WS change. Data side: [data model §77](../data-model/README.md#77-sprint-5-compressed--cached-built-client-d12).

| Route (`start` only; dev = Vite) | Answer | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /assets/*` | `serveStatic({ root, precompressed: true, onFound })`: `Accept-Encoding` has `br` → the `.br` sibling, `Content-Encoding: br`; else `gzip` → `.gz`, `gzip`; else the plain file. `Vary: Accept-Encoding` and `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` on every found file, plain ones too; a missing file → 404 without them. No `ETag` / `Last-Modified` | FR-DICEX-52 |
| `GET /`, `GET /p/*` | DM / player `index.html`, `Cache-Control: no-cache`, never compressed | FR-DICEX-52 |
| Build (`vite.config.ts` `precompress()`) | `writeBundle`: every `assets/*.js` / `*.css` ≥ 1,024 B gets `.br` (quality 11) and `.gz` (level 9) beside it; an in-memory build (`write: false`) writes none | FR-DICEX-52 |

- **Visibility:** the same files and headers for the DM and every player (public build output, no secrets). The asset store (`/api/assets/*`, §14) is unchanged.
- **Tests:** `src/server/static.test.ts` (`FR-DICEX-52 compressed chunk served`: br / gzip / none, `Vary` on a sibling-less file, 404 bare, HTML `no-cache`), `e2e/static-compress.e2e.ts` (`FR-DICEX-52 compressed build output`: siblings decompress to the same bytes, none under 1 KB, `dice3d` `.br` ≤ 140 KB, the e2e server's `dice3d` answer).
- **Measured** (this build): `dice3d` 632,343 B → 132,499 B br / 160,853 B gz.

## 100. Sprint 5: popup line (D11)

From [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-46, -47 (§6.1 tables A, B; OQ-DICEX-33 A, -34 A, DM gate #256; seams S2, S15). **Built by `build/popup-line`**: popup internals only, both clients. No record type, intent, event, signal or wire field; W1's `toast-region.ts` constants (`ROLL_POPUP_W` 320, `ROLL_POPUP_H` 190, `ROLL_POPUPS_MAX` 3) and `dmStackAt` unchanged. Amends §75's card and PiP rows. Data side: [data model §78](../data-model/README.md#78-sprint-5-popup-line-d11).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Line (`popup-logic.ts` `popupLine`) | The frame's `throwDice` list in order, in 44 px cells (`CELL_PX`), 4 px gap, 6 per line (284 px wide), ≤ 2 lines; > 12 dice (dice beyond the throw's 20 counted too) → 11 cells + a "+N" cell (N = every die not shown, its `entryId` = the first hidden die's entry; click → `focusLogEntry` there). Never scaled by count or viewport |
| Card (`RollPopup.tsx`) | 320 wide, padding 10 top / bottom, 12 sides (border included); rows 22 px each (actor plain, label, **each row's own total** at its right with its markers, "Rolling…" until its entry lands), ≤ 3 visible (more scroll); ✕ and ⚙ at the first row's right; 8 px; the line(s); 4 px tag overhang. `popupHeight(rows, lines)`: 98 (1 + 1) … 190 (3 + 2) |
| Cells | `chip` (flat / off / no 3D, non-mesh dice: `FlatDie` / `DieShape` size `lg`, 44 px, fading in), `handoff` (the die's own still, kept upright), `play` (the miniature). Dropped dice dimmed (3D: the renderer's dropped opacity; chips struck), offered dashed. MOD / offered tag on the cell's bottom edge cut with "…"; full label (+ "on a hit") in the cell's `Tooltip` |
| Hand-off (`Throw3D.tsx`) | `PlayHandle.handoff()` also returns each die drawn **alone** (`Still.dice`, no neighbour in its box) and its upright screen turn (`Still.turns`, from `uprightTurn`). Each flies centre to its cell (`registerPopupCell` / `handoffCells`), scaled so the die spans 44 px (`CELL_STILL_PAD` 1.25 margin), turning upright (0.4 s); dice without a cell fly into "+N" and fade. The cell keeps an upright copy (`rollPopups.still(id, {key: canvas})`) |
| Miniature (`Miniature`) | One `playThrow` over the line box: simulation arena = a line wide, 3 lines deep (44 px dice, same seed); `PlayOptions.view` (camera extents: 44 px per `DIE_WORLD`), `cells` (each die's rest point: the recorded motion is shifted there and turned about +Y so its value ends upright; the up face never changes) and `maxMs` 1200 (`MINIATURE_MS`, the recording sped up). Clip and same-task still as §75 |
| Short screens (FR-DICEX-47) | On each render the stack counts the cards that fit (`popupsThatFit`, heights from `popupHeight`, 8 px gaps, newest first incl. the pending card): players from the stack's bottom up to the top bar + 12, the DM from the stack's top down to the viewport bottom − 12; the oldest beyond are dropped at once (`rollPopups.drop`); the newest always shows |
| Test IDs | `dicex-popup` + `data-shown` (cells), `data-dice` = every die of the frame (incl. those beyond 20); `dicex-popup-cell` (+ `data-entry-id`, `data-line` = 1 \| 2, `data-index`, `data-faces`, `data-kept`, `data-offered`, `data-value` only once its entry landed); `dicex-popup-more` (+ `data-value` = N, `data-entry-id`); §75's other IDs unchanged |
| Bundle | Lazy `RollPopup` **5.8 KB gz** (cap 8); dice-extras initial 13.1 KB gz (cap 15); 3D chunk 157.6 KB gz (cap 170) |
| CON-44 | 0 B: no record, field or frame added |

## 101. Sprint 5: ally side client (R4)

From [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#63-sprint-5-ally-side) FR-CBT-98–104 (#252; OQ-CBT-71 A, -72 C, -73 A, -74 A, #256; seams S9). **Built by `build/combat-sides`** on R0's server core ([§83](#83-sprint-5-combat-core-r0): `cbt.setSide`, `CombatantSideChanged`, the redaction, the `cbt.side` kind). **No wire change**: no new intent, event, record type, signal or field (CON-44: 0 B beyond §83's measured rows). Data model: [§79](../data-model/README.md#79-sprint-5-ally-side-client-r4).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `sideChip(c)` (`@ui/features/combat-tracker/side`) | `{side, secret}` or null. DM (row carries `publicSide`): `sideOf(c)` when it differs from the kind's default **or** players see another side (`secret` = `sideOf ≠ publicSideOf`, dashed); players: their `side` (= `publicSideOf`) when it differs from the kind's default, never secret | FR-CBT-104 |
| Initiative row chip `cbt-init-row-ally` (both clients) | `data-value` `ally` \| `enemy`, `data-secret`; lucide `handshake` "Ally" / `swords` "Enemy", `badge` look, dashed while secret (Tooltip "Secret: players see …"). In the name line before the effect chips, never folds (FR-CBTX-54) | FR-CBT-104 |
| Token badge `cbt-token-side` (overlay `cbt`) | The chip's rule as a `TokenBadge` (`icon` `handshake` \| `swords`, `neutral`, label "Ally" / "Enemy"); not on removed tokens (Out) | FR-CBT-104 |
| DM row menu `cbt-init-row-side` / token menu `cbt-token-menu-side` (entry `cbt.side`, order 70.5) | Submenu trigger "Set as ‹other side›" (`data-value` = that side); items `-secret` (tick, `data-value` `true` \| `false`, keeps the menu open) and `-apply` → `cbt.setSide {combatantId, side, secret}`; while secret, a top-level `-reveal` "Set as ‹true side› (show players)" → `cbt.setSide {side: sideOf, secret: false}` | FR-CBT-99, -104 |
| Likely targets (`placementTokenIds`, DM popup / card toast) | The other **side** (`sideOf`), read at open | FR-CBT-101 |
| Builder entry `cbt-enc-entry-friendly` (Switch, `data-value` = entry side; entry row `data-side`) | Sets the entry's `side` in the whole-replace PUT (§83's `side?`); a pick adds to the creature's enemy entry; switching onto a side the creature already has merges the two entries (the server's merge, per definition + side) | FR-CBT-98, -104 |
| Difficulty card (`rateEncounter`, `@shared/combat-tracker/difficulty`) | Rates enemy entries only; total CR of enemy entries; `cbt-enc-friendly` "Friendly: Goblin ×1 (CR 1/4)" when any entry is friendly; all friendly → rating "—". Reads the entries, never the combatants (planning-only) | FR-CBT-102 |

- **`DomainEvent.quietWhenUnchanged?: true`** (`src/server/events/types.ts`; transport hint, never in the event log, which stores type and payload only): `hub.deliver` sends a connection **no** frame when the event's surviving changes for it were all identical upserts (FR-SYNC-19's empty frame otherwise). Acks are separate frames, so the sender loses nothing; `seq` stays continuous (it counts frames sent). Set only on `CombatantSideChanged`: a secret side change (or an open one players already see) sends players nothing (FR-CBT-100; walkthrough R4 leak fix).
- **Fixtures:** unit `enc-friendly` (planned "Escort to the Ford": Goblin ×2 enemy + Goblin ×1 friendly, party null), appended to bundles `late-damage` and `session-sim-9`.

## 102. Sprint 5: tray preview (D10)

From [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-48 (§6.1 table C; [ADR-0051](../architecture/adr/0051-dice-landing-line.md) Previews; DM gate #256: OQ-DICEX-35 A, -36 A, -37 A). **Built by `build/dice-tray-3d`**: client only. No intent, event, record type, signal or wire field. Data side: [data model §80](../data-model/README.md#80-sprint-5-tray-preview-d10).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Preview (`TrayPreview.tsx`, `@ui`) | `compose`, `adjust` and `rolling` (dimmed, read-only): one `dicex-tray-die` per die of the staged expression (crit per FR-DICE-31; the first expression's d20 pair tagged in adv / dis) and of each `trayExtras` entry (inside its labelled `dicex-tray-extra-die`), highest face up (`data-value` = faces), then the constant ("+1"). More than 12 dice (`GROUP_OVER`, extras counted) → one die per term with `data-group` = N and "×N". `compose`: a button (click removes one of its term; hover / focus → red ring); otherwise a span |
| Mode (`trayPreviewMode`, `tray-logic.ts`) | `3d` iff the effective display is `screen` / `zone` (so not Flat, Off, auto-flat, no WebGL, failed chunk), the chunk is `ready` and the stills haven't thrown; else `chips` (`DieShape` `md`, the highest face; d100 "00" + "0"). The tray loads the chunk only in a 3D display (`trayWantsChunk`); a chunk arriving later swaps chips for dice. d2, d3, d1000 → round `DieShape` in either mode |
| Stills (`dice3d/preview.ts` `drawTrayStills`, lazy chunk) | Each mesh die's DOM mirror holds `[data-still]` cells (40 px; a d100 two: `d10:tens`, `d10:units`). A redraw (dice, tags, look or the area's size change; `ResizeObserver`, no rAF) renders them as a **temporary arena** at the top-left of the page's one WebGL canvas (`renderer.ts` `drawStill`: box scissor-cleared, the cells rendered alone, `drawImage`d into the tray's own 2D canvas `dicex-tray-preview`, the dice taken out of the scene, then the canvas drawn again without them) in one task; `addStillArena` stays the look editor's. Pose: `stillPose` (`dice3d/stills.ts`, pure), default labels with the top value up, turned 0.35 rad, camera tilted as the look editor's. Backing store at the renderer's pixel ratio (≤ 1.5) |
| Running throw | `requestStill`: no loop → now; a loop → kept per tray canvas (a newer redraw replaces it) and run by `draw` right after the loop's next frame (`flushStills`); unmount → `cancelTrayStills` |
| Failure | A throw from `drawTrayStills` (no WebGL, lost context) or no dice layer → `chips` until the preview remounts, one console warning |
| Test IDs | `dicex-tray-preview` (+ `data-mode` = `3d` \| `chips`); `dicex-tray-die` gains `data-group` and `data-value` (its `data-faces`, `data-term`, `data-tag` and the `dicex-tray-extra` / `dicex-tray-extra-die` structure unchanged) |
| Tests | `tray-preview.test.tsx` (`FR-DICEX-48 tray preview per die type`, poses, round chips, grouping, adv, extras, compose / rolling, fallback, no 3D import in flat / off, one-task arena, next-frame copy, 0 rAF), `e2e/dice-tray-3d.e2e.ts` (3D or chips; Game log over the tray wins `elementFromPoint` and hides every Crimson pixel at a die's centre) |
| Bundle | dice-extras in each client's initial chunks 13.0 KB gz (cap 15); `dice3d` 158.2 KB gz (cap 170) |
| CON-44 | 0 B: no record, field or frame |

## 103. Sprint 5: combat trails drawn from the token (R3)

From [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-65–67 and [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBT-97 (sim-8 glitch 6; OQ-MAP-50 A, OQ-MAP-51 A, #256). **Built by `fix/combat-trails`** on R0's core ([§83](#83-sprint-5-combat-core-r0)): client drawing only. No intent, event, record type, field or signal beyond R0's `token.trail`. Replaces §78's trail state. Data side: [data model §81](../data-model/README.md#81-sprint-5-combat-trails-drawn-from-the-token-r3).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Draw (`DragPaths.tsx`) | Every token the canvas draws with a non-null `trail` (and `pathShown`, FR-MAP-53): one segment per consecutive cell pair, cells = `dragPath(from, to)` (`trailSegments`, `drag-path.ts`), opacity 0.4, under the live paths; in the token owner's colour at draw time (K3, [§106](#106-sprint-5-player-colours-on-the-map-k3); nothing stored) | FR-MAP-67 |
| Removed | `TrailState`, `nextTrails`, `tokenMovesOf`, `knownCells`, `combatTurnKey`, the preview store's `trails` and `clearTrails`: no derivation from upserts; a snapshot or scene switch drops nothing (the trail comes back with its token) | FR-MAP-67 |
| Konva `map-drag-trail` | id = token id; attrs `segments` = cells − 1, **`cells`** = the stored cells "c,r …" (new, for equality checks) | FR-MAP-67 |
| Visibility | As the token record (R0's redaction): players draw player tokens' trails only; a hidden, fog-hidden or blind-dropped token takes its trail with it | FR-MAP-66 |

- **Fixtures:** unit `trail-moves` (the DM moves Mira (2, 7) → (4, 7) → (5, 6), Thorn (2, 9) → (4, 10), Goblin (12, 6) → (10, 6) in round 1, Wolf's turn), appended to bundles `late-damage` and `session-sim-9`.
- **Tests:** `drag-path.test.ts` (`FR-MAP-67 trail drawn from the record`, `FR-MAP-67 no client trail state`); `fixtures/units/combat-tracker/trail-moves.test.ts` over real sockets (`FR-MAP-67 trail equality after reload`: DM + two players, reconnect and restart; `FR-MAP-66 hidden token`; `FR-MAP-66 party knowledge` (blind player-2, then Next clears)); `e2e/combat-trails.e2e.ts` (CBT §13.1 L1, L2, L5; bundle `session-sim-9`).
- **CON-44:** 0 B beyond R0's rows (§83).

After layout and on every own resize: `reportKeepOut("map.toolbar", rect)` and, when the rect differs from the last push for this tool and layout key, `push(rect)`; never on window changes. Unmount → keep-out null | FR-MAP-60, FR-SHELL-48 |

The `62rem` cap is gone; `fog-no-vision` sits under the bar inside the panel, so it is in the reported rect | FR-FOG-58 |
| Push (`manager.ts` action `{type: "push", rect}`; `usePushWindows()` in `ShellContext` → `{push, layoutKey}`, no-op outside a provider) | Windows whose rendered rect overlaps the bar grown by 12, in x order: x = bar right + 12, past any open window it lands on (right + 12), repeatedly; same y and stored height; width kept if it fits before vw − 12, else shrunk toward min; no room right at min width → **below the bar** (orchestrator ruling): top = bar bottom + 12, same width, the clear x nearest its own column (≥ 68), height kept or cut to the clear space down to min (e.g. Compendium 94, 196, 440×376 at 1440×900 beside the pushed Scenes); neither fits → launcher window auto-minimised (`data-auto-minimised`), `defaultOpen` / `fixedDefault` at the right edge at min width. Written with `area`, `placed` = `pushed` (`data-placed`); a feature open treats `pushed` like `auto` | FR-SHELL-55 |
| Live viewport follow (action `{type: "viewport"}`, `followViewport(ws, registry, vp, regions)`; dispatched by `PlayFrame` on every viewport size change) | The FR-SHELL-53 follow run on the current windows: a never-resized window at a known default size takes this viewport's default (kept in place if inside and clear, else auto-placed), written; other windows unchanged (render-only fit and clamp). DM Game log 1150×240 → 690×160 at 1280×720 and back | FR-SHELL-51, -53 |
