## 106. Sprint 5: player colours on the map (K3)

From [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-68–70, -72, -73, [scene-tools](../specs/scene-tools.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SCN-47 and [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-40's cue ring ([ADR-0055](../architecture/adr/0055-player-colour.md); OQ-MAP-52 A, OQ-MAP-53 A, OQ-SCN-41 A, OQ-CAMP-25 A, OQ-CAMP-27 A, #256). **Built by `build/map-colours`** on K0's `colorOf` ([§91](#91-sprint-5-player-colours-core-k0)): client drawing only. No intent, event, record type, field, registry or signal field; every signal frame keeps its sprint 4 shape. Data side: [data model §84](../data-model/README.md#84-sprint-5-player-colours-on-the-map-k3).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Person per mark | Ping / pull and laser: the frame's server-stamped `from` (the pointer store keeps `{role, playerId}` per ping and trail; own marks = the own person). Drag path + trail: the token's `ownerPlayerId` (null → the DM), whoever drags. Kept template: `placedByPlayerId` (null → the DM); ephemeral: the `scn.template` frame's `from`; own: the own. Cue ring: the notification's `actionPlayerId` | FR-MAP-68–70, FR-SCN-47, FR-NOTE-40 |
| Colour | `markColor(store, person)` = `colorOf` (`scenes-map/mark-colors.ts`), resolved on every draw from the store's `player` / `campaign` records: a `PlayerColorSet` / campaign upsert recolours live pings, trails, paths, templates and cues with no resend. Unknown player or key → the role accent. Hidden templates keep `visibility.hidden` (no casing). Ruler, drag target, box select stay `primary` | FR-MAP-72, -73; OQ-SCN-41, OQ-MAP-53 |
| Casing | Opaque `#000` under every coloured stroke, 2 screen px wider (`casingWidth`): laser, ping ring, path / trail line and step dots, template outline, cue ring. Konva `map-casing`, id = its mark's id | FR-MAP-72 |
| Name chip | Others' pings (under the pulse, its 2 s) and laser trails (up-right of the head, fading with it): 11 px name ("Player 1" / "DM") in the colour on a dark pill; none on own marks. Konva `map-pointer-chip`, id = the mark's id, attr `by` | FR-MAP-68, -69 |
| Template label | Pill: 8 px colour dot + "‹label› · N squares"; hovered (read from the stage pointer; the layer stays non-listening) or selected → "… · by ‹name›" | FR-SCN-47 |
| Konva attrs | `map-ping` (id = `<senderKey>:<n>`), `map-laser` (id = `senderKey`, own too): `color`, `by`; `map-drag-path`, `map-drag-trail`: `color`, `by`; `scn-template`: `color` (drawn hex; hidden → the hidden tone), `by`; `scn-template-label` id = template id, child `scn-template-label-dot`; cue group (`note-token-cue`): `color`. `by` = player id \| `dm` everywhere (own marks carry the own id) | MAP §7, SCN App. A |
| Contract | Token overlay `cue.color?` ([§26](#26-combat-registries-and-seams) token overlays row) | FR-NOTE-40 |

- **Fixtures:** none new: bundle `player-colours` (K0) is the walkthrough's; `session-sim-9` already holds `pc-colours`.
- **Tests:** `scenes-map/map-colours.test.ts` (`FR-MAP-68 laser colour`, `FR-MAP-69 ping colour`, `FR-MAP-70 path colour`, `FR-MAP-72 casing`, `FR-MAP-73 recolour`), `scene-tools/template-colour.test.ts` (`FR-SCN-47 template colour` ×2), `notifications/token-cue.test.ts` (`FR-NOTE-40 names and cue`: the cue colour); server `scenes-map/map-colours.sync.test.ts` over real sockets (`FR-MAP-73 signal frames unchanged`: ping, pull, laser, drag, `scn.template` keep `{type, name, data, from}`, no `color`, before and after a recolour; leak scans clean); `e2e/map-colours.e2e.ts` (`player-colours`: CAMP §13 K2's template recolour, K3, K4, K5).
- **CON-44:** 0 B (no record, field or signal byte).

## 107. Sprint 5: player colours in the Game log, roll popup and notifications (K4)

From [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICE-60, -61 (OQ-DICE-39 A′, OQ-DICE-40 A) and [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-39–41 (OQ-NOTE-22–24 A, OQ-CAMP-27 A; DM gate #256). **Built by `build/log-note-colours`**: client only; reads K0's `rollerRole` / `rollerPlayerId` and `byRole` / `byPlayerId` / `closedBy*` stamps ([§91](#91-sprint-5-player-colours-core-k0)) through `colorKeyOf`. No intent, event, record type, signal or wire field. Data side: [data model §85](../data-model/README.md#85-sprint-5-player-colours-in-the-game-log-roll-popup-and-notifications-k4).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Rule (`@ui/features/dice-roll-log/name-color.tsx`) | `rollerColorKey(keyOf, entry)`: `kind === "roll"` → `colorKeyOf({role: rollerRole, playerId: rollerPlayerId})`, else null. Reads only `kind` and the stamp, never the payload. null = the default text colour: other kinds (`cbt.attack`, `cbt.hp`, `cbt.side`: DM-applied, stamped `dm`), a roller without a `player` record, an unknown key (no role accent in text). `useColorKeyOf()` re-renders on a `player` / `campaign` change only (recolour ≤ 1 s, OQ-CAMP-21) |
| Game log (`GameLog.tsx`) | `dice-log-entry` + `data-roller` (player id \| `dm`, every entry) and `data-color` (the key; absent = neutral). The `actor` segment (`dice-log-entry-actor`, bold) gets `color: colorHex(key)`; targets, totals, chips, badges unchanged. Hidden lines (DM) keep the muted + eye-off styling, the name still coloured |
| Roll popup (`RollPopup.tsx`, one line) | The row's name is `<RollerName entryId name>`: the entry from the store, the same rule; `dicex-popup-actor` + `data-color` |
| Toast + history row (`ToastRuntime.tsx`, `HistoryWindow.tsx`, `parts.tsx`) | `useNoteStripe(n)`: key = `colorKeyOf(causerOf(n))` (`byRole` null → none; unknown player → none). `note-toast` / `note-history-row` + `data-by` (player id \| `dm` \| absent) and `data-color` (absent = no stripe); `<NoteStripe>` = `note-stripe`, a 3 px absolute left stripe inside the border (box `relative`; a pending row's `border-primary` kept). Toast otherwise unchanged (OQ-NOTE-24 A) |
| "Resolved by ‹name›" | `closerOf(n)` (resolved with `closedByRole` set; "Closed" rows null). The badge reads "Resolved by ‹name›" as before; the name span `note-history-closed-by` + `data-color` in the closer's colour. The badge is `outline` (the window surface `#171717`, every key ≥ 4.5 : 1, CAMP §7) instead of `secondary` (`#262626`: vermillion 3.91, bronze 3.82 : 1). "Closed" unchanged (`secondary`) |
| Not here | The DM's action-cue ring colour (FR-NOTE-40 second half): K3 (`cue.color?`) |
| Fixtures | Units `log-colours` (dice-roll-log: Mira's Athletics by `player-1`, a roll by `player-2`, the DM's DEX save for Mira) and `note-colours` (notifications, test mode: `player-1`'s pending card, the DM's info to Player 2, a row resolved by Player 1); bundle `log-note-colours` = `player-colours` + both; `session-sim-9` + `log-colours` |
| Tests | `name-color.test.tsx` (`FR-DICE-60 log name colour` ×3 over real entry shapes incl. the DM's `cbt.attack` apply → neutral, hidden line, popup name; `FR-DICE-61 kinds and fallback` ×2), `note-colors.test.tsx` (`FR-NOTE-39 accent stripe` ×2, `FR-NOTE-40 names`, `FR-NOTE-41 old rows`), `log-note-colours.fixtures.test.ts`; e2e `log-note-colours.e2e.ts` (CAMP §13 K6, K7) |
| CON-44 | 0 B: no record, field or frame |

## 108. Sprint 6: image tags core (IM-1)

From [assets-pipeline](../specs/assets-pipeline.md#63-sprint-6-image-search-and-tags-track-im) FR-ASSET-39–43, -49, -50 (DM gate #288; seams S1, S12; rulings IM #10, #23, #25, #28). **Built by `fix/im-core`**: server, shared schemas, fixtures; no client UI (picker: IM-2). Data side: [data model §86](../data-model/README.md#86-sprint-6-image-tags-core-im-1).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Shared (`@shared/assets-pipeline/tags.ts`) | `ImageTag`, `ImageTags` (FR-ASSET-39 rules), `tagKey` (= compendium `fold`), `IMAGE_TAGS`, `ImageContext`, `IMAGE_CONTEXT_TAG`, `ASSET_INTENTS`, intent schemas (strict), `TagChangeResult {changed}`, `DroppedTag {tag, reason: image-full \| campaign-full}`; messages "Too many tags (50)", "Built-in tag", "Tag not found" |
| Record type `asset` | + `tags: string[]` (row null → `[]`). Visibility unchanged: DM `full`, players `hidden` (audience DM; no player snapshot list). Listed rows only |
| `POST /api/assets` `tags` field | Optional multipart `tags` = JSON array under `ImageTags`; anything else → 400 `invalid` (path `tags`), nothing written. Response `{asset, droppedTags?}` (omitted when empty); `asset.tags` = the row's tags after the upload. Parsed in the assets feature route; no `app.ts` change |
| `assets.ingest` `tags?` | `created`: row gets the tags (campaign spelling) or `[]`. `existing`: the sent tags are added (union, existing spelling) → `AssetTagsSet` if anything was added (then `AssetListed` if the request also lists an attachment). A tag past 8 on the image (`image-full`) or a new DM tag past 50 distinct (`campaign-full`) is dropped; checked inside the transaction after the files are written, so the upload never fails for a tag |
| `assets.setTags {assetId, tags}` (DM) | Whole replace, campaign spelling. Asset not in `ctx.campaignId` → `not_found`. Same tags by fold and order → `ack`, no event, no log row. After-state > 50 distinct → `conflict` "Too many tags (50)", nothing written. Result: none (`ack`). Event `AssetTagsSet` |
| `assets.renameTag {from, to}` (DM) | `from` built-in → `invalid` "Built-in tag"; held by no asset of the campaign → `not_found` "Tag not found". Every holder (listed + attachments): `from` → `to` in place, or just `from` removed if it holds `to` (merge); `to` takes the campaign spelling unless fold-equal to `from` (respell). Nothing changed → `{changed: 0}`, no event. Result `{changed}` |
| `assets.deleteTag {tag}` (DM) | Held by none → `not_found`. Removed from every holder, built-ins too. Result `{changed}` |
| `AssetTagsSet {assetId, tags}` | Logged, not undoable. `asset` upsert if listed; an attachment's has no change |
| `AssetTagRenamed {from, to, assetIds}` / `AssetTagDeleted {tag, assetIds}` | Logged, not undoable. `assetIds` = every changed asset (listed + attachments, seq order); one `asset` upsert per changed listed asset. One frame per DM connection (≈ 423 B per image, ASSET §12; ≤ 4 MiB send buffer) |
| `AssetIngested` | Payload + `tags` (the new row's) |
| Visibility (FR-ASSET-50) | All three tag events change only `asset` records → DM connections only; players at most an empty-change frame. Event payloads never go on the wire. No tag in any player REST body (compendium entry bodies carry IDs only) |
| Start hook `assets-pipeline.tags-backfill` | FR-ASSET-49, once per DB (`db_meta` `asset-tags-backfill`); see data model §86. No event, no log row |
| Fixtures | `ingestStep(file, {name?, listed?, tags?})`; units `images` (tagged), `image-tags`; bundles `image-search`, `session-sim-10` |
| Tests | `src/shared/assets-pipeline/tags.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-39 tag rules`, `FR-ASSET-40 built-ins`), `tags.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-39 spelling`, `cap 50`, `FR-ASSET-40 built-ins`, `FR-ASSET-41 auto-tag on upload`, `re-upload adds`, `cap on upload`, `FR-ASSET-42 set tags`, `FR-ASSET-43 rename merges`, `delete`, `crash`), `backfill.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-49 backfill guesses`, no null row), `tags-leak.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-50 tags never reach a player`), `image-tags.fixtures.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-39 session-sim-10 loads`, CON-44 sim-10), `snapshot-size.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-39 CON-44 tags`), `src/server/db/im-schema.test.ts` (push) |
| CON-44 | Heavy + 500 images: DM 817,909 → 925,409 B worst tags (59 % of 1.5 MiB); players byte-identical. Upload `tags` ≤ 220 B per POST |

## 109. Sprint 6: window reopen, footprints, token-free spots (WF1)

From [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-56, -57, -58, -61, -62 and FR-SHELL-59's export (DM gate #288: OQ-SHELL-41 A, -42 A, OQ-MAP-57 D; rulings-b #1, #2, #5, #16, #17, #19). **Built by `fix/window-reopen`**: browser only, no record type, intent, event, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Supersedes §18's "Stored geometry is always reused as is" and §89's user-open rows where they differ.

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Reopen (`manager.ts` `open`, `isCovered`) | A user open (`toggle`, `open` without `by: "feature"`) of a `closed` / `minimised` window: **covered** = its shown rect (stored geometry fitted, clamped, grown as it would render now) overlaps ≥ 1 px of another rendered open window or a hard keep-out (players' toast corner, `map.toolbar`); the DM's soft box and tokens don't count. Not covered → the stored rect; covered → auto placement as a first open (may auto-minimise the least recently used other launcher window, never itself), written with `area`. `fixedDefault` → unchanged. Protected (`defaultOpen`) → keeps its stored rect; every other open window over it is auto-placed (the protected one an obstacle), lowest z first, written, or auto-minimised when nothing is clear (protected / `fixedDefault` ones only move to a clear spot). Already open → front, never moved. Applying the layout (load), snapshots and viewport resizes never re-place | FR-SHELL-56 |
| `data-placed` (`shell-store.ts`) | + `reopened`: a covered user reopen (even when placement lands on the same rect after parking the window over it); a not-covered reopen and a protected one → `stored`; windows moved off a reopened protected one → `auto` | FR-SHELL-56 |
| `data-pc-clear` (`ShellWindow`, `shell-store` `pcClear`) | `"true"` on a window whose rect auto placement chose (`default`, `auto`, `reopened`) with no PC footprint + 4 px under it at that moment; dropped when the user moves it | FR-SHELL-57 |
| Footprints (`token-boxes.ts`) | `tokenScreenBoxes(ids, store?)`: with the store, each box = the `map-token` group's absolute position + `footprint(size)` (FR-MAP-14: tiny / small / medium 1, large 2, huge 3, gargantuan 4 cells) × the scene's `gridCellSize` × the absolute (stage) scale; the record's size by the node's id, no child-index read; a token the store lacks → the node's client rect (the toast stack and attack popup still call it without a store). Pure helpers `footprintSide(store, id)`, `screenFootprint(canvas, pos, scale, side)` | FR-SHELL-57 |
| PC rule (`placement.ts`) | Rank (2): no overlap with a PC footprint grown by `PC_MARGIN` 4. `clearOfPcs` cuts a candidate to end 12 px before the nearest grown PC box on a side (narrower keeping its left / right edge, shorter keeping its top / bottom); a cut below min size is dropped. `pcBoxes(tokens)`, `pcClear(rect, tokens)` exported | FR-SHELL-57 |
| Token-free rank (`placement.ts`) | New (2c) after (2b): over no token footprint (no margin); inward columns also when no edge candidate passes (1), (1b), (2), (2c). Token footprints (≤ `TOKEN_EDGES_MAX` 200, nearest the fitted default rect first) give rows (bottom + 12, top − 12 − h) and inward columns (right + 12, left − 12 − w), rounded to whole px; spots (columns × rows) ≤ `SPOTS_MAX` 4,000, the token-derived ones farthest from the default rect dropped first (`capSpots`). Still nothing clear and over no PC and no token at full width → the same columns at min width, taken only where clear and over no PC and no token (a narrower window ranks (6)). ≈ 1–2 ms for 10 windows × 200 boxes (asserted ≤ 5 ms best-of-5; ≤ 10 ms for a narrowing window) | FR-SHELL-58 |
| Shown stack (`toast-region.ts`) | `reportShownStack(id, rect \| null)`, `shownStack(client, reports?) → Rect \| null` (bounding box of the reported DM rects; players always null), `shownStacks()`, `subscribeShownStack(fn)`; `summaryStrip(vp)` = 320 × 57 at the DM stack home ((vw − 320) / 2, 100). `ui-toast-corner` gains `data-shown` = "x,y,w,h" while something is reported. Reporters (WF2 notifications, WF3 roll popups) and the Fit's use (`fitObstacles`, WF3) come later | FR-SHELL-59 |
| Contract (`layout-checks.ts` `contractProblems`) | A new window type: not `defaultOpen`; min ≤ 320 × 240 (`CONTRACT_MIN_EXCEPTIONS`: `shell.prefs` 320 × 280, S3); default width ≤ 440 and height ≤ 412 or `minOpenH` ≤ 412 at the four reference sizes. The registry tests open every non-default kind (launcher or not, test-mode included) through `openWindow`; stand-ins for `shell.prefs` (360 × 560, `minOpenH` 400, min 320 × 280, top-right `dx` 12) and `charx.companion` (320 × 360, min 280 × 240, top-left `dx` 56) until PF1 / CP4 register them; *(built, build/prefs-window)* `shell.prefs` is registered in both clients and appended to both FR-SHELL-62 golden kind lists (goldens regenerated, existing kinds unchanged) | FR-SHELL-61 |

| Contract (`layout-checks.ts` `contractProblems`) | A new window type: not `defaultOpen`; min ≤ 320 × 240 (`CONTRACT_MIN_EXCEPTIONS`: `shell.prefs` 320 × 280, S3); default width ≤ 440 and height ≤ 412 or `minOpenH` ≤ 412 at the four reference sizes. The registry tests open every non-default kind (launcher or not, test-mode included) through `openWindow`; stand-ins for `shell.prefs` (360 × 560, `minOpenH` 400, min 320 × 280, top-right `dx` 12) and `charx.companion` (320 × 360, min 280 × 240, top-left `dx` 56) until PF1 / CP4 register them *(CP4 registered `charx.companion` in both clients and appended it to both golden lists, build/cp-card)* | FR-SHELL-61 |
| Layout tests (`layout-checks.ts` `layoutRun`, `bruteBest`, `placeFields`; `test-cells.ts`) | Per client, from each reference size down the chain 1600×900 → 1440×900 → 1280×800 → 1280×720: token-free default layout, the `session-sim-9` Goblin Ambush footprints at the scene fit, open / close / reopen, `viewport` + reopen at each smaller size, then the protected reopen. Checks: no overlap unless no launcher window is left to park; a reopen moves only when covered; the brute oracle (16-px grid, 4-px refine around the winners, sizes full / `minOpenH` / min, footprints with the 12-px candidate gap) never finds a better [clear, PC, `minOpenH`, token] than the chosen rect when a clear spot exists. Goldens per kind for a fixed list (DM 9, player 4) in `windows.golden.json` next to each `windows.test.ts`; `UPDATE_GOLDEN=1` rewrites (PowerShell `$env:UPDATE_GOLDEN=1; bun test <file>`); PF1 / CP4 append their kind and regenerate | FR-SHELL-62 |
| CON-44 | 0 B | — |

## 110. Sprint 6: free player colour core (PF0)

From [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#9-api--events) FR-CAMP-30–34, -36, -37 ([ADR-0057](../architecture/adr/0057-free-player-colour.md); DM gate #288). **Built by `fix/colour-free-core`**: the colour on every field and frame is a lowercase `#rrggbb`. No new route, intent, event, record type, signal or registry; the picker is PF2. Data side: [data model §87](../data-model/README.md#87-sprint-6-free-player-colour-core-pf0).

**Intent** (strict zod `SetColorInput`; command `src/server/features/campaign-management/colors.ts`)

| Intent | Who | Validation (in the command's transaction, in order) | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `camp.setColor {playerId: Id \| null, color}` *(changed)* | DM, player | zod: `color` = `^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$`, stored lowercase (palette names and `#rgb` → `invalid` "Invalid input"); permissions and `not_found` as [§91](#91-sprint-5-player-colours-core-k0); `colorProblem` → `invalid` "Too dark to read on the map" \| "Too grey" \| "Too close to the danger red"; same as the current colour → `ack`, no write, no event; distance < 9 from the DM colour (player targets only) or another player's → `conflict` "Taken by ‹name›" (distance 0) \| "Too close to ‹name›'s colour" (+ " for colour-blind players" when normal vision alone is ≥ 9); the closest holder is named | `{}` | FR-CAMP-31–33 |

**Record types** (unchanged visibility: every connection of the campaign)

| Type | Change | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `campaign` | `dmColor` = `#rrggbb` | FR-CAMP-30, -37 |
| `player` | `color` = `#rrggbb` (never null, never a key: the boot migration runs before the server listens) | FR-CAMP-30, -34, -37 |

- The wire zod still reads `color` / `dmColor` as a non-empty string: an older peer's key maps to its palette hex, anything else to the role accent (FR-CAMP-36).

**Domain events**

| Event | Changes | Player sees | Logged | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PlayerColorSet {playerId: Id \| null, color}` *(changed)* | as §91; `color` = lowercase `#rrggbb` (old log rows: a palette key; the payload schema reads both) | the upsert (every connection of the campaign) | yes | no |

**Helpers and seams**

- `@shared/campaign-management/colors.ts`: `colorProblem`, `colorDistance`, `colorClash`, `defaultColor`, `normalizeColor`, `paletteKeyOf`, `colorName`, `HexColorInput`, `PaletteKey` (`PlayerColor` kept as a deprecated alias of `PaletteKey` for old tests).
- Client `colorOf(store, person)` keeps its signature and returns a CSS colour string (seams S8); `colorHex(color)` takes a hex or a key; `colorKeyOf` gives the `data-color` value (palette name for a palette colour, else the hex). `GameLog.tsx`, `name-color.tsx`, notifications `parts.tsx`: type-only `PlayerColor` → `string`.
- Start hook `campaign-management.color-backfill` now runs `migratePlayerColors` (two passes, one transaction).
- ~~Until PF2: the sprint-5 `ColorPopover` sends the palette hex and marks a swatch taken by FR-CAMP-32's distance; its `data-value` stays the palette name (the hex for a free colour).~~ *(built, build/colour-picker-2: `ColorPopover` replaced by the `ColorPicker`, [§119](#119-sprint-6-colour-picker-and-dice-section-pf2); `PlayerColor` alias removed)*
- **CON-44:** −21 to +14 B per snapshot frame (data model §87); event frames ± 3 B; signals 0 B.

## 111. Sprint 6: Initiative rows — whole names, whole trigger chips, nothing below "↓ N more" (WF4)

From [combat-extras](../specs/combat-extras.md) FR-CBTX-57, -58 and [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md) FR-CBT-125 (BL-43, sim-9 c03 / c10, #286). **Built by `fix/init-rows-s6`**: client only; no record type, intent, event, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Amends [§95](#95-sprint-5-initiative-trigger-chips-effect-source-one-invisible-w4) (full trigger chips: no 96-px cap) and the `cbt.initiative` row of the window table (footer no longer sticky). Data model: [§88](../data-model/README.md#88-sprint-6-initiative-rows-wf4).

| Piece | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| `fitNameLine(widths, gap = 4) → {nameWrap, split, room}` (`@ui/features/combat-extras/logic`) | Pure, over natural widths `{line, name, marks[], side, end, chips}`. `room` = line − name − marks − side chip − Moved − gaps (what `fitChipRow` runs against). `split`: name + marks + side + Moved don't fit, or < `MORE_W` left for chips → the side chip and the chips take a second line (marks stay beside the name; chips get the line after the side chip, and after Moved when the name wraps). `nameWrap`: name + marks wider than the line → the name wraps onto two lines | FR-CBTX-57 |
| `NameLine` (`parts.tsx`; DM and player Initiative rows) | `data-name-line` (`data-split`), always `flex-wrap`: `data-name-head` (`RowName` + `data-name-marks`: eye-off, skip), `data-name-side` (side chip), the chips, `end` (DM Moved, `data-name-end`). Measures a hidden copy of the name (`data-name-measure`), on every render and on resize; gives `EffectChips` its `room` (context). `RowName` (`cbt-init-row-name`, `data-wrap`) has no truncation and no native `title`: one line, or wrapped (`wrap-anywhere`), never "…" | FR-CBTX-57 |
| Trigger chip `cbtx-trigger-chip` | Full chip: Zap + name + uses (+ check), `whitespace-nowrap`, **no max-width, no `truncate`**; its measuring copy likewise, so a chip that would be cut goes icon-only (FR-CBTX-54 (2)). Icon-only unchanged | FR-CBTX-58 |
| List viewport `cbt-init-list` (DM: the rows' scroll box; player: the `ul`) | The rows' own clipping viewport (`overflow-y: auto`); `cbt-init-more` sits **under** it (not sticky, not over the rows), absent while N = 0. While the window grows (FR-SHELL-39) the body (`cbt-init`) gets `max-height` = the height the window would have for the uncapped content (`windowRect` with the measured natural height, the window's growth caps) − title bar, borders, body padding, so the window body never scrolls and no row paints in its padding. `listClip(natural, avail)` (`below-fold.tsx`): cap iff natural > avail + 1 px. N = rows not wholly above the list's bottom (FR-CBT-77). DM Pending: its own 3-row scroll, unchanged | FR-CBT-125 |
| Tests | `parts.test.tsx` (`FR-CBTX-57 hidden ally name whole` ×4, `FR-CBTX-58 trigger label never cut` ×3), `below-fold.test.ts` (`FR-CBT-125 nothing below N more` ×2), `chips-fit.test.tsx` (FR-CBTX-54, markup updated); e2e `init-rows-s6.e2e.ts` (session-sim-9, PLAY §13 F10: DM 400 / 320, player-1 280 / 240) | |
| CON-44 | 0 B: no record, field or frame | |

## 112. Sprint 6: companions core (CP0)

From [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#9-api--events) FR-CBT-105–124, [entities](../specs/entities.md#9-api--events) FR-ENT-31–38, [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#9-api--events) FR-MAP-74, -78, -79 and [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md) (`audiencePlayerId`) (#252, #288; [ADR-0056](../architecture/adr/0056-companion-visibility.md)). **Built by `fix/cp-core`** (server + shared; no UI). Data side: [data model §89](../data-model/README.md#89-sprint-6-companions-core-cp0).

**REST and intents**

| Intent / route | Who | Validation | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/pets {baseDefinitionId, companionOf, name?, turnMode?}` *(new; command `entities.createPet`)* | DM (no secret → 401) | base not a monster (`resolveDefinition`) or `companionOf` not a character of the campaign → `invalid`; a 4th pet of a PC / the campaign's 61st → `conflict` "Mira already has 3 pets"; name taken → "Owlbear (Mira)", then " 2", " 3" | `201 {entity}` | FR-ENT-32, -35 |
| `PUT /api/entities/:id` + `companionOf?`, `turnMode?` | DM | whole replace: omitted = null (release); a character with them → `invalid`; release / owner change while an active combatant → `conflict` "Dismiss it from the fight first"; caps as above | as before | FR-ENT-33 |
| `cbt.setCompanion {combatantId, owner: {characterId, turnMode?} \| null, side?}` *(new)* | DM | PC target → `invalid`; inactive / unknown / not started → `not_found`; owner PC not active in the encounter → `conflict` "Mira isn't in this fight"; owner without a player → `invalid` "Mira has no player"; a pet's owner change or release → `conflict` "Change a pet's owner in Party Manager"; the turn combatant to `in-owner-turn` → `conflict` "Change it after its turn"; a 9th companion of one owner → `conflict`; same values → ack, no event | — | FR-CBT-111, -121 |
| `cbt.start` + `petIds?` (≤ 60) | DM | each a pet of a joining PC, else `invalid` | — | FR-CBT-109 |
| `cbt.attack`, `cbt.rollDamage` as a companion | DM; player with `ownerPlayerId = actor` | another's companion → `forbidden`; hidden → `not_found`; a player's `attackId` not a `card` row id, or a spell → `not_found` | as before | FR-CBT-114 |
| `cbt.rollInitiative` | player | a companion → `forbidden` (players roll only their PC) | as before | FR-CBT-108 |
| `cbt.undo` | DM | a step with an entry on an inactive (dismissed) target → `conflict` "Dismissed: adjust it in Party Manager", whole step | as before | FR-CBT-112 |
| `char.edit` / `char.putRow` / `char.deleteRow` on a pet | DM; the pet's owner | owner: `set` keys ⊆ `ac`, `hpMax`, `speed`, and `list: "attacks"` only (≤ 8 rows: a 9th → `conflict`), no lock; anything else → `forbidden`; another's pet → `forbidden`; any other monster → `not_found`; `char.moveRow` → `forbidden` | as before | FR-ENT-38 |

**Record types**

| Type | Player visibility | Snapshot | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `combatant` (fn takes the viewer) | hidden / inactive / **`ownerHidden`** → hidden for every player. Companion: everyone the monster redaction + `ownerCharacterId`, `ownerPlayerId`, `turnMode`; the **owner** also `hpCurrent`, `hpMax`, `tempHp`, `pet`, `card` *(CP4, build/cp-card)* and, on a pet, `entityId` (its own pet row; never a summon's definition id, never to other players). `ownerHidden`, `concentrationEffectId` DM only. `position` / `playerPosition` null for an in-owner-turn companion | as before | FR-CBT-116 |
| `entity` (pets) | owner: full minus `derivedFromId`, `petPresent`; other players: only while `petPresent`, `RedactedPet {id, kind, name, portraitAssetId, ownerPlayerId, companionOf, band}`; else hidden | as before | FR-ENT-37 |
| `game-log-entry` + `audiencePlayerId` | a non-public entry reaches that player too (redacted: no `dmPayload`) | as before | FR-CBT-114 |

**Domain events** (`src/shared/events/combat-tracker.ts`, `entities.ts`)

| Event | Changes | Player sees | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CompanionChanged {encounterId, combatantId, ownerCharacterId \| null, turnMode \| null, side?}` *(new)* | `combatant` + reranked rows; the bound `token`'s owner; one `cbt.companion` line | upsert per FR-CBT-116 (the old owner loses `card` / HP, the new one gains them) | no |
| `CompanionsSummoned`, `CompanionDismissed` *(new; defined here, emitted by CP3)* | per CBT §9 | per CBT §9 | no |
| `PetHpAdjusted {petId, before, after}` *(new; defined here, emitted by CP1's `pet.adjustHp`)* | `entity` | per FR-ENT-37 | no |
| `EntityCreated` + `companionOf?` | the pet | its owner: the pet; others: nothing (not present) | no |
| `EncounterStarted` | + pet combatants; the joining pets' `entity` (`petPresent`) | per FR-CBT-116 / FR-ENT-37 | no |
| `EncounterEnded` | + pets' `entity` (HP write-back, presence), summons' `token` deletes (`deleteTokenIn`) | the pet per FR-ENT-37; token deletes | no |
| `EntityUpdated` (a character's owner change) | + its pets, their tokens, its active companion combatants and their bound tokens (one hook line) | per rule | no |
| `TurnChanged` | + reranked rows (owner states), the in-turn companions' `movedFt` reset | as before | no |
| Token writes (place, duplicate, hide / reveal, delete), `SceneActivated`, `SceneDeleted` | + the pet's `entity` when `petPresent` flips | upsert / delete for non-owners | as before |

**Helpers and hooks**

- scenes-map server: `deleteTokenIn(env, tokenId)` (`commands.ts`: token-changed hooks, the row, its dependants; rows for the caller's event); `setTokenOwnerIn(env, tokenId, owner)` and `tokenCopies` (`token-owner.ts`); `placeTokenIn` gives a pet's token the pet's owner (FR-MAP-74).
- combat: `rerank` copies `ownerHidden`, ranks per mode (owner eligibility from removed / dead; the turn combatant keeps its rank until the turn moves on); `turnStartersOf(tx, encounterId, turnId)` = the turn combatant + its in-turn companions: combat-extras' expiry and effect-modifiers' `turnStart` reminders use it (FR-CBT-108). Card refresh in `combatant-copies` (definition hook).
- Registry lines (append-only): definition-changed `entities.pet-owner-copies`; scene visibility `entities.pet-presence` (`rederive`); scene lifecycle `entities.pet-presence` (`activated`, `deleting`); log-entry kind `cbt.companion`; route mount `petRoutes`.
- Fog (FR-MAP-78, OQ-MAP-56 C): `sceneSources` orders PC tokens first, then the rest by `seq`, within the 4-source cap.

## 113. Sprint 6: image picker search + tags (IM-2 + IM-3)

From [assets-pipeline](../specs/assets-pipeline.md#63-sprint-6-image-search-and-tags-track-im) FR-ASSET-41 (client), -44–48; hosts [FR-MAP-86, -87](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements), [FR-SCN-55](../specs/scene-tools.md#6-functional-requirements), [FR-ENT-41](../specs/entities.md#6-functional-requirements), [FR-PARTY-47, -48](../specs/party-board.md#6-functional-requirements), [FR-CMP-55](../specs/compendium.md#6-functional-requirements) (DM gate #288; seams S3, S6). **Built by `build/im-picker`**: DM client only; no new intent, event, record, field, route or schema (sends IM-1's, [§108](#108-sprint-6-image-tags-core-im-1)).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Matcher (`@shared/assets-pipeline/search.ts`) | `matchImages(assets, q, filter)`: filter narrows first (`all` \| `untagged` \| one tag by `tagKey`), then `searchTier(foldName(name), textWordsOf(tags), queryWords(q))` from `@shared/search/match` (no copy); order tier, then picker order; empty `q` → picker order. Folded name / tag words memoised per record object. `tagChips` (built-ins in `IMAGE_TAGS` order, then DM tags A–Z, counts, Untagged), `tagSuggestions` (≤ 8, by `nameTier`), `existingSpelling`, `renamePrompt` / `deletePrompt` |
| Picker (`AssetPicker`, `AssetPickerDialog`) | + `context?: ImageContext` (`data-context`), search Input (the Dialog focuses it on open; the inline New scene picker never does), Esc clears a non-empty search (window capture, before the Dialog's Esc), chips (one at a time, AND search), selected image's tag editor (`assets.setTags`, whole list), Manage tags (`assets.renameTag` / `assets.deleteTag`). Tag controls disabled unless `live` ("Reconnecting…"); search and chips work on the store. Search and filter reset per open (state lives in the mounted picker) |
| `ImageAttach` | + `context?` (`data-context`): its uploads / drops / pastes send the context tag; From library opens the picker with it |
| `POST /api/assets` from the client | Multipart `tags` = `JSON.stringify([IMAGE_CONTEXT_TAG[context]])` when the context has a tag; no field otherwise (`compendium`, none). `droppedTags` → one Toast each: "Tag ‹tag› not added: 8 tags on this image \| 50 tags in this campaign" |
| Hosts | token Image… `token`; New scene `map` (inline); Props place / Change image `prop`; creature form Portrait… `portrait`; handout editor `handout` (Images block above the body, FR-PARTY-48); homebrew slots `compendium-item` for items, else `compendium`; `asset.test` none. Players: no picker (FR-ASSET-23, -27) |
| Visibility | Unchanged (FR-ASSET-50): tags only on the DM's `asset` records; IM-1's leak property covers every frame |
| Test IDs | ASSET §7 S6 list, plus `asset-picker-selected` (the selected image's name, also when hidden by search / filter), `asset-picker-no-match-clear` (Clear search in the empty result), `asset-tags-rename-to` (Rename Input), `asset-tags-prompt` (merge / delete line), `asset-tags-dialog` |
| Tests | `src/shared/assets-pipeline/search.test.ts` (`FR-ASSET-44 "Gob el" finds Elite Goblin`, `tags searched`, `order`; `FR-ASSET-46 tag filter`; `FR-ASSET-47 edit tags` helpers), `src/client/common/features/assets-pipeline/picker.test.tsx` (`FR-ASSET-45 search`, `FR-ASSET-46 tag filter`, `FR-ASSET-47 edit tags`, `FR-ASSET-48 context tags uploads`, `FR-ASSET-41 cap on upload` client), one `FR-ASSET-48 every picker filters` test per host (`scenes-map/image-pickers.test.tsx` FR-MAP-86 / -87, `scene-tools/props-host.test.tsx` FR-SCN-55, `combat-tracker/portrait-picker.test.tsx` FR-ENT-41, `party-board/editor-images.test.tsx` FR-PARTY-47 / -48, `compendium/homebrew-context.test.tsx` FR-CMP-55); e2e `image-search.e2e.ts` (bundle `image-search`) |
| CON-44 | 0 B: no record, field or frame. Upload `tags` field ≤ 220 B per POST (as §108) |

## 114. Sprint 6: the DM toast stack stays home (WF2)

From [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-42, -43 (amends FR-NOTE-32) and [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-59's notifications reporter (DM gate #288: OQ-NOTE-25 A, OQ-MAP-57 D). **Built by `fix/pending-toast-home`**: browser only, no record type, intent, event, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Supersedes the P3 / sim-6 candidate list in the `dmStackPlacement` row ([§33](#33-sprint-2-phase-1-combat-p2a)) where it differs.

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Lefts (`keep-out.ts` `dmStackLefts(vp, initiative, width?)`) | Home `(vw − 320) / 2` (480 / 480 / 560 / 640 at 1280×720 / 1280×800 / 1440×900 / 1600×900), then FR-NOTE-32's shift `initiative.x − 12 − 320` when left of home and ≥ `RAIL_CLEAR`; nothing else (no rail edge, no beside-window / beside-token lefts) | FR-NOTE-42 |
| Spots (`dmStackPlacement(vp, initiative, stackH, width?, avoid?, windows?, hard?)`) | Tops as built (100, below each title bar, below each window, above / below each target token) + below each hard rect (bottom + 8). Clear = off Initiative (12 px gap), every title bar and every `hard` rect (≥ 1 px overlap = not clear); then least target-token area, then least window body, ties → the earlier spot; must fit above the launcher. Nothing fits → the top row if clear, else FR-NOTE-32's spot / the top row off the hard rects | FR-NOTE-42, -43 |
| Runtime (`ToastRuntime.tsx`) | `hard` = `hardRects(useKeepOuts("dm"))` (the toast box is soft, so only reported chrome: `map.toolbar`); a report (tool opens, bar wraps, tool closes) re-places the stack. Measured height via `stack-height.ts` `useStackHeight`. Players unchanged (bottom-right) | FR-NOTE-43 |
| Shown stack | The DM stack calls `reportShownStack("note.toasts", {x: left, y: top, w, h})` after layout while it shows (measured height > 0), null when empty or unmounted; `ui-toast-corner` `data-shown` follows. Players never report | FR-SHELL-59 |
| Tests | `keep-out.test.ts` (`FR-NOTE-42 stack stays home` ×4: sim-9 a01, FR-NOTE-32 cases, `avoidTokens` first, lefts ∈ {home, shift} at the four sizes; `FR-NOTE-43 never over a tool bar` ×3: Initiative at x 600 → below the bar, sim-9 b01, hard rect under the home), `toast-runtime.test.tsx` (`FR-NOTE-43` re-place on keep-out change; players unaffected); e2e `pending-toast-home.e2e.ts` (`toast-home-e2e` = `session-sim-9`, own server: 16 / 16 Fog bar controls hit at their centre with the summary showing at 1440×900 and 1280×720, AoE and Structure too; `data-shown` = the stack's box) | — |

## 115. Sprint 6: tray keeps Roll visible (WF5)

From [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-53, -54 (amend FR-DICEX-01's body, FR-DICEX-48's "40 px, wrapping" and §6.1 table C "Size" / "Grouping"; §12 tray budget; rulings-b #14). **Built by `fix/tray-roll-label`**: client only. No intent, event, record type, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Amends §102's "40 px" stills.

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Body (`TrayWindow.tsx`) | One column (`h-full min-h-0`, 4-px gaps): fixed header row (state, gear; 24 px), palette (one row of seven equal grid cells, 28 px), then the **dice area** (`dicex-tray-area`, the one flexible part: `flex-1 min-h-0`, own vertical scroll, 6-px padding top / bottom / right for tags and "×N"; also holds the result and the physical-dice form), the expression / constant row (28 px), errors (one truncated line each) and the bottom bar (`dicex-tray-bar`: `border-t`, 4-px top padding, a 28-px row: mode toggle 26-px items in a 1-px border, DM eye, Clear, Roll / Roll again, Options; 33 px). Only the dice area shrinks; the window body never scrolls at any size ≥ min | FR-DICEX-53 |
| Budget (`trayLayout(bodyH, parts)`, `TRAY_PART_PX`, `TRAY_GAP` 4, `DICE_AREA_PAD` 12) | Dice height = body − parts − 4 px per part − 12. Measured (e2e, Chromium): header 24, palette 28, expression 28, bar 33. At 300 × 240 (body 182 px) compose / result leave **41 px** (the 40-px row pinned), adjust / rolling 105 | FR-DICEX-53, rulings-b #14 |
| Still size (`stillSize(o, area)`, `TrayPreview.tsx`) | Cells = one per still (d100 two) + one per constant and per slot label + ≈ two per extra's text. 40 px while all cells fit the area's rows (4-px gaps); else 28; else one die per term "×N" (40 if it fits, else 28; `grouped` = forced by room, not by > 12 dice); else 28 grouped and the area scrolls. The area's content box comes from a `ResizeObserver` on `dicex-tray-area`; unmeasured → 40. Fallback chips: `DieShape` `md` at 40, `sm` at 28 | FR-DICEX-54 |
| Test IDs | `dicex-tray-preview` gains `data-cell` (40 \| 28), `data-grouped` (`true` when "×N" was forced by room), `data-scroll` (`true` when the grouped dice still don't fit); new `dicex-tray-area` (dice area), `dicex-tray-bar` (bottom bar); parts carry `data-tray-part` = header \| palette \| expression \| bar | FR-DICEX-53, -54 |
| Tests | `tray-layout.test.tsx` (`FR-DICEX-53 tray Roll visible at min`: budget at 360 × 300, 320 × 260, 300 × 240 in compose / adjust / rolling / result, 3 dice + constant, 12, 20, MOD extra; the classes draw the assumed heights; `FR-DICEX-54 tray dice area fits`: 40 → 28 → grouped → scroll, attrs, chip size), `e2e/tray-label-s6.e2e.ts` (2d6 + 1d8 + 1 and 12 d6 as opened (360 × 300, or narrower when placement avoids PCs) and at 300 × 240: Roll inside the window and the `elementFromPoint` hit, body not scrolling, measured part heights) | — |
| CON-44 | 0 B | — |

## 116. Sprint 6: template "by ‹name›" on hover (WF6)

From [scene-tools](../specs/scene-tools.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SCN-52 (amends FR-SCN-47's hover; sim-9 j09, BL-47). **Built by `fix/tray-roll-label`**: client only. No intent, event, record type, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Amends §106's label hover (pill only).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Hover target (`templateUnder(p, drawn, scale, grid, pills)`, `templates.ts`, pure) | Map point `p` (map px) → the id of the topmost drawn template (last in draw order) whose true outline (`templateArea`, uncut) contains `p` or lies within `TEMPLATE_HIT_PX` 6 screen px, or whose label pill rect (map px, as drawn) contains `p`; else null. Shares `templateAt`'s geometry (`isUnder`) without its edit / role filters: every template this client draws counts (kept or ephemeral, others' too; the DM's hidden ones while drawn) | FR-SCN-52 |
| Layer (`TemplatesLayer.tsx` `useTemplateHover`) | Still `listening={false}`; stage `mousemove` (any tool) → the pointer in the layer's map px, the pills' current client rects → `templateUnder`; a held button (dragging) or `mouseleave` → none. The hovered pill is read grown, so hover ends only once the pointer leaves both the area (+ 6 px) and the grown pill: no flicker. Hovered or selected → "‹label› · N squares · by ‹name›" | FR-SCN-52 |
| Konva attrs | `scn-template-label` gains `hovered` (`true` \| absent) | FR-SCN-52 |
| Tests | `template-hover.test.ts` (`FR-SCN-52 template label on hover`: any drawn template's area incl. one the player can't edit, 6-px tolerance per scale, topmost, the pill and its grown rect), `e2e/tray-label-s6.e2e.ts` (Player 1 hovers Player 2's kept Thunderwave on a square Web doesn't cover → "· by Player 2" + `hovered`; away → gone; the pill, then six steps across its grown part → hovered throughout) | — |

| Companion line `cbt-init-companion` (+ `data-record-id`) | Inside the owner's `cbt-init-row`: the row's parts with the line's own `cbt-init-row-*` IDs (`data-record-id` = the companion): name, AC / HP, band, chips, Attack ▸, menu; no turn marker, initiative or owner chip | FR-CBT-118, OQ-CBT-79 |
| DM initiative cell of an owner-ranked companion | `cbt-init-row-init` as text (`data-value` = the owner's number), no input or roll; "Start round 1" and the skip toast ignore `after-owner` / `in-owner-turn` companions | FR-CBT-108 |
| Companion ▸ `cbt-init-row-companion` (row, line) / `cbt-token-menu-companion` (token menu entry `cbt.companion`, order 70.6, monsters) | Owner ▸ (`cbt-companion-owner-menu`; items `cbt-companion-owner`, `data-record-id` = character id, the fight's active PCs, disabled "Mira has no player"); Turn mode radio items `cbt-companion-mode` (`data-value`); `cbt-companion-release`; `cbt-companion-hostile`. Each sends `cbt.setCompanion` (owner: `{characterId, turnMode?}`; mode: `{characterId: current, turnMode}`; release: `owner: null`; hostile: `owner: null, side: "enemy"`). A pet's owner / release / hostile disabled ("Change a pet's owner in Party Manager"); the server decides | FR-CBT-111, -118 |
| Player row of an own companion | HP `cbt-init-row-hp` "45 / 59" instead of the band; card icon `cbt-init-row-card` (+ `data-record-id`); a click on the row opens the card too | FR-CBT-119 |
| Card request (`@ui/features/combat-tracker/companion-card-request`) | `useOpenCompanionCard()(id)`: `useCompanionCardRequest` `{id, n}` (n bumps per request) + opens `charx.companion`; the card (CP4) selects the latest request | FR-CBT-119 → FR-CHARX-59 |
| Start dialog pets `cbt-enc-pet` (Checkbox, `data-record-id` = pet id, `data-checked`) | Joining PCs' pets (`petOffers`): on the map → under the PC with its binding row, ticked by default; else under "Not on this map", unticked; never a new token; ticked → `cbt.start {petIds}` | FR-CBT-109 |
| Turn alert (`turnAlertFor`) | `TurnChanged` to a combatant with `ownerPlayerId = me` → "Your turn" / "Owlbear (Mira)"; to own PC → "Brakka + Drake Companion, Fire Elemental 2" (its lines) | FR-CBT-115 |

- **Fixtures:** none new (bundles `companions-start`, `companions-ally` from CP0). E2E `e2e/cp-turns.e2e.ts` on `companions-start`.

## 118. Sprint 6: Fit without the empty box, pcs keeps the top row, players' reveal refit (WF3)

From [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-80, -81, -82, [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-59 (the Fit's use), -60 and [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-55 (DM gate #288: OQ-MAP-57 D, OQ-SHELL-43 A; rulings-b #3, #4, #18, #21). **Built by `fix/fit-s6`**: client only, no record type, intent, event, signal, frame or Konva layer (CON-44: 0 B). Amends §93's `fitObstacles` / `fitVisible` rows and §109's "the Fit's use comes later".

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Fit obstacles (`free-area.ts` `fitObstacles(client, windowRects, regions, vp, shown = shownStack(client))`) | DM: the rendered windows + `summaryStrip(vp)` (always) + the shown stack (null → nothing), **not** the 356 × 238 box; players: the windows + their hard corner box (`toasts`), as before. Tool bars never count. `useFreeArea()` adds `obstacles` (what `MapCanvas` fits around) and computes `toastFreeAreas` from them; it re-renders on shown-stack reports. Placement is unchanged (the DM box stays a soft keep-out) | FR-MAP-80, FR-SHELL-59 |
| Shown-stack reporter (`RollPopup.tsx`) | The DM popup stack reports `reportShownStack("dicex.popups", rect \| null)` after each render (its rendered rect while it draws, else null; cleared on unmount); `shownPopupStack(dm, rect)` (`popup-logic.ts`). Notifications' stack reports through WF2 | FR-SHELL-59, FR-DICEX-55 |
| DM popup clearance (`RollPopup.tsx` → `dmPopupClear` → `dmStackAt`) | `clear` = Initiative + open non-modal dialogs + the DM's hard keep-outs (`useKeepOuts("dm")`: `map.toolbar`) + every other reported shown stack (`note.toasts`), inset by `EDGE_GAP` − `STACK_GAP` (4 px) per side; a shift left onto the bar (no room at the rail) drops below it. So the popups never overlap the toast stack: they sit 8 px under it in its band (at home: unchanged), or left of it; the toast stack keeps its spot (it never reads the popups). Players unchanged | FR-DICEX-55 |
| `pcs` pan (`camera.ts` `pcsPan(tokens, obstacles, area, view, from, cell)`) | When `fitVisible` ends `pcs`: at `FIT_MIN_CELL_PX` 48 (capped by max zoom). Inputs: the PCs + the 30 (`PCS_PAN_MAX_TOKENS`) tokens nearest the PCs' box centre; > 6 obstacles in the area, no PC, or no pan keeping every PC inside `visibleArea` and clear → null (step 1 kept, FR-MAP-46). Rank: (1) no drawn footprint (on the viewport) with its top above the area's (64 px), if any pan allows; (2) most footprints inside and clear; (3) least Manhattan distance to step 1's pan, then higher, then left. Candidates = every edge where a count changes, cross product, swept per x. `fitScene` returns it with `floor: true`. Worst bounded case (36 tokens, 6 obstacles, 1280×720) best of 5 ≈ 3 ms (gate 25 ms, `camera.bench.ts`) | FR-MAP-81 |
| Reveal refit (`camera.ts` `revealRefit(prevDrawn, nextDrawn, cam, obstacles, area, mode)`, `createRevealRefit(refit, timers?)`, `refitsOnReveal(client)`) | **Players only.** After each token-list change on the same scene (once the scene-open fit ran): a token id not drawn before whose footprint is outside `visibleArea` or under an obstacle at the current camera, while `fitted` → `reveal()`: one refit `REVEAL_REFIT_MS` 1000 later (later reveals ride along; `cameraOn("reveal")` refits only while `fitted`). Pan / zoom gesture start (middle press, a left press on empty canvas where the stage pans, wheel) → `gesture()`: cancelled, the camera `moved` at once. A press on a token or for a tool → `press()`: cancelled, re-armed at the window `pointerup` / `pointercancel` (`release()`); reveals meanwhile wait for it. Leaving sight, moving or already drawn tokens never count; a plain reconnect never refits | FR-MAP-82, FR-SHELL-60 |
| `map-canvas` attrs | + `data-fit-reason` = `open` \| `fit` \| `appearance` \| `resize` \| `reveal` (the last fit's cause) | FR-MAP-82 |
| Named outcomes (`fit-visible.test.ts`, `session-sim-8` cells, nothing shown) | DM Game log + Initiative `all` 36 / 37 / 42 / 42 px, + Scenes 30 / 30 / 38 / 42 (1280×720 / 1280×800 / 1440×900 / 1600×900); no footprint under the strip. sim-9 a03 (box forced, `pcs` 48 px) → pan (578, 16), the top row at y 64. Live `session-sim-9` (e2e): 42 px at 1440×900, 34 px at 1280×720. Players unchanged | FR-MAP-80, -81 |
| Layout tests (`test-cells.ts`) | The DM's Goblin Ambush cells stay at sim-9's measured fit: `fitObstacles` with the whole box passed as the shown stack (the strip lies inside it); the DM goldens were regenerated for FR-MAP-81's pan at 1280×720 / 1280×800 | FR-SHELL-62 |
| CON-44 | 0 B | — |

## 119. Sprint 6: colour picker and Dice section (PF2)

From [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CAMP-35 (OQ-CAMP-28 A, -29 B + (a), -31 A; BL-36) and [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-57, -58 (OQ-DICEX-48 A). **Built by `build/colour-picker-2`**: client only; sends PF0's `camp.setColor` ([§110](#110-sprint-6-free-player-colour-core-pf0)) and the existing dice settings / `dicex.setLook`. No new route, intent, event, record type, signal, browser key or snapshot byte. Supersedes [§98](#98-sprint-5-colour-picker-k2). Data side: [data model §92](../data-model/README.md#92-sprint-6-colour-picker-and-dice-section-pf2).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Model (`@ui/features/campaign-management/color-picker.ts`, pure) | `hsvToHex` / `hexToHsv` (h whole degrees, S / V whole %); `parseHexInput(text, short)` (`#rrggbb`, `#rgb` when `short`, `#` optional, any case → lowercase); `judge(hex, others)` = FR-CAMP-31 then FR-CAMP-32 (`reason` `too-dark` \| `too-grey` \| `too-red` \| `taken` \| `too-close`, the shared messages); `pickerRules(others)` memoises per hex, `key` = the others' colours sorted | FR-CAMP-35 |
| Clamp (`clamp(target, rules, cache)`, `searchSv`) | Every target (pointer, arrow key, hue change with the marker where it is) judged by the exact rules; allowed → taken. Else nearest first: column (same S, V ± d; ties higher V), row (same V, S ± d; ties higher S), rings (Manhattan d; higher V, then higher S). None at this hue → the target with `none` (reason `no-colour` "No allowed colour at this hue", Apply disabled), cached per hue and `rules.key`, so the next target there costs no check. Measured (Bun): worst case 10,201 checks ≈ 40 ms (7 other colours) | FR-CAMP-35 |
| Mask (`maskPath(h, rules)`) | Drawing only: one SVG path in a 32 × 32 viewBox, a rect per run of blocked cells (judged at each cell centre), filled with the `background` token at 70 %. ≈ 12 ms per hue change (Bun, 7 others) | FR-CAMP-35 |
| `ColorPicker` (`ColorPicker.tsx`, `target` = player id \| null = DM) | Palette row (8, palette order, one row; a palette colour breaking FR-CAMP-32 for this person is `aria-disabled` at 40 %, still hoverable / focusable: its reason goes to the status line, **no Tooltip**; the target's own stored colour is never blocked); S × V square (288 × 128, pointer capture, `role="slider"`, arrows 1 % / Shift 10 %, Home / End = S 0 / 100, PageUp / PageDown = V 100 / 0); hue Slider (0–359, the existing Slider over a hue gradient); hex Input (live on a full `#rrggbb`, `#rgb` on Enter / blur; Enter sets the draft, never sends); preview (the name chip in the draft colour on a `#000` 85 % pill, on a light and a dark tile); status line (hovered / focused swatch → its block or its name; else the draft's problem; else, while a drag is held off a refused target, that target's reason; `accent.danger` for a block, muted for a name); others ("● DM · ● Player 2" in their colours); Apply = one `camp.setColor {playerId: target, color: draft}`, disabled while unchanged, refused, not `live` or in flight. A reject → Toast (`toastReject`) and the draft resets to the stored colour; the draft follows the store once they match | FR-CAMP-35 |
| Places | Preferences `camp.color` "Your colour" (order 10, both clients; the player's own colour, the DM's DM colour). DM Players panel: "Your colour (DM)" and each row's 16-px swatch (`data-value` = hex) open a Popover "Colour for ‹name›" with the picker. The player header's dot retired (the identity chip shows the colour) | FR-CAMP-35 |
| Dice section (`@ui/features/dice-extras/DiceSection.tsx`) | `dicex.dice` "Dice" (order 30, both clients): the gears' `SettingsPanel` (lazy: the chunk the roll popup's gear already loads); "Dice look…" switches the section to the look pane in place (`dicex-prefs` `data-pane` = `settings` \| `look`). Same `useDiceSettings` store (`pp.dicex.v2.<campaignId>.<dm \| playerId>`) and `dice-look` record as the tray / popup gears, which stay (OQ-DICEX-48 A); `TrayWindow.tsx` unchanged | FR-DICEX-57, -58 |
| Test IDs | `camp-color-picker` (`data-target` = player id \| `dm`), `camp-color-swatch` (`data-value` = hex, `data-name`, `data-blocked-by` = player id \| `dm` \| absent, `data-state`), `camp-color-sv` (`data-hue`), `camp-color-sv-marker`, `camp-color-hue`, `camp-color-hex`, `camp-color-preview` (`data-color`), `camp-color-status` (`data-reason`), `camp-color-others`, `camp-color-apply`; kept `camp-color-dm`, `camp-player-color` (`data-value` = hex), `camp-color-popover`; retired `camp-my-color`. Dice: `dicex-prefs` (+ the panel's existing `dicex-settings-*`, `dicex-look-*`) | CAMP §7, DICEX §7 |
| Bundle | The picker's own code (`ColorPicker.tsx`, `color-picker.ts`, `ColorSection.tsx`), measured as the OQ-CAMP-31 spike (`bun build --minify`, everything else external, `gzip -9`): **4.8 KB gz** (spike 3.6 KB; this build adds the Players panel Popover and the section); `bundle-size.test.ts` gate 5 KB. The Dice section adds no eager settings code | OQ-CAMP-31 |
| Tests | `color-picker.test.ts` (`FR-CAMP-35 picker` model ×3; `FR-CAMP-35 clamp` (a) hue change → column, (b) row / rings and none + cache, (c) mask vs rules), `ColorPicker.test.tsx` (`FR-CAMP-35 picker` ×7: palette row, static render in the section and the Players panel trigger, reason line / Apply, not live, keyboard, send / reject; `FR-CAMP-35 clamp` held-drag reason), `bundle-size.test.ts`, player `screens.test.tsx` (`camp-my-color` gone), `{dm,player}/features/campaign-management/pref-sections.test.ts`, `dice-section.test.tsx` (`FR-DICEX-57 dice section` ×2, `FR-DICEX-58 one store`); e2e `colour-picker-2.e2e.ts` (`prefs-s6-e2e`: SHELL §13 P3–P6, P9, P10, P14); `colour-picker`, `map-colours`, `log-note-colours` and `prefs-window` moved to the Preferences picker / the new section lists | |

Entry `public: false`, `audiencePlayerId` = the owner (DM + owner only). DM unchanged (definition roll, DM-only). `CharacterRolled` unchanged (`characterId` = the pet) | FR-CHARX-69 |

| Data (`picker.ts`, pure) | `companionEntries(viewer, entities, combatants)`: player → own pets (full rows) + own active companion combatants of the started encounter; DM → all; a pet in a fight once (by the combatant's `entityId`: the DM's row and the owner's view of a pet); sorted by owner PC, then name. Card = the combatant's `card` in a fight, else `companionCard(pet.statBlock)`; HP from the combatant, else the pet | FR-CHARX-60, -61, -67 |
| Selection | `localStorage` `pp.companion.v1.<campaignId>.<dm \| playerId>` (as FR-CHARX-47); requests from `useCompanionCardRequest` (`@ui/features/combat-tracker/companion-card-request`, combat's helper) select by pet or combatant id; the shown one leaving → "‹name›: No longer your companion" 3 s, then the first remaining | FR-CHARX-61, -68 |
| `combatant` owner view + `entityId` | a **pet** companion's owner view (`PlayerOwnedCompanion`) carries `entityId` = its pet row (the owner already has that row in full, FR-ENT-37), so the card lists the pet once; summons none; other players never (leak test `FR-CHARX-61 owner view names its pet`). CON-44: ≤ 50 B per owned pet combatant, owner frames only | FR-CHARX-61, FR-CBT-116 |

| HP | pet out of a fight: amount + Damage / Heal → `pet.adjustHp {petId, delta}` (owner, DM; CP1's intent); in a fight: owner read-only + "The DM applies damage in combat", DM button → `cbt.applyHp` dialog; summons never ± | FR-CHARX-63 |
| Edit (pets) | toggle for the owner and the DM: AC / HP max / Speed → `char.edit {creatureId: pet, set}`; attack rows as the Actions tab's edit mode (`AttackEditFields`, exported from `ActionsTab.tsx`) → `char.putRow` / `char.deleteRow` (`list: "attacks"`; no move); Add hidden at 8 rows | FR-CHARX-63, FR-ENT-38 |
| Walkthrough fixes | **(7)** a companion's outcome lines (`cbt.applyHit`, `cbt.applyCard`): `payload.toHit` = `{dice: null, constant: 0, total, natural: null}` (`companionSafeToHit`), the face in `dmPayload.toHitNatural` (new, optional; the DM's line reads it for "(natural N)" and "DM ruled"), entry `rollIds` without the card's rolls (`companionLineRollIds`): other players read "— DM: hit." / "— DM: critical hit!" / "— DM: miss" and the total only. **(9)** DM: card icon `cbt-init-row-card` (as the player's) on every companion row and in-owner-turn line, and Companion ▸ **Card** (`cbt-companion-card`), both `useOpenCompanionCard()(combatantId)`. **(19)** the player's `available` holds for `GONE_MS` (3 s) after the last companion leaves (`createOwnershipHold`); the open card shows `charx-companion-gone`, then closes itself (`goneOutcome`) | FR-CBT-114, FR-CHARX-59, -68 |

## 121. Sprint 6: pets — New pet, pet HP, Companions tab (CP1)

From [entities](../specs/entities.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-ENT-32 (UI), -33 (form), -36, -39 and [party-board](../specs/party-board.md#67-sprint-6-companions-tab) FR-PARTY-41–46 (#252, #288; [ADR-0056](../architecture/adr/0056-companion-visibility.md)). **Built by `build/cp-pets`**: one new intent; everything else uses CP0's routes, events and visibility ([§112](#112-sprint-6-companions-core-cp0)). Data side: [data model §93](../data-model/README.md#93-sprint-6-pets-cp1).

| Piece | Contract |
|---|---|
| Intent `pet.adjustHp {petId, delta}` *(new; `@shared/entities/pet-hp.ts`, command in `entities/pet-hp.ts`)* | DM; player only for a pet with `ownerPlayerId = actor` (else `forbidden`). `delta` int −999…999, ≠ 0 (else `invalid`); unknown id, another campaign's or not a pet → `not_found`; the pet is an active combatant → `conflict` "In a fight the DM applies damage". `hpCurrent` clamped 0…`statBlock.hpMax`; unchanged → ack `{before, after}`, no event. Else one transaction: `entity.hp_current`, `PetHpAdjusted {petId, before, after}` (logged, not undoable) with the `entity` upsert. Ack result `{before, after}` (sender only). Not idempotent: a lost ack → the caller says "Connection lost — check the HP before trying again" |
| `PetHpAdjusted` visibility | The `entity` change per FR-ENT-37: DM full; owner full minus `derivedFromId` / `petPresent`; other players the redacted row (new `band`) only while the pet is present, else nothing |
| `POST /api/pets` from the client | The Make pet / New pet dialog (`src/client/dm/features/entities/pet-dialog.tsx`): body `{baseDefinitionId, companionOf, name, turnMode}`; the name is prefilled with FR-ENT-32's free-name rule (mirrored in `pet-logic.ts`), a taken name is refused inline before sending. Rejects inline (`ent-pet-error`) |
| `PUT /api/entities/:id` from the client | Whole replace with the loaded `companionOf` / `turnMode`: the creature form's Edit (`keptPetFields`) and the Party row menu's Rename / Turn mode / Owner… / Release… (`petPutBody`; release sends `companionOf: null`, `turnMode: null`) |
| Creatures window | Pet rows list with the customs; `CreatureRow.pet` (owner PC + player); Badge "Pet of ‹PC›" (`ent-list-row-pet` + `data-record-id`, Tooltip the owner player) instead of "Custom"; the same badge text in the combat lookups. Row action `ent.make-pet` (`creatureActions`, append) on every monster row. The encounter builder's lookup leaves pets out (they join at Start, FR-CBT-109) |
| Party `party.board` | Tabs `handouts`, `inventory`, `loot`, **`companions`** (tab memory accepts it); the strip scrolls sideways where four labels don't fit (min width 340), labels never cut. Window geometry unchanged (440×560, min 340×360) |
| Companions tab (`Companions.tsx`, both clients) | Reads `entity` + `combatant` only (no record type, FR-PARTY-45). Groups by owner PC (`companionOf`), colour dot `colorOf(player)`; rows by name. HP numbers for the DM and the owner (the active combatant's in a fight, else the pet row's); a band for others (combatant's in a fight, else the redacted row's). The pet's combatant: by `entityId` where the view has it (DM; the owner's view of its pet combatant, added by CP4); other players (never `entityId`) by `ownerCharacterId` + name, never an owner-seen `pet: false` row. Accepted edge (ruling): a pet renamed mid-fight shows other players its pet row's band until End. Mode icon: CP2's `ModeIcon`. Card → `useOpenCompanionCard()(petId)` (`companion-card-request.ts`), shown only while `charx.companion` is registered (CP4). DM parts (`companion-menu.tsx`, passed by the DM window): New pet…, row menu Rename…, Turn mode ▸, Owner…, Open NPC sheet, Release… (Owner… / Release… disabled in a fight: "Dismiss it from the fight first"). Lost REST → Toast "Connection lost — check the pet before trying again" |
| Test IDs | ENT §7 S6 (`ent-list-row-pet`, `ent-pet-dialog`, `ent-pet-<lookup\|owner\|name\|mode\|submit\|error>`) plus `ent-pet-make` (row action, `data-record-id`), `ent-pet-base` (the chosen creature), `ent-pet-owner-option` (`data-record-id`), `ent-pet-name-error`; PARTY §7 S6 plus `party-tabs` (the strip), `party-companion-name`, `party-companion-mode-icon` (`data-value`), `party-companion-base`, `party-companions-footer`, `party-companion-dialog`, `party-companion-name-input`, `party-companion-save`, `party-companion-owner-select`, `party-companion-owner-option`, `party-companion-owner-save`, `party-companion-error` |
| Fixtures | `pet-owlbear` + `pet.adjustHp −14` (→ 45 / 59); `session-sim-10` + `pet-owlbear`, `pet-drake`; bundle `cp-pets-e2e` (= `companions-prep`, the e2e's own) |
| Tests | `entities/pet-hp.test.ts` (`FR-ENT-36 pet HP`, guards, per viewer), `entities/pet-hp-sync.test.ts` (real sockets on `companions`: owner adjust, `player-2` rejected, nothing then a band, leak scan before / after reconnect), `dm/features/entities/pets-ui.test.tsx` (`FR-ENT-39 pet badge`, `FR-ENT-32` dialog rules, `FR-ENT-33` whole `PUT`), `party-board/companions.test.tsx` (`FR-PARTY-41 companions tab`, `FR-PARTY-42 band for other players`, `FR-PARTY-44 empty states`, `FR-PARTY-46 tabs fit at min width`); e2e `cp-pets.e2e.ts` (bundle `cp-pets-e2e`: New pet "owl" → Owlbear, owner / other player, badge, Release; tabs at 340 px) |
| CON-44 | `session-sim-10` (two pets, not present: Town active): DM +8,074 B over `session-sim-9` with IM-1's tags; player-1 (owner) +1,462 B; player-2 +0 B. Worst case unchanged (CP0's rows, §112) |
