Another player / unmarked token / the card not registered → nothing | FR-MAP-77, OQ-MAP-55 |
| DM: `tokenDoubleClick` line `charx.companion-card` (order 10, `available` = marked token; `dm/features/character-sheet/companion-double-click.tsx`) | Its `Dialog` opens the card once on mount and closes; `DmMap` resolves a double-click entry's `Dialog` as well as a menu entry's | FR-MAP-29, -77 |
| Player: `PlayerMap` `onTokenDoubleClick` | `useCompanionTokenOpen("player")` (players have no token registry) | FR-MAP-77 |

- **Fixtures:** none new; bundle `cp-tokens` (= `companions-start`, for the e2e). E2E `e2e/cp-tokens.e2e.ts`.

## 124. Sprint 6: summons (CP3)

From [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#64-sprint-6-companions) FR-CBT-110, -112, -113, -120, -121, [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-75 and [combat-extras](../specs/combat-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBTX-44 (OQ-CBT-81 B, -82 A, -83b A, -83c A, -84 a / c / d; #252, #288). **Built by `build/cp-summon`** on CP0 ([§112](#112-sprint-6-companions-core-cp0)) and CP2 ([§117](#117-sprint-6-companion-turns-client-cp2)). Data model: [§95](../data-model/README.md#95-sprint-6-summons-cp3).

| Intent | Who | Errors (one code per case) | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cbt.summon {encounterId, definitionId, count 1–8, ownerCharacterId, turnMode?, hpMode?, hidden?, at?, linkConcentration?}` *(new)* | DM (player → `forbidden`) | not started → `conflict`; not a monster → `invalid`; a pet → `invalid` "Pets join as themselves"; owner not an active PC combatant → `conflict` "Mira isn't in this fight"; owner without a player → `invalid` "Mira has no player"; a 9th companion of the owner (pets count) / 57th combatant / full scene → `conflict`; `rolled` without a formula → `invalid`; `linkConcentration` without an active concentration → `invalid` "Mira isn't concentrating" | `{combatantIds}` | FR-CBT-110, -121 |
| `cbt.dismiss {combatantId}` \| `{notificationId}` *(new)* | DM; player: `{combatantId}` of their own summon (`ownerPlayerId = actor ∧ ¬pet`) | not a companion of the started encounter → `invalid` "Only companions can be dismissed"; inactive → `invalid`; the turn combatant → `conflict` "Press Next first"; a player on a pet / another's → `forbidden`, on a hidden one → `not_found`; `{notificationId}`: player → `forbidden`, not this kind → `not_found`, not pending → `conflict` | — | FR-CBT-112, -120 |
| `cbtx.addCombatants` + pets | DM | a pet item (its token or `definitionId`) joins as itself: quantity > 1 → `invalid`; already in combat → `conflict`; owner PC not in the fight → `conflict` "Mira isn't in this fight"; > 8 companions → `conflict` | as before | FR-CBTX-44, OQ-CBT-84 a |

| Event | `changes` | Players receive | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CompanionsSummoned {encounterId, combatantIds, ownerCharacterId}` | `token` creates (owner = the owner's player; FR-MAP-75 beside the owner's token, else `at` / centre) first, `combatant` creates, HP / `own` initiative `roll` entries (DM-only), others' ranks, encounter, one `cbt.companion` line; then the `added` rule-hook rows | visible tokens + combatants per FR-CBT-116 (owner: `card`, HP); hidden → nothing (owner too); the line iff visible | no |
| `CompanionDismissed {encounterId, combatantId, by}` (one per companion; the card form emits one each) | `combatant` `active: false` + reranks; ended `effect`s (`removed`); summon: `token` delete (`deleteTokenIn`); pet: `entity` write-back + presence; a `setCompanion` companion: its `token` owner cleared; the effect hooks' rows; one line. Then `NotificationResolved` of a card left without summons | combatant delete; token delete / upsert; pet per FR-ENT-37 | no |
| Effect events (`EffectsEnded`, `ConcentrationSet`, `ConcentrationRolled`, `EffectsAdded` (Incapacitated), apply events, …) whose concentration a summon was linked to | + the summons' `combatant` (`concentrationEffectId` null) + the card's `notification` create (**no `NotificationCreated` of its own**: `effectHooks` never emit; the DM client infers "new" from the store, FR-NOTE-13) | nothing (identical redacted upsert dropped, FR-SYNC-17 / -19; the card is `audience: dm`) | as the event |
| `CompanionChanged` (release / turn hostile) | as §112; then `NotificationResolved` (`hostile` / `gone`) of a card left without summons | as §112 | no |
| `EncounterEnded` | summons' tokens deleted **only if `summoned`** (a `setCompanion` companion keeps its token, owner cleared); then `NotificationDismissed` of pending summon cards (end rule) | token deletes / upserts | no |

| Record / kind | Visibility | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `combatant.summoned` | DM only (never in a player redaction) | FR-CBT-110, -113 |
| Notification kind `cbt-summon-concentration` *(new)* | `audience: dm`; payload `{encounterId, ownerCharacterId, effectId, combatantIds 1–8}`; action "Dismiss summon(s)" (role dm → `cbt.dismiss {notificationId}`); resolution `{outcome: dismissed \| hostile \| gone}`; end rule `onEncounterEnd: dismiss`; text "Thorn lost concentration (Conjure Elemental): Fire Elemental 3" | FR-CBT-120 |

- **Walkthrough fixes:** a companion attacker's `cbt.attack` outcome line has **no `attackName` in `payload`** (it moves to `dmPayload.attackName`; the owner has it on its roll line and card), so other players read totals only (FR-CBT-114, ADR-0056; leak test strings "Beak", "Claws", "Bite"). `cbt.companion` gains action `mode` (`payload.turnMode`): "Owlbear now acts in Mira's turn" / "… now acts after Mira" / "… now has its own initiative" when only the mode changed. Player rows of `after-owner` companions show "After Mira" (`cbt-init-row-after`). The Summon dialog's concentration tick starts unticked. The DM's Attack ▸ line shows the row as the card does ("+6 · 1d6+4 piercing + 1d6 fire").
- **Registry lines (append-only):** notification kind `summonCardKind` (`kinds.ts`), DM view `dmSummonCardView` (`notification-views.ts`), effect hook `combat-tracker.summon-concentration` (`effect-hooks.ts`).
- **DM UI:** header `cbt-init-summon` and a PC row's `cbt-init-row-summon` ("Summon for Mira…") open the Dialog `cbt-summon` (`cbt-summon-lookup` → `cbt-lookup-option`, `cbt-summon-picked`, `-count`, `-owner` (+ `data-value`), `-mode` (+ `data-value`, `data-state`), `-hp`, `-hidden`, `-concentration` (iff the owner concentrates), `-submit`, `-error`); Companion ▸ `cbt-companion-dismiss` (disabled on the turn combatant: "Press Next first"); the card's `cbt-summon-hostile` (`cbt.setCompanion` release + enemy per summon left) and `cbt-summon-outcome`. Companion card (`charx.companion`): `cbt-companion-dismiss` → inline confirm `cbt-companion-dismiss-text` / `cbt-companion-dismiss-confirm` → `cbt.dismiss`; shown to the DM for any companion in the fight and to the owner for a summon only (never a pet), disabled on the turn combatant. A dismissed companion (inactive in the DM's store) leaves `useCombatants`.
- **Fixtures:** unit `cp-combat` (bundles `companions`, `session-sim-10`); e2e `e2e/companions.e2e.ts` on `companions-e2e`.

## 125. Sprint 7: gems core and campaign feature switches (GM0)

From [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CAMP-38–41, -44, -45, [gems](../specs/gems.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-GEM-01–07, -09–16, -37, -39, -40, [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICE-69 and [live-sync](../specs/live-sync.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SYNC-39 (DM #140, #312, #315; [ADR-0058](../architecture/adr/0058-feature-switches.md)). **Built by `fix/gems-core`** (server + shared + `useFeature`; no gem UI: GM3). Data side: [data model §96](../data-model/README.md#96-sprint-7-gems-core-and-campaign-feature-switches-gm0).

**Intents**

| Intent | Who | Errors (one code per case) | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `camp.setFeature {feature, on}` *(new)* | DM (player → `forbidden`) | key not in the registry → `invalid`; same value → ack, no event | `{}` | FR-CAMP-39 |
| `gem.adjust {characterId, gem, delta}` *(new)* | DM | gems off → `forbidden` "Gems are off in this campaign"; `delta` 0 or outside −5…+5 → `invalid`; result < 0 → `invalid` "Mira has no Fortune gem to remove", > 99 → `invalid` "At most 99 Fortune gems"; not a character of the campaign (pets, monsters, other campaigns) → `not_found` | `{held}` | FR-GEM-03, -07 |
| `gem.spend {characterId, gem, use?}` *(new; GM0: plain spends)* | DM any character; player own (entity `ownerPlayerId`) | gems off → `forbidden`; another player's character → `forbidden` "Not your character"; unknown → `not_found`; `use` missing on Action / present on another gem, `gem: "combat"` (GM2: card), extra keys (`entryId`: GM1) → `invalid`; none held → `conflict` "No Fortune gem left"; Action (2) with 1 → `conflict` "2 Action gems needed, 1 left" | `{held, entryId}` | FR-GEM-11–16 |

**Record types, kinds and the "off" rule**

| Thing | Visibility | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `campaign.features` | every connection of the campaign; **present only when non-empty** (all off: no key) | FR-CAMP-38 |
| `character-gems {id (= characterId), campaignId, characterId, inspiration, fortune, combat, divine, action}` *(new; tag `gems`)* | gems on: DM and every player full (OQ-GEM-01 A); off: hidden for everyone | FR-GEM-06, -10 |
| Log kinds `gem.award {characterId, gem, count, held}`, `gem.spend {characterId, gem, count, use?}` *(new; tag `gems`)* | public (stamped `public: true` whoever sends; roller = sender); off: hidden for everyone | FR-GEM-09, -13, FR-DICE-69 |
| `roll` kind trace (gems) | gems off: an entry with `supersededBy` set is hidden; `replaces` stripped from every payload item | FR-GEM-05, OQ-GEM-21 B |
| **Hub rule** (`ws/records.ts`) | before every role rule, DM included: a type registered with `feature` off → no snapshot key, no frame change, `sync.load` → `invalid` "Unknown record type"; a record whose `featureOf` (log entries: the kind's `feature`) is off → hidden; each `tracesOf` trace of an off feature → `hides` → hidden, `strip` → the record as sent. Window cut before the filter (ADR-0029) | FR-CAMP-44, FR-SYNC-39 |

**Registration API** (server + shared)

- `RecordTypeDef` + `feature?`, `featureOf?(r)`, `tracesOf?(r) → FeatureTrace[]` (`{feature, hides?(r), strip?(r)}`); `Viewer.features?` (the hub always sets it at snapshot time; absent = the campaign's current switches, test viewers); `RecordRegistry.provideCampaignFeatures(fn)` (campaign-management), `featuresOf(campaignId)`, `typeOff(def, viewer)`.
- `LogKindDef` + `feature?`, `traces?: LogKindTrace[]` (`@shared/dice-roll-log/kind-types.ts`); `game-log-entry` registers `featureOf` / `tracesOf` from the kind.
- Feature registry `@shared/campaign-management/features.ts` (append-only): `{key, label, description, offConfirm?, ownerSpec}`; `gems` first. Server: `featureOn(env, key)`, `assertFeatureOn(env, key, message?)` (campaign-management `features.ts`); client: `useFeature(key)` / `isFeatureOn(store, key)` (`@ui/features/campaign-management/use-feature`).

**Domain events** (`src/shared/events/campaign-management.ts`, `gems.ts`; logged, not undoable)

| Event | Changes | Player sees |
|---|---|---|
| `CampaignFeatureSet {feature, on}` *(new)* | `campaign` upsert. After the commit the listener `feature-switch` **resyncs every connection of the campaign** (fresh snapshot, viewer `features` re-read); the named exemption from ADR-0021's per-record changes | a fresh snapshot (client-only UI state kept), then the campaign upsert |
| `GemsAdjusted {characterId, gem, delta, held, entryId?}` *(new)* | `character-gems` (created on the first award) + the `gem.award` line (awards only) | both, while on |
| `GemSpent {characterId, gem, cost, held, entryId, rollIds, rerollEntryId?, supersededEntryId?, cardId?}` *(new; the optional fields: GM1 / GM2)* | `character-gems` + the `gem.spend` line | both, while on |

- **Fixtures:** units `gems-on`, `gems-awarded`, `gems-hidden`; bundles `gems` (= `session-sim-10` + on + awarded), `gems-hidden`, `gems-e2e` (e2e `e2e/gems-core.e2e.ts`). Every other bundle loads with gems off; `session-sim-11` adds none.
- **CON-44** (`snapshot-size.test.ts` heavy harness): gems off with 8 rows at 99 → every frame byte-identical (0 B); on → +20 B (`"features":["gems"]`) + 1,754 B of rows per viewer (DM 693,796 B, 879,068 B left of 1.5 MiB; players ≤ 290,926 B of 512 KiB).

## 126. Sprint 7: combat follow-ups (FU-combat)

From [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBT-141, -145 (OQ-CBT-92 A, #315) and [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-88. **Built by `fix/fu-combat`**. Data side: [data model §96](../data-model/README.md#96-sprint-7-combat-follow-ups-fu-combat). **No new intent, event, record type or signal.**

| Event | `changes` (sprint 7) | Players receive | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|
| `EncounterStarted` | combatant creates carry kept HP (FR-CBT-141; no HP roll entry for a kept row); + a pet token's owner upsert (`setTokenOwnerIn`) **only when it differs** (safety net, FR-MAP-88) | per FR-CBT-37 / -116; token upserts as any owner change | no |
| `CombatantsAdded` | the same kept HP and pet-token safety net | as above | no |
| `EncounterEnded` | + `token` deletes of **every** summoned combatant's surviving token (released, turned hostile, removed mid-fight; FR-CBT-145), before the other companions' rows; pets' tokens untouched (owner kept, FR-MAP-88); a `setCompanion` (charmed) companion's token owner cleared (#310 (2)) | token deletes to everyone | no |

- **FR-MAP-88 root cause (FU0 replay on `session-sim-10`):** Dismiss Fire Elemental, Turn hostile Fire Elemental 3, End, Start "Escort to the Ford" with both pets → the drake's token gets **no change at all** (`ownerPlayerId` = Player 1 after every step; the Add-joined drake row has `pet: true`, so `companionLeaves` never runs for it). Suspect (1) refuted on the server. **Client cause (e2e):** Start's refit (DM 1440×900, 34 px cells) leaves the medium drake's label row room for one chip; BL-35's rule folds the combat overlay's "Ally" **and the paw** into "+N" (tip "Ally, Player 1's companion"); the large Owlbear's row fits both. The fix is FU3's (the paw's priority in the label row); `combat-followups.e2e.ts` accepts the paw drawn or inside "+N" until then.
- **Crash hooks:** `cbt.start:after-commit`, `cbt.end:before-commit` (all or nothing, `fu-combat-crash.test.ts`).

**Client (FU2, both clients; no record, intent or frame):**

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| DM companion line (`cbt-init-companion`, in-owner-turn) | one row via `NameLine`: name (+ mode icon, eye-off, skip), side slot `cbt-init-row-hp` "22 / 22" (+ `cbtx-init-row-temp`) + `cbt-init-row-ac`, chips, end slot `cbt-init-row-card` + `cbt-init-companion-menu` (⋯: `cbt-init-row-attack` submenu, Check ▸ / Save ▸, `cbt-init-row-hp-adjust`, combat-extras items incl. `cbtx-init-row-effect-new`, Side, `cbt-init-row-companion`, Remove) | FR-CBT-142 |
| Player companion line | owner: `cbt-init-row-hp` + temp + `cbt-init-row-ac` (from `card.ac`), chips, `cbt-init-row-card`; other players: `cbt-init-row-band`, chips | FR-CBT-142 |
| `NameLine` / `RowName` | each word measured (`data-word-measure`); a wrapped name's `min-width` = its longest word (capped at the line), `overflow-wrap: break-word` | FR-CBT-143 |
| End confirm (`EndConfirmDescription {area}`) | `${area}-end-summons` (`data-value` = count; ≤ 5 names + "and N more"), one `${area}-end-charmed` per charmed companion, then `${area}-end-pending`; from the DM store at open time | FR-CBT-144 |
| Start dialog | `cbt-enc-start-kept-note` while a monster binding takes a placed token | FR-CBT-141 |
| Encounter editor | `cbt-enc-editor-back`; `cbt-enc-editor-body` scrolls between the header and `cbt-enc-editor-actions`; `cbt-enc-entry-name` (wraps between words, ≥ 96 px); list `cbt-enc-row-name` wraps | FR-CBT-146, -147, -149 |
| Concentration chip (`EffectChips`) | fitted like a trigger chip (`data-compact`), never cut; DM / the PC's owner: `cbtx-conc-chip` → `cbtx-conc-menu` (`cbtx-conc-title`, `cbtx-conc-rounds`, `cbtx-conc-ends` from the viewer's store, `cbtx-conc-end` → `cbtx.endEffect`, DM `cbtx-conc-edit`); others: a focusable chip with its Tooltip | FR-CBTX-59–61 |
| Turn-mode radios / portrait | `aria-label` = label on `cbt-summon-mode`, `cbt-companion-mode`, `ent-pet-mode`; `cbt-form-portrait` | FR-CBT-151, FR-ENT-44 |

| `party.addItem` notification text | `loot-received` reads "Party inventory: ‹name› ×‹qty›" (`itemAddedText`); coin / loot entries keep "Party loot: …" (FR-LOOT-13); stored rows keep their text | as before (`all`) | FR-PARTY-49 |
| `party.addItem` ack in the DM's Add to party | Popover shows `cmp-add-to-party-done` (`role="status"`) "Added: ‹row name› ×‹qty›" for 1.5 s, then closes; the row name (after a merge) from the store, else a one-id `sync.load` of `party-item`, else the name sent. Reject / lost intent unchanged | DM only | FR-CMP-57 |

| `roll` entry `offered[].type` (written by `rollLine`, `cbt.attack` / `cbt.rollDamage`) | an untyped trigger part takes the attack's `damageType` (FR-MOD-62's rule; a free-text type that isn't a part type stays null): "(+ 8 piercing Sneak Attack (3d6: 5 + 2 + 1) on a hit)". The card part and `hp_change.triggerParts` unchanged | as before | FR-MOD-69 |
| Roll-bar Options entry `pp.dice.options.v1.<campaignId>.<dm \| playerId>` | value `"1"` / `"0"` (open, as before) or, with a player's manual actor pick, `{"open": bool, "actor": "" \| entityId}` (zod `RollBarEntry`; broken → closed, no pick). A player's actor: the pick (still theirs) → the sheet's PC (`sheetSelection`, own only) → the only PC → "Me". The DM's pick stays per page | per browser, never sent | FR-DICE-77 |
| DM pending summary toast | `note-toast-pending-summary` + `data-waiting` = M, the started encounter's pending rows owned by a player (linked by the kind's view, FR-NOTE-29); text "N pending action(s) · M waiting for players" ("1 waiting for a player"), the waiting part alone at N = 0; shown on connect when N + M > 0, recounted with the store | DM only (players: M = 0) | FR-NOTE-45 |
| `party.board` tabs | each `TabsContent` `forceMount`, mounted on its first visit and kept while the window is open; the others `hidden` | client only | FR-PARTY-50, FR-LOOT-29 |

Window size unchanged (320 × 360) | as before | FR-CHARX-82 |
| Creatures rows / Make pet | `ent-list-row-name` wraps onto ≤ 2 lines (`line-clamp-2`, Tooltip keeps the whole name); `ent-pet-make` on a pet row: Tooltip "Copy as a new pet…", aria-label "Copy ‹name› as a new pet" | DM only | FR-ENT-42, -43 |
| Effect toggles row, Features tab row | no `mod-toggle-overspent` / `charx-feature-overspent` Badge beside "−1 / 3" / "−1 / 3 left" (the Tooltip moves onto the count, `OverspentTip`; `OverspentBadge` removed) | as before | FR-MOD-68, FR-CHARX-58 |

- **Root cause, stray space (FR-DICE-78 / FR-DICEX-63):** the line builders' text is clean ("(3d6: 5 + 2 + 1)", pinned for every registered kind and the adv / dis / dropped / offered / manual / modifier-only cases). The " )" came from reading the rendered line: Chromium's `innerText` (copy / paste, DOM tools) breaks around each die chip's SVG `<text>` (even an inline SVG), and the accessibility tree lists each chip as its own `img`. `textContent` is clean (e2e). The popup row texts moved to `popupItemLabel` / `popupOfferText` (`popup-logic.ts`).

E2E `e2e/small-copy-s7.e2e.ts` on `fu-small-copy`; `e2e/cmp-add-to-party.e2e.ts` and `e2e/uses-negative.e2e.ts` texts updated.

## 128. Sprint 7: gems sheet block, counts and Campaign options (GM3)

From [gems](../specs/gems.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-GEM-08, -17, -32, -35, -36, [character-sheet-full](../specs/character-sheet-full.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CHARX-71–76 and [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CAMP-42, -43 (#315: OQ-GEM-03 B, -06 A, -11 D, -12 A, -14 A; OQ-CHARX-37 A; OQ-CAMP-33 A). **Built by `build/gems-sheet`**: client only; sends GM0's `gem.adjust`, `gem.spend` (plain spends) and `camp.setFeature` ([§125](#125-sprint-7-gems-core-and-campaign-feature-switches-gm0)). No new route, intent, event, record type, signal, browser key or snapshot byte. Data side: [data model §98](../data-model/README.md#98-sprint-7-gems-sheet-block-counts-and-campaign-options-gm3).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Sheet section `gem.counts` (`@ui/features/gems/GemBlock.tsx`, appended to `sheet-sections.ts`) | slot `header`, order 60 (after `cbtx.effects`), kinds `character`, both clients; `available` = gems on (`isFeatureOn`) and the entity is a character. Five chips in GEM order (Inspiration, Fortune, Combat, Divine Knowledge, Action), lucide `sparkles` / `coins` / `swords` / `eye` / `zap` in `text.primary`, zeros muted, name + where it's spent in a Tooltip, `aria-label` "‹Name›: N"; static "max 5" (Tooltip "A table rule, not enforced"); counts above 5 drawn like any other | FR-CHARX-71, -72, FR-GEM-08, -35 |
| Controls | **Owner** (entity `ownerPlayerId` = you) and **DM**: "Spend" menu: Fortune, Divine Knowledge, Action gem Bonus action (1) / Reaction (1) / Action (2), Inspiration: to recall; an item is disabled while fewer than its cost are held; a pick shows "‹Spend a … gem?› ✓ ✗" for 4 s, ✓ sends one `gem.spend {characterId, gem, use?}`. **DM**: each chip opens a Popover with − / + (`gem.adjust` ±1; − disabled at 0). The sheet lock never blocks them; not `live` → disabled, a pending confirm resets; one intent in flight; a reject → inline `gem-error` (server text) | FR-CHARX-72, -76, FR-GEM-17, -32 |
| Summary card (`SummaryCard.tsx`) | gems on: one read-only row of the five counts under "Level N Class" (`charx-summary-gems`); off: nothing | FR-CHARX-73 |
| Players window (`PlayersPanel.tsx`) | header line: "Options" (lucide `settings-2`) → Dialog "Campaign options — ‹campaign›" (`CampaignOptions.tsx`): one row per `FEATURES` entry (label, description, Switch from `campaign.features`); on → `camp.setFeature {on: true}` at once; off → the row shows `offConfirm` + "Turn off" / "Cancel" (no second dialog); disabled unless `live`; the switch's resync keeps it open. Party lines: gems on → "· ‹counts›" after the passives (`PartyLineGems`) | FR-CAMP-42, -43, FR-GEM-36 |

## 129. Sprint 7: window frame — auto-minimise instead of covering PCs, useful sizes, pushed windows return, Space + focus Tooltips (FU1)

From [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-77–81, -83–88 (FR-SHELL-82 retired) and [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-44 (DM gate #315: OQ-SHELL-52 A, -53 B, -54 A, -55 B; review-b rulings #1–#3, #7–#9, #12, #22). **Built by `fix/fu-frame`**: browser only, no record type, intent, event, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Supersedes §104's "never restored when the tool closes" and §109's contract min (320 × 240 → 320 × 280) where they differ.

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Auto placement (`manager.ts` `autoPlace`) | Every auto placement of `W` (first open, covered reopen, protected-reopen moves, feature opens, appearances, settles; not the default layout / Reset, not pushes): (a) `placeWindow` with `floor` = `useful(def, vp, registry)`; (b) accepted iff `clear` and `pcClear` (no PC footprint + 4 px); (c) else the **look-ahead**, once per placement: the same with every auto-minimisable window removed; accepted → auto-minimise the first in `autoMinimiseOrder` and repeat (each window at most once, ≤ 40); (d) else every shrink to the min size, accepted when clear and PC-free; (d') else the look-ahead at the min size: it helps → auto-minimise the first and repeat (orchestrator ruling: a PC is covered only when no auto-minimise can prevent it); (e) else `leastPc`: the clear spot over the least PC footprint area (a 16-px sweep of the usable area joins the candidates); none clear (screen full, FR-SHELL-47) → the first auto-minimisable window goes and placement repeats; nothing left → the protected last resort. Auto-minimisable = rendered, open, type neither `defaultOpen` nor `fixedDefault`, not `W`. `ctx.onAutoMinimise(id, forKind)` reports each move. Budgets (`bun run bench`): one placement with 12 windows ≤ 25 ms (≈ 17 ms), 39 auto-minimised in turn ≤ 100 ms (≈ 31 ms) | FR-SHELL-77 |
| PCs for placement (`token-boxes.ts` `isPlacementPc`, `ShellContext` `placementTokens`) | Orchestrator ruling (sprint 7, #310 "PCs"): a PC for the PC rule (FR-SHELL-57, -77, `data-pc-clear`) is a player character's token, `characterId` and `ownerPlayerId` both set; owned pets, summons and companions rank like any other token (rank (2c), token area). The map's Fit keeps its owner-based PCs (FR-MAP-46) | FR-SHELL-57, -77 |
| Order (`autoMinimiseOrder`) | Ascending z, ties → earlier in the layout entry; a window placed earlier in the same sequence (`late`: FR-SHELL-56 (5)'s re-placements) last | FR-SHELL-78 |
| Useful size (`useful`, `usefulSize`, `bandAt`; `WindowDef.usefulSize?: Size`) | Width `usefulSize.w` ?? the fitted default width; height min(`usefulSize.h` ?? `minOpenH` ?? min(default h, 360), band); within [min, default]. Band = the work area above the lowest bottom-anchored `defaultOpen` window of the token-free default layout (DM 412 / 492 / 512 / 512, player 412 / 492 / 472 / 472 at 1280×720 / 1280×800 / 1440×900 / 1600×900). `placeWindow` `floor` keeps every candidate ≥ it (rows still come from the min height too); `pcClear` on the result. Declared: `charx.companion` 320 × 360, `cbt.encounters` 440 × 412 | FR-SHELL-79 |
| Reset layout (`reduce` `reset`, `createShellController({onReset})`, `free-area.ts` `requestFit`) | Writes the token-free default layout (whatever tokens are drawn), other windows closed, `fixedH` cleared; then `requestFit()` (counter beside `noteAppearance`, `FreeAreaState.fitRequests`); `MapCanvas` runs one Fit (`data-fit-reason="fit"`) on the client that reset | FR-SHELL-80 |
| Pushed windows return (`reduce` `return`, `ManagerContext.prePush`, `ShellState.prePush`) | A push keeps each moved window's pre-push rect and `userSpot` (the first push of a tool use wins) and the rect it wrote; a move, resize, close, minimise, reset, return or applied layout drops it (a reload starts empty). `ShellProvider` dispatches `return` when a reported keep-out (`map.toolbar`) goes (`subscribeKeepOuts`): lowest z first, a window still open at its pushed rect goes back iff its pre-push rect (as rendered) overlaps no other rendered open window or hard keep-out; PCs don't count; written with `area` and its `userSpot`; `data-placed="returned"` | FR-SHELL-81 |
| Reopen (`isReopenCovered`; stored `userSpot?: true`) | A user reopen re-places when `isCovered` or, for a spot the shell chose (no `userSpot`), its shown rect covers a PC footprint + 4 px. `setRect` sets `userSpot`; a shell placement (auto, push, viewport follow, return without one) drops it; a feature open re-places a non-user spot over a PC or a `returned` one | FR-SHELL-56 (OQ-SHELL-55 B) |
| Space (`keys.ts` `spaceAction`, `trackFocusModality`, `isKeyboardFocus`; `MapCanvas`) | Space `keydown` → `browser` (no `preventDefault`) when typing, inside a dialog / popover / menu / listbox, or on a control (button, `role` button / switch / checkbox / radio / tab / menuitem* / option, link, summary, checkbox / radio input) or inside a shell zone **when** the focus was keyboard-driven (`:focus-visible` read at `focusin`) or the pointer is off the map; else `pan` (and its `keyup` prevented). The canvas container is `tabIndex=-1` and takes focus on pointer-down | FR-SHELL-83 |
| Tooltips on focus (`overlays.tsx` Tooltip, `roving.ts`) | Every Tooltip trigger carries `data-tip`; one that can't take focus gets `tabIndex` (`data-roving`); inside `[data-chip-row]` the row is one tab stop (roving `tabIndex`, ←/→ / Home / End move, the last focused keeps the stop); a trigger inside a focusable element of the row (the DM's chip menu button) opens on that element's keyboard focus. Opens 400 ms after keyboard focus, closes on blur / Escape; pointer focus doesn't open | FR-SHELL-84 |
| Size table (`size-table.ts`, `layout-checks.ts` `sizeTableProblems`) | Per client, every registered kind (test-mode ones aside): default (fitted for protected rows), useful, min at the four sizes and the band = §7's sprint-7 table; ST's `stats.board` adds its row in its branch | FR-SHELL-85 |
| Contract (`contractProblems`) | Not `defaultOpen`; min ≤ 320 × 280 (`CONTRACT_MIN`); default ≤ 440 wide and ≤ 412 high or `usefulSize` / `minOpenH` ≤ 412. Stand-in `stats.test` 400 × 412, min 320 × 280, top-left `dx` 56 in both FR-SHELL-61 runs until ST2 registers `stats.board` | FR-SHELL-86 |
| Layout tests (`layoutRun` `autoPlaceProblems`; `test-cells.ts` `SIM10_AMBUSH_*`, `sim10Tokens`) | After every auto placement: no PC + 4 px under it and ≥ useful size while the look-ahead (brute, 16-px grid, useful floor; or the placed rect itself accepted) succeeds; auto-minimising only then or with the screen full, always FR-SHELL-78's first; brute check at the size taken; over PCs, PC area ≤ `brutePcArea`. sim-10 replays per client in `windows-s7.test.ts`. Goldens regenerated once (FU1): DM 158 / 162, player 63 / 92 steps changed, no rect smaller (useful sizes; the auto-minimise choices shift with them; 22 reopens now `reopened` under OQ-SHELL-55 B) | FR-SHELL-87 |
| Test hooks | `ui-window` `data-auto-minimised-for="<kind>"` while auto-minimised for that newcomer (cleared on restore); `data-placed` + `returned`; `ui-play-page` `data-auto-minimise-log` = the last placement's moves `parked>for;…` (test mode only) | FR-SHELL-88 |
| DM toast stack (`notifications/keep-out.ts` `dmStackPlacement`; `content-heights.ts` `reportFirstRow`, `firstRows`) | While a hard keep-out (the tool bar) is shown, every window's title bar, first `PUSHED_BODY_CLEAR` 40 px of body and first content row block the stack like a title bar (pushed windows included), so it shifts or drops below that strip before it takes "the least window body". `ShellWindow` reports each open window's first record row (`[data-record-id]` below the body top) as `{x, y, bottom}` at its rendered top-left (walkthrough U4: Scenes' "Goblin Ambush" row at 145…179, under title + 40 = 133); a report is matched to a window by that top-left (± 1 px). Players unchanged | FR-NOTE-44 |
| CON-44 | 0 B | — |

## 131. Sprint 7: map follow-ups (FU3)

From [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-88 (client part), -89–92 and [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBT-150 (#315: OQ-MAP-61 A; seams S6). **Built by `fix/fu-map`**: client only, no record type, intent, event, signal, frame or Konva layer (CON-44: 0 B). Amends §118's `pcsPan` row ("> 6 obstacles → null"), reveal-refit row ("once the scene-open fit ran": now the baseline that fit took) and `data-fit-reason` row. Data side: [data model §102](../data-model/README.md#102-sprint-7-map-follow-ups-fu3).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Companion paw priority (`label.ts` `BadgeIn.priority`, `LabelsLayer`) | The label row puts `priority` badges first; `LabelsLayer` marks `map-token-companion`. Where one row holds only the "+N", the paw stays as the chip beside it, or alone where both don't fit (the others then show only on the token's own overlays); squeezed rows the same. FU-combat's e2e note (§126: the paw folded into "+N" at 34 px) no longer applies | FR-MAP-88 |
| Label-row press (`label-hit.ts` `labelHitAt`, `labelRowItems`, `tokenIdAt`) | A pointer-down whose point lies in a token's label-row part (name pill, badges, chips: the children of its group in `map-labels`, topmost last) counts as a press on that token: the reveal refit's `press()` (not a pan gesture, no `moved`), no empty click (DM select); a stage `dblclick` there is the token's double-click (FR-MAP-77). The labels layer still doesn't listen; dragging from there pans as before | FR-MAP-89 (1) |
| Load / open fit (`MapCanvas`) | The scene-open fit waits like the appearance refit (next frame, re-armed by each token, window-rect or canvas-size change, ≤ 10 frames) so restored windows and grown heights are obstacles; the first on the page sets `data-fit-reason="load"`, later ones `open`. The reveal baseline is taken by that fit, so a token arriving after it is a reveal | FR-MAP-89 (2), (3) |
| No jump (`camera.ts` `appearanceKeeps(cam, tokens, obstacles, view)`) | An appearance refit (both clients) first tests the current camera: every footprint inside `visibleArea` and clear of every obstacle → no move, `data-fit-reason` unchanged; else the fit. DM Start notes the appearance after a second `settle` (Initiative opened and laid out, its grown height reported) | FR-MAP-90, FR-CBT-150 |
| Obstacle bound (`fitVisible`, `panAtScale`) | The bound of 6 counts, per scale, only the obstacles some token's forbidden pans reach inside the pan range; a scale past it isn't searched (was: > 6 obstacles in the area → `pcs` without search) | FR-MAP-90 |
| `pcs` pan (`pcsPan`) | Past 6 obstacles in the area: the 6 nearest the PCs' box (on screen at `from`; ties → larger, higher, more left), not null. No pan keeps every PC clear → `leastCoveredPan`: the pan (PCs inside the area) with the least PC footprint area under those obstacles, then nearest `from`, higher, left; candidates = every pan where a PC edge meets an obstacle edge (the overlap is bilinear between them). Worst case (36 tokens, 8 obstacles, no PC-clear pan) best of 5 ≈ 3 ms (gate 25 ms) | FR-MAP-90 |
| Fit past toasts (`fitPastToasts(tokens, grid, map, view, room, stripRoom)`; `useFreeArea().stripOnly`) | Fit (button, Reset layout), the resize refit and the appearance refit (FR-MAP-90, walkthrough U13) fit twice on the DM client: with the shown stack (`room`) and with only the summary strip (`stripRoom`). The first is `all` at ≥ `FIT_PAST_TOASTS_SHARE` 0.75 × the second's scale → the first; else the second when it is `all` or the first isn't either (`data-fit-reason="fit-past-toasts"`); else the first. So a tall stack (Start's initiative roll popup under a card) never turns an `all` fit into `pcs` (U13: was `pcs` 48 px, monsters under Initiative; now `all` 36 px, as Fit). Players and nothing shown: one fit. Info toasts (the overkill toast, S6) never report a shown stack | FR-MAP-91 |
| Resize refit | `createResizeRefit` (200 ms after the last `resize`) now schedules the settled fit (as the load fit: next frame, re-armed by window-rect / canvas-size changes) instead of fitting at once, then Fit's rules (FR-MAP-91); `moved` → none | FR-MAP-92 |
| `map-canvas` attrs | `data-fit-reason` + `load` \| `fit-past-toasts` | FR-MAP-89, -91 |
| Named outcomes (`fit-followups.test.ts`, both clients) | sim-10 b00 (1440×900, Scenes + Party + Initiative + Game log; was 53 px at (225, 72), Thorn under the Game log) → `pcs` 48 px at (328, 56): Thorn, Brakka, the Owlbear, the drake clear, Mira partly under Party (least PC area); with 7 obstacles the same three clear. b06's 24 px came from Scenes at its pushed spot (546, 60), toast or not; Scenes at home + the card → 36 px, the card clear (registry layout: 36 px, kept). b07 (sheet + 280 × 308 card) → one reveal refit, 24 px `all`; live with the card at 320 × 360 the reveal / load fit ends `pcs` (PCs clear) | FR-MAP-89–92 |
| CON-44 | 0 B | — |

| `stats.query {scope: "fight", encounterId?}` *(new)* | DM, player | unknown, planned or (player) not listed fight → `not_found`; an actor's 5th computed answer in 1 s with no earlier answer for that input → `conflict` "Too many stats queries, try again in a moment" (per viewer: role + player, all their tabs; not coalesced. *Sprint 9, FR-STATS-69:* the client keeps the last result and asks again once 1 s later with the latest input; only a second `conflict` shows "Couldn't load stats") | `{fight: {key, label, round, status, rows} \| null, fights: [{key, label, status}]}` (`fights` = the picker, newest first, ≤ 50) | FR-STATS-15, -16, -18 |
| `stats.query {scope: "sessions"}` *(new)* | DM, player | rate as above | `{sessions: [{fromN, toN, from, to, fights}]}` (≤ 200, newest first) | FR-STATS-13, -19 |
| `stats.query {scope: "session", n?}` *(new)* | DM, player | `n` in no session the viewer can list → `not_found`; rate as above | `{session: {fromN, toN, from, to, fights, awards[9]} \| null}` (absent `n` = the newest; null = none yet) | FR-STATS-12, -13 |
| `stats.showAwards {fromN}` *(new)* | DM (player → `forbidden`) | `fromN` not a session key → `not_found` | `{notificationId}` | FR-STATS-20 |
| `cbt.applyHp` + `sourceId?` *(changed)* | DM | not an active, non-removed combatant of the started encounter → `not_found` (the target itself allowed) | as before | FR-CBT-136 |

- **Read-only:** `stats.query` runs in the command pipeline with no write, event, log row or `seq` (answered in this connection's frame order). Results parse with the role's strict schema (`@shared/stats/schemas`: `PlayerFightRow` has no `hidden`, `downs`, `effective`, monster or special rows; ADR-0059 §2).
- **Viewer filter before aggregation:** a fact (`hp_change`, roll) counts only if its log line is visible: DM all; **players the hub filter for a viewer with no `playerId`** (public lines only, so every player gets the same answer); feature-tagged kinds skipped while off (DM too); undone and superseded lines never.
- **Keys and labels:** a fight's `key` = its encounter id (a player lists only fights with a public fact, whose public lines already carry the id); `label` = the DM's encounter name, a player's "Fight N · Tue 20 Oct" (N by `startedAt` among the fights they list; the date in the server's local time). A session's key = the unclipped `fromN` for every viewer; `from` / `to` are clipped to the viewer's visible entries.
- **Award** `{key, value, winners: [{characterId, name, playerId, with?}], more?, detail?: {from, to, raw, fight}}`; keys `biggest-hit`, `biggest-overkill`, `most-dealt`, `most-taken`, `most-healing`, `most-kills`, `most-nat20`, `most-nat1`, `most-downed`. Biggest overkill: the DM's `value` = the overkill amount; a player's = the callout line's `raw` (= `detail.raw`).
- **Rate (FR-STATS-16):** answers are memoised per (campaign, role + enabled features, input) and staled by a commit hook on any change to `game-log-entry`, `hp-change`, `combatant`, `encounter`, `entity` or `campaign`. A fresh memo is free; otherwise ≤ 4 computed answers per actor (role + player: commands see no connection) per second; the excess is answered with the last answer for that input (coalesced), or `conflict` when there is none.
- **Session boundary cache** (FR-STATS-14): server memory per campaign, extended from the last `n`; the active campaign's warmed by the start hook `stats-sessions`.

**Lines and notification** (no new record type or event)

| Thing | Visibility | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `cbt.attack` / `cbt.hp` payload `callout?: "overkill"` *(new)* | as the line: `hpAfter = 0` and `overkill ≥ max(10, hpBefore + absorbed)`, PCs too; the amount is DM-only: `cbt.attack` `dmPayload.overkill` (existing int), `cbt.hp` `dmPayload.overkill?` *(new)* | FR-CBT-137 |
| `cbt.hp` payload `sourced?: true` *(new)* + `actorName` = the source | the line reads "Sela → Mira, heal 8" / "Goblin → Mira, 8 damage"; a hidden source makes the line DM-only | FR-CBT-136 |
| Notification kind `stats-awards` *(new; `notifications/kinds.ts`)* | audience `all`, info, no action; payload `{fromN, toN, from, to, label}` (players' clipped times, "Tue 20 Oct"); text "Session awards: ‹label›" | FR-NOTE-48 |

- **Read helpers:** `combat-tracker/stats-read.ts`: `combatFacts(db, {encounterId} | {campaignId, fromN, toN})` (a session bounds every fact, `hp_change` and roll, by its line's `n`, never by time: a row may be stamped 1 ms after its line), `fightCombatants`, `fightsOf`, `publicFactEncounters`, `factRefs`; `dice-roll-log/stats-read.ts`: `logTimeline(db, campaignId, afterN?) → {n, at, public, feature}[]`, `rollFacts(db, viewer, campaignId, fromN, toN) → {rollId, natural, category, actorKind, actorId, public}[]` (final roll only: lines undone or superseded skipped, and a roll another roll replaces via `replacesRollId` never counts), `entryVisible(viewer, entry)`.
- **Fixtures:** units `st-past-crypt`, `st-ambush-facts`; bundles `stats-past` (base + the ended "Old Crypt", included first: one started fight per campaign), `stats` (= `stats-past` + `session-sim-10` + `st-ambush-facts`), `stats-e2e`; `session-sim-11` includes `stats-past`.
- **CON-44** (heavy harness): +189 B per viewer (9 callout lines in the window); worst, every combat line in the 100-row window a sourced callout: players ≈ +3.6 KB, DM ≈ +5 KB (DM ≤ 940 KB of 1.5 MiB with the sprint's worst; players ≤ 293 KB of 512 KiB). `stats-awards` rows sit in the closed-notification window (no growth). Stats add no snapshot field.

## 132. Sprint 7: stats core — `stats.query`, Show awards, From on heals, overkill line callout (ST-core)

From [stats](../specs/stats.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-STATS-01–16, -20, -21, -23, -25, [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#65-sprint-7-stats-facts) FR-CBT-136–138, [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICE-72, -73 and [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-48 (DM #311, #315; [ADR-0059](../architecture/adr/0059-stats-read-models.md)). **Built by `fix/st-core`** (server + shared + the DM dialog's From; the Stats window and toasts: ST2). Data side: [data model §102](../data-model/README.md#102-sprint-7-stats-core-st-core).

**Intents**

| Intent | Who | Errors (one code per case) | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `gem.spend {characterId, gem: "inspiration", entryId, manual?}` *(GM1: + `entryId`, `manual`)* | DM any character; player own | as GM0, plus: `entryId` on another gem / `manual` without `entryId` → `invalid`; a line the sender doesn't receive or unknown → `not_found` (checked before the count); `conflict`: "Already rerolled", "Undone", "Initiative can't be rerolled", "No d20 roll to reroll", "Only the newest 100 lines can be rerolled", "Not Mira's roll" (the roll's actor isn't the character), "Death and concentration saves are rerolled at the table" (a line with an outcome `note`, OQ-GEM-20 A), "The attack was already applied" / "The DM already ruled on this attack" (a combat to-hit whose card isn't pending / is ruled); `manual` not fitting the d20 term (count, 1–20, kept sum) → `invalid` | `{held, entryId, rerollEntryId}` | FR-GEM-18–21, -37 |
| `cbt.applyCard`, `cbt.applyHit` *(+ required `partRollIds {toHit: Id or null, extra: Id[]}`)* | DM | missing → `invalid`; another to-hit / extra part roll on the card than the popup's → `conflict` "The card changed — check and Apply again", nothing written (after `damageRollId`'s "Damage just arrived") | unchanged | FR-CBT-129 |

**Internal helpers** (no event of their own; the caller's `GemSpent` lists their changes)

| Helper | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `dice.reroll(env, {entryId, rollId, manual?}) → {roll, item, was, entry, superseded}` | entry kind `roll`, not undone / superseded; `rollId` its roll with a `1d20` / `2d20kh1` / `2d20kl1` term. New `roll` row (`replacesRollId`): only that term's dice new (`ctx.dice` or `manual`), every other die and the constant copied, roll hooks **not** run (the old item's `mods` copied); new `roll` entry = the old payload with the item replaced + `replaces {entryId, total}`, `category` / `public` / `audiencePlayerId` / `encounterId` / `dmPayload` copied, roller = the actor; then `gameLog.supersede` | FR-DICE-62, -64 |
| `gameLog.supersede(env, oldId, newId) → RecordChange` | `supersededBy` once; already set → `conflict` "Already rerolled", undone → `conflict` "Undone" | FR-DICE-63 |
| `cards.findToHitCard`, `cards.toHitBlock`, `cards.replaceToHit(env, cardId, item)` (`combat-tracker/cards.ts`) | the pending, unruled card takes the new to-hit (dice, constant, total incl. the copied mods, natural, manual); `crit` = natural 20; `updateNotification` (no toast, same `seq`). Crit changed with damage on the card → damage to `unusedRolls` (`{rollId, total}`, cap 3), `damage: null`, its offered `trigger_use` rows deleted, `diceMaxed` dropped; the next `cbt.rollDamage` of the row attaches (doubled after a natural 20) | FR-CBT-126, -127 |
| `rerollableRoll(entry)`, `countedRollIds(entries)` (`@shared/dice-roll-log/reroll.ts`) | the first d20 roll of a live, non-initiative `roll` entry; the rolls of entries neither undone nor superseded (stats contract) | FR-DICE-67, -70 |

**Events and visibility**

| Event | Changes (order) | Player sees |
|---|---|---|
| `GemSpent {…, rerollEntryId, supersededEntryId, cardId?, rollIds: [new roll]}` | `character-gems`, the `gem.spend` line (n), the new `roll` entry (n + 1), the old entry's supersede upsert; then a pending card's `NotificationUpdated` | the count and the public spend line; the new / old entries per the entry rule (audience copied, so a reroll never reveals more); the card only its owner (and the DM) |

- **Spend line target** (`gem.spend` payload + `target? {kind: "entry", id, label, from, to?}`; new `dmPayload {target}`): a public target → `payload.target` ("Mira spends an Inspiration gem — rerolls Perception (7)"); a narrower one (hidden roll, companion line) → `payload.target: null` + `dmPayload.target` (players: "Mira spends an Inspiration gem"). FR-GEM-13.
- **Walkthrough fixes:** `cbt.applyHit` now also gives its card `ruled: true` (`NotificationUpdated`, no toast, after `HitApplied`; amends FR-CBT-88's "card unchanged"): the owner, who never gets the DM-only `hit-ruling`, learns only that a ruling exists, so the reroll isn't offered. The window rule is shared (`inRerollWindow(n, newest)`: `newest - n < 100`, entries numbered per campaign and never deleted); the server passes the campaign's newest `n`, a client its store's. The confirm's 4 s timer stops while the physical form is open; a close that sent nothing resets the roll control.
- **Gems off** (GM0's trace, now written): a superseded entry hidden, `replaces` stripped, for every viewer; spend lines hidden (tagged kind). On → back. FR-GEM-05.
- **Client:** `dice-log-entry` + `data-superseded`; a superseded line struck through and dimmed with `dice-log-rerolled` (+ `data-record-id` = the new entry) → `focusLogEntry`; undone + superseded → "undone" only. Entry actions `dice-log-actions` / `dice-log-action` (+ `data-value` = action id, `data-record-id` = entry), confirm `dice-log-action-confirm` / `-yes` / `-no` (4 s). Gems' `gem.reroll` ("Inspiration: reroll", lucide `sparkles`, "Spend?"): owner and DM, ≥ 1 Inspiration, gems on, an eligible line (combat to-hit: card pending and unruled in the viewer's store); a `RollButton` on ✓, so physical mode asks for the d20(s) or "Roll for me". DM popup `cbt-popup-crit-changed` and the owner's card "Critical changed by a reroll — roll damage again" (`critChanged(p)`: to-hit card, no damage, unused rolls; 0 B).
- **Fixtures:** unit `gems-rolls` (Mira's Perception 7 and adv Stealth 11 by `player-1`, the DM's hidden "DM-SECRET-GEM" 8, an initiative line), added to bundle `gems`; e2e alias `gems-reroll-e2e` (`e2e/gems-reroll.e2e.ts`). `session-sim-11` adds none (gems off).
- **CON-44** (heavy harness, `con44-supersede.test.ts`): 33 superseded + 33 `replaces` → +3,564 B per viewer (DM 694,182 B of 1.5 MiB; players ≤ 292,511 B of 512 KiB); 0 B without a reroll. A reroll's frame: spend line + new entry + supersede upsert (+ the card for its audience).

## 133. Sprint 7: Stats window — Fight leaderboard, Session awards, live refresh, overkill toast, Show awards (ST2)

From [stats](../specs/stats.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-STATS-17–20, -22, -24, -25, [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-93, -94 and [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-48 (views), -49 (DM #311, #315; OQ-NOTE-28 A). **Built by `build/st-window`**: browser only. The intents, ack shapes, `callout` line fields and the `stats-awards` kind are [§130](#130-sprint-7-stats-core--statsquery-show-awards-from-on-heals-overkill-line-callout-st-core)'s; no new record type, intent, event, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Data side: [data model §104](../data-model/README.md#104-sprint-7-stats-window-st2).

| Kind | Owner | Clients | Default open | Default geometry | Launcher | Gate | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `stats.board` *(new; `@ui/features/stats/window`, one line per client registry)* | STATS | both | no (parkable, never protected, no keep-out) | top-left `dx` 56, 400×412, min 320×280, no `usefulSize` (useful 400×360 at the four sizes, FR-SHELL-79); body scrolls; goldens + size-table row added (FR-SHELL-86) | "Stats" (lucide `Trophy`, order 59: right after Party); no `activity`; `companion` = the stats runtime | — | FR-SHELL-93 |

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Body (`StatsWindow.tsx`) | Tabs Fight / Session (`stats-tab` + `data-value`); only the shown tab queries. Renders the ack as is (rows in server order, no client aggregation); parsed with the viewer's role schema (`DmFightResult` / `PlayerFightResult`, `SessionResult`, `SessionsResult`) | FR-STATS-18, -19 |
| Fight tab | `stats-fight-picker` (the ack's `fights`, value = encounter id; none picked = the server's default); `stats-fight-status` ("Round N" / "Ended"); `stats-fight-table` of `stats-fight-row` (`data-record-id` = combatant key, `data-kind`; companions "↳" under the owner): `stats-fight-name` / `-dealt` / `-taken` / `-healed` / `-kills`. DM: monster and special rows, `data-hidden` + `stats-fight-hidden` (eye-off), column "Eff." `stats-fight-effective` (effective dealt; Tooltip: dealt · taken · healed · downs) | FR-STATS-18 |
| Session tab | `stats-session-picker` (`sessions`, value = `fromN`; label "Tue 20 Oct · 19:02–23:41 · 3 fights", browser local time, "Today" for today's; none picked = the newest); `stats-awards` grid of 9 `stats-award` (`data-record-id` = award key): title, `stats-award-value` (sum / count / max + unit, "—" = nobody), `stats-award-winner` (`data-record-id` = character id, player colour via `colorOf`, "Mira + Owlbear"), "+N more", `stats-award-detail`. 3 columns from a 372-px body (window ≈ 400 px), else 1 (container query). DM: `stats-show-awards` → `stats.showAwards {fromN}`; a reject toasts | FR-STATS-19, -20, -25 |
| Refresh (`refresh.ts`, `query.ts`) | Per query: asks on open and on a picker / tab change (an older input's answer dropped); a live `game-log-entry` upsert re-asks 1 s later (trailing, coalesced; Fight: lines of the picked fight, or of any fight while none is picked; Session: any line); each snapshot (reconnect, resync) re-asks at once; minimised (`useWindowShown`) asks nothing and asks once on restore if anything came; one request in flight. `not_found` for a picked fight / session → back to the default | FR-STATS-17, -23 |
| States | `stats-loading` (skeleton rows, first answer); `stats-error` "Couldn't load stats" + `stats-retry`; `stats-empty` "No fight yet" / "No damage yet this fight" / "No sessions yet"; offline: the last result muted (`opacity-60`), pickers and Show awards off, `data-state="offline"` on `stats-fight` / `stats-session` | FR-STATS-24 |
| Overkill toast (`runtime.tsx`, `logic.ts` `overkillToast`) | From live event frames only (`useEventFrame`: never a snapshot, reload or resync): the frame's first `cbt.attack` **or `cbt.hp` damage** upsert with `callout`, not undone, each line once. `stats-overkill-toast` "Overkill! Thorn → Goblin 1: 30 damage", the DM + " (+23 over 7 HP)" from `dmPayload.overkill`; sonner `toast` id `stats-overkill` (one at a time), 4 s; no notification row, bell or history; mounted as the window's companion, so it shows with the window closed | FR-STATS-22, FR-NOTE-49 |
| Awards open (`open.ts` `openStatsAwards(fromN)`) | A live frame caused by `NotificationCreated` with a `stats-awards` row (payload parsed with `StatsAwardsPayload`) → the viewer's view `{tab: "session", sessionFromN}` (page memory, key campaign + DM / player id), then `openWindow("stats.board", "main", {by: "feature"})`: a closed window auto-placed, a minimised one restored, an open one fronted and switched | FR-SHELL-94 |
| `stats-awards` view (`note-view.tsx`, both `notification-views.ts`) | lucide `Trophy`; the text, then **Open** `note-stats-awards-open` (client-only, not an action) → `openStatsAwards(fromN)`; info toast per FR-NOTE-13 (OQ-NOTE-28 A) | FR-NOTE-48 |

## 134. Sprint 7: Combat gem (GM2)

From [gems](../specs/gems.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-GEM-05, -13, -25–31, -34, -37, -38 and [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBT-128–135 (#140, #315; OQ-GEM-07 A, -08 A, -13 A, -14 A, -21 B, OQ-CBT-87 A; rulings-a #1, #15, #16). **Built by `build/gems-combat`**. Data side: [data model §105](../data-model/README.md#105-sprint-7-combat-gem-gm2).

**Intents**

| Intent | Who | Errors (one code per case) | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `gem.spend {characterId, gem: "combat", cardId, dice: [{part: "base" or 0–1, index}] 1–3, known?: [{part, dice: int[]}]}` *(GM2: + `cardId`, `dice`, `known`)* | DM any character (OQ-GEM-14 A); player own | as GM0, plus: `combat` without `cardId` + `dice`, or `cardId` / `dice` / `known` on another gem → `invalid`; a card the sender doesn't receive or unknown → `not_found`; the attacker isn't the character's combatant → `conflict` "Not Brakka's attack"; more dice than the level allows → `invalid` "Level 6 needed for 2 dice" / "Level 12 needed for 3 dice"; then `cards.planMaxDice`: card not pending → `conflict` "Already applied", no damage → `conflict` "No damage on the card yet", `diceMaxed` → `conflict` "Dice already maxed on this card"; `invalid`: "No such damage part", "No such die", "A dropped die can't be maxed", "Already at its max", "Each die once", "Enter each die first" (a physical total without `known`), "Enter N dice", "A die is above its faces", "The dice add up to X, not Y", "This part's dice are known". All checked before the count: a reject writes nothing | `{held, entryId}` | FR-GEM-25–28, -37 |
| `cbt.applyCard` | DM | unchanged, except: a non-null `damageRollId` the card has replaced since (a maxed base part) → `conflict` "The card changed — check and Apply again" (null → still "Damage just arrived") | unchanged | FR-CBT-129 |

**Internal helpers** (`combat-tracker/cards.ts`; no event of their own: the caller's `GemSpent` names the new rows)

| Helper | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `cards.planMaxDice(env, cardId, picks, known?) → MaxDicePlan` | checks only (above); per touched part the dice with every value known and the picks at their faces; `dice [{part, type, faces, from, to}]`; `from` / `to` = the card's damage (base total incl. mods + every extra part) before / after | FR-CBT-128 |
| `cards.applyMaxDice(env, plan, ids?) → rollIds` | per touched part a new `roll` row (a copy with the picked dice at their faces, `replacesRollId` = the old row, `manual` kept, `total` recomputed, roller = the actor); the card part takes `rollId`, `dice`, `total` (base: old card total − old row + new row, so mods stay) and `maxed` (picked indices, ascending); card `diceMaxed: true`; `updateNotification` (no toast, same `seq`). `ids`: the rows' ids, named by the caller's event first | FR-CBT-128 |
| `cards.maxDice(env, cardId, picks, known?)` | plan + apply | FR-CBT-128 |

**Events and visibility**

| Event | Changes (order) | Player sees |
|---|---|---|
| `GemSpent {characterId, gem: "combat", cost: 1, held, entryId, cardId, rollIds: [new rows]}` | `character-gems`, the `gem.spend` line; then the card's `NotificationUpdated` | the count and the public spend line; the card only its audience (owner + DM, or the DM alone), as before |

- **Spend line** (`gem.spend` payload `target {kind: "card", id, label = attack name, from, to}` + `dice [{part?, faces, from, to}]` ≤ 3; `dmPayload {target, dice}`): the owner's card with a visible attacker → `payload` ("Brakka spends a Combat gem — Flame Tongue greatsword: slashing d6 3 → 6 (rolled 21 → 24)"; `part` = the damage type only when the card has more than one part); a DM-made card or a hidden attacker → `payload.target: null` + `dmPayload` (players: "Brakka spends a Combat gem"). "rolled" because the DM may apply a typed value (OQ-CBT-87 A). FR-GEM-13, -30.
- **Apply** reads the card's (new) roll rows: the maxed totals; `hp_change.rollIds` lists the new rows; the DM's `cbt.attack` line `dmPayload.rolls.damage.maxed` / `rolls.extra[i].maxed` label those chips `max` (`MAX_DIE_LABEL`); the public text is unchanged. Undo reopens the card with `maxed` / `diceMaxed`; End / Dismiss dismiss it; no refund. FR-CBT-131, -133.
- **Gems off** (FR-GEM-05, OQ-GEM-21 B): the attack line's `rolls.*.maxed` are stripped (`gemsAttackTrace`, declared on `cbt.attack`). The card's `maxed` / `diceMaxed` are stripped too (`gemsCardTrace`, declared on the `damage-card` kind through the new notification kind `traces?`); dice and totals stay (the final roll).
- **Client:** `cbt-card-actions` (+ `data-record-id` = card). Gems' `gem.combat`: `gem-combat` (+ `data-record-id`) → `gem-combat-picker`; chips `gem-combat-die` (+ `data-part`, `data-index`, `data-state` = `open` | `picked` | `max` | `dropped`); physical totals `gem-combat-known` (+ `data-part`, `data-index`) and `gem-combat-known-error`; `gem-combat-submit` ("Spend (n/limit)", the one confirm), `gem-combat-cancel`, `gem-error`. Faces come from the roll's log line (the card keeps `[value, kept]`). Maxed dice: `cbt-card-die-max` (+ `data-part`, `data-index`; "max" Badge, Tooltip "Set to max") in the popup's damage cell (extra parts too) and the player's card view (`cbt-card-maxed`).
- **Fixtures:** no new unit (`gems`: Brakka level 5, Combat 2, the Flame Tongue greatsword); e2e alias `gems-combat-e2e` (`e2e/gems-combat.e2e.ts`). `session-sim-11` adds none (gems off).
- **CON-44** (heavy harness, `con44-maxed.test.ts`): 29 B a maxed card (1 die; 33 B with 3), 20 pending maxed cards → DM +580 B (691,159 B of 1.5 MiB); players +87…+116 B for their own 3–4 cards (≤ 289,034 B of 512 KiB). A spend's frame: count + spend line + the card for its audience.

## 135. Sprint 8: table-tools core — macros, roll requests, audio assets, pause and sound fields (TT0)

From [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-64, -67, -73, -77, -92, -93, [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICE-79, -80, -83, [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-58, -64, [assets-pipeline](../specs/assets-pipeline.md#64-sprint-8-audio-assets-track-tt) FR-ASSET-54, -57, -59 and [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CAMP-46, -48 (#334, #335; [ADR-0061](../architecture/adr/0061-roll-requests.md)). **Built by `fix/tt-core`** (records, zod, events, kind def, helpers; no intent: macros / requests TT1+2, sounds TT3, pause TT4). Data side: [data model §106](../data-model/README.md#106-sprint-8-table-tools-core-tt0).

**Record types** (scope `campaign`)

| Type | Visibility | Snapshot | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `roll-macro {id, campaignId, playerId \| null, creatureId \| null, name, expression, category, order, createdBy \| null}` *(new)* | DM full; a player iff `playerId` = them (DM-only rows, `playerId` null, never). `createdBy` null = the DM's row (read-only for players) | every row (≤ 200) | FR-DICEX-64, -67 |
| `roll-request {id, campaignId, seq, roll {kind, key, expression}, label, dc, dcShown, mode, results, state, targets [{characterId, playerId, name, notificationId, state, entryId, total, natural, success, at}] 1–12, createdAt, closedAt}` *(new)* | **`audience: dm`**: no player key, change or load | window: open pinned + newest 10 closed by `seq` (the provider reads only that, SQL) | FR-DICEX-73 |
| `audio-asset {id, campaignId, seq, name, mime, bytes, durationMs, createdAt}` *(new)* | **`audience: dm`**, **lazy `all`** (ADR-0045): in no snapshot; `sync.load` DM only (player → `forbidden`) | — | FR-ASSET-57 |
| `asset` | unchanged, but **images only** (`kind = image`): a sound never appears in it | as before | FR-ASSET-57 |
| `campaign` | + `pause {message \| null, imageAssetId \| null, backAt \| null, startedAt}` only while paused; + `alertSounds {turn?, request?, pause?, resume?: assetId \| "none"}` only when some alert has a pick. Every connection of the campaign. `toCampaignRecord` takes the whole row, so every campaign upsert (rename, colour, switch) keeps both | as before | FR-CAMP-46, -48 |

**Notification kind** `roll-request` *(new; `kinds.ts` append; toast class **card**, FR-NOTE-52)*: audience `player` = the PC's owner, action "Roll" owned by that player; payload `{requestId, characterId, characterName, roll, label, dc | null, mode}` (strict: no `success` / `results`; `dc` null unless shown; ≤ 1 KB); resolution `{outcome: rolled | declined | missed, total?}`; `genericResolve: false` (`note.resolve` → `forbidden`), no end rule.

**Domain events** (`src/shared/events/dice-extras.ts`, `campaign-management.ts`; all logged, none undoable; emitted by TT1–TT4)

| Event | Changes | Player sees |
|---|---|---|
| `MacroSaved {macroId}` / `MacroDeleted {macroId}` | `roll-macro` upsert(s) / delete | iff `playerId` = them; else no frame |
| `RollRequested {requestId}` | `roll-request` upsert (with `NotificationCreated` per target) | **no frame** (FR-SYNC-17); a target gets only its own prompt |
| `RollRequestAnswered {requestId, characterId, entryId}` | answer entry + `roll-request` upsert (then `NotificationResolved`) | the entry per its audience (public, or `audiencePlayerId` = them); the roller their resolve |
| `RollRequestDeclined {requestId, characterId}` | `roll-request` upsert (then `NotificationResolved`) | the target's player their resolve |
| `RollRequestClosed {requestId, rolled?: entryId[]}` | `roll-request` upsert + the entries rolled for missing targets (then `NotificationResolved` per open target, by rule) | the entries per audience; each target's player their resolve |
| `CampaignPaused {campaignId}` / `CampaignResumed {campaignId}` | `campaign` upsert | every connection (no resync) |
| `AlertSoundSet {alert}` | `campaign` upsert | every connection |

**Internal helpers** (server only; no intent field reaches them)

| Helper | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `gameLog.append(env, {…, audience?})` | `audience` `{public: true}` or `{public: false, audiencePlayerId}` stamps the entry (hidden = DM + that player), whoever rolls; wins over the role / category stamp and `forcePrivate`. A player-sent `public` stays `forbidden` on every intent | FR-DICE-79, -83 |
| `roll` payload item `answer?: true` | a request answer (data only, no line tag); `rerollableRoll` → null and `isAnswerEntry(entry)` (`@shared/dice-roll-log/reroll`): never rerollable | FR-DICE-80 |
| `assets.require(env, id, {kind?})` | default `image`; a row of the other kind → `invalid` "Not an image" / "Not a sound"; unknown → `not_found` | FR-ASSET-59 |
| `dice-extras/table-rows.ts` | `assertMacroRoom` (≤ 24 per list, ≤ 200 per campaign → `conflict`), `assertRequestRoom` (≤ 5 open → `conflict`), row ↔ record mappers | FR-DICEX-64, -74 |

- **Zod** (`@shared/dice-extras/macros`, `requests`; `@shared/assets-pipeline/audio`; `@shared/campaign-management/table-state`): records, the TT1 / TT2 intents (`dicex.saveMacro`, `deleteMacro`, `moveMacro`, `request`, `answerRequest`, `declineRequest`, `closeRequest`, `rollForMissing`; none takes `public` or an audience), the prompt payload / resolution, the pause card, the picks.
- **Image route** `GET /api/assets/:id/{thumb,display}`: a sound → the common 404 (its own route is TT3's).
- **Fixtures:** bundle `session-sim-12` (= `session-sim-11` + an empty sprint-8 section, one comment per track WF / TT / SB / CB); no unit yet.
- **CON-44** (`src/server/ws/testing/con44-tt.test.ts`, heavy harness at the caps): DM 690,790 → 894,324 B (+203,534 B; 678,540 B left of 1.5 MiB): `roll-macro` 72,390 B (200 rows), `roll-request` 70,526 B (5 open + 10 closed × 12 targets), prompts 60,185 B (60); players +10,500…+39,461 B (worst 325,266 B of 512 KiB); `audio-asset` 0 B in every snapshot (`sync.load` of 50 = 13,967 B).

## 136. Sprint 8: combat polish core — `cbtx.moveCombatant`, tie order (CB0)

From [combat-extras](../specs/combat-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBTX-64–69 and [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBT-162–164, -168 (#334, #335; OQ-CBTX-31 A′, -32 A, -33 A, OQ-CBT-99 A, -100 A; review-b #16, #23). **Built by `fix/cb-core`**. Data side: [data model §107](../data-model/README.md#107-sprint-8-combat-polish-core-cb0). Schema lane: merged after TT0.

**Intents**

| Intent | Who | Errors (one code per case) | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cbtx.moveCombatant {encounterId, combatantId, afterId: ID \| null}` (`CBTX_INTENTS.moveCombatant`, strict; `afterId` null = top) | DM; player → `forbidden` | ids of no / another campaign or encounter → `not_found`; encounter not `started` → `conflict` "Combat is not running"; combatant inactive → `conflict` "Goblin is not in combat", removed → `conflict` "Restore Goblin first", riding its owner (`after-owner` / `in-owner-turn` with the owner in the fight) → `conflict` "Goblin 2 moves with Mira"; `afterId` itself, inactive, removed, riding its owner or without initiative → `invalid` "Drop below a row with initiative"; no other row has initiative → `conflict` "Roll initiative first"; a PC without a number and no PC number around → `conflict` "Set a number first". Same order as today → `ack`, no write, no event | none | FR-CBTX-64 |

**Rule** (`planMove(rows, movedId, afterId)` in `@shared/combat-tracker/rules`, pure; the server and CB1's DM number hint run it). `rows` = movable rows (active, not removed, not riding the owner) by `position`. **(a)** a private moved row (not a PC) takes the anchor's number (top: the first other numbered row's). **(b)** a PC (hidden ones too: a reveal never shows a monster's number on a PC) keeps its number, lowered to the nearest PC above the drop spot and raised to the nearest PC below; private rows above it with a lower number, or below it with a higher one, take that number. Changed numbers null `initRollId`; no roll, no log line. Then `tieOrder` 1…k on every movable row with the arranged number, in the dropped order (others keep theirs; removed rows keep theirs), then `rerank`. Order everywhere: `compareOrder` = initiative desc → `tieOrder` asc (null after any number) → init bonus → PC → `seq`; riding companions follow their owner (FR-CBT-162).

**Tie order cleared** (FR-CBT-163 (c)): `cbt.setInitiative` and `cbt.rollInitiative` (and Start / add rolls) write `tieOrder: null` on the row; `rerank` clears it on any row riding its owner (turn mode or owner change onto attached). Remove / restore, hide / reveal, death keep it; Next / Prev never re-sort; new combatants start null.

**Events and visibility**

| Event | Changes | DM | Players | Undoable |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CombatantMoved {encounterId, combatantId, afterId \| null, initiativeBefore \| null, initiativeAfter}` (new, `src/shared/events/combat-extras.ts`; logged) | `combatant` upserts: the moved row (`initiative`, `initRollId`, `tieOrder`), rewritten tie rows, reranked rows (`position`, `playerPosition`). No `encounter` change (the turn never moves, FR-CBT-164) | full | each changed visible row's redaction (no `tieOrder`, never a monster's number); unchanged redactions dropped; **`quietWhenUnchanged`**: a move players can't see (hidden, fog-hidden, `ownerHidden` row) sends them **no frame** (FR-CBTX-67) | no (drag back) |

- No `TurnChanged`, turn alert, `turnChanged` rule hook, `movedFt` reset or expiry on a move (FR-CBT-164).
- **Server registration:** `combatPolish` feature line (`src/server/features/combat-extras/move.ts`) in the server feature list.
- **Fixtures:** units `cb-ties`, `cb-fog-goblin`, `cb-party` (join `session-sim-12`, sim-11-safe); bundle `combat-polish` (= `session-sim-11` + the three); e2e aliases `cb-reorder-e2e` (`e2e/cb-core.e2e.ts`), `cb-turn-ring-e2e`, `cb-party-e2e`.
- **CON-44** (heavy harness, `con44-cb.test.ts`): `tieOrder` on 46 DM combatant rows → DM +736 B (null) / +635 B (all set), 691,492 B of 1.5 MiB; players 0 B (byte-identical, worst 289,088 B of 512 KiB).

## 137. Sprint 8: alert sounds — audio upload + probe, sound picks, shell playback (TT3)

From [assets-pipeline](../specs/assets-pipeline.md#64-sprint-8-audio-assets-track-tt) FR-ASSET-52–61, [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CAMP-48–50, [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-130–134 and [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBT-41 (#334, #335). **Built by `build/tt-sounds`** on TT0's records and events ([§135](#135-sprint-8-table-tools-core--macros-roll-requests-audio-assets-pause-and-sound-fields-tt0)). Data side: [data model §108](../data-model/README.md#108-sprint-8-alert-sounds-tt3).

**REST**

| Route | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/assets` (DM `Bearer`) | Content sniffed (never the name or `Content-Type`): `RIFF…WAVE`, `OggS`, `ID3` or an MPEG Layer III frame → a **sound**, probed (`audio-probe.ts`, hand-rolled, no dependency): > 512 KB → `413` "Too large (max 512 KB)"; header unreadable / not PCM-float WAV, Vorbis / Opus OGG or Layer III → `415`; > 5,000 ms → `422` "Too long (max 5 s)". Code stays `invalid` (the status carries the cause; `RestErrorCode` has no `too_long`). New optional multipart field **`kind`** `image` \| `audio`: what the uploader expects; other content → `415` ("Not a sound (MP3, OGG or WAV)" for `audio`); anything else in the field → `400`. A sound ignores `listed` / `tags`. `201` created / `200` existing (same hash in the campaign), body `{asset}` in the image shape (sizes 0). 51st sound in a campaign → `409` "Sound library full (50)" (a duplicate still answers `200`) | FR-ASSET-52, -55, -58, -60 |
| `GET /api/assets/:id/audio` (no credential; the ID is the capability, ADR-0023) | The stored original, `Content-Type` = the row's mime, `Cache-Control: private, max-age=31536000, immutable`, `ETag "<id>-audio"` (`304` on match), `nosniff`; iff the asset is in the active campaign, of kind `audio`, its file present; any mismatch (image ID, bad ID, other campaign, file lost) → the common 404 body | FR-ASSET-56 |

**Intent** `camp.setAlertSound {alert: turn | request | pause | resume, sound: "default" | "none" | assetId}` (DM; WS): `default` removes the key, `none` silences, an asset via `assets.require(…, {kind: "audio"})` (image → `invalid`, unknown → `not_found`); bad key / value → `invalid`; player → `forbidden`; same value → ack, no event. Else `AlertSoundSet {alert}` with the `campaign` upsert to every connection (logged, not undoable; two DM tabs: last wins). FR-CAMP-48.

- **Record flow** (TT0's types): an upload's `AssetIngested` carries an **`audio-asset`** upsert (DM only; never `asset`); players get no frame. The library reaches the DM only by `sync.load {type: "audio-asset"}` (the Campaign options section, on open); a player's load → `forbidden`. Players learn a sound's ID only as a pick in `campaign.alertSounds`, and fetch only the picked sounds' capability URLs. `assets.setTags` on a sound → `not_found` (FR-ASSET-60).
- **Client playback** (`play-shell/{sounds,audio,alerts}.ts`, `AlertLayer.tsx`): `alert({…, sound?: key | id, flash?})`; new `useAlertSound()(key)` (sound alone; TT2 / TT4 call it for `request`, `pause`, `resume`). A key resolves via `campaign.alertSounds`: asset → that sound; `none` → silent (nothing recorded); absent → `ping` for turn / request, silent for pause / resume. Picks preload at idle after the snapshot and on change (`fetch` + `decodeAudioData` on an offline context, once per asset ID); not ready or undecodable → `ping`, outcome `fallback`, one console warning (no ID). Shared context, a gain per sound, every source stopped at 5 s. One at a time: due while another plays → `skipped`; due in one task → turn > request > resume > pause (a higher one cuts a lower; a lower one is dropped). Muted / volume 0 / locked → nothing for any key. Test mode `data-test-last-sound` = `<key>:<played | muted | locked | failed | skipped | fallback>:<n>` (plain ids keep `<sound>`). The turn alert (FR-CBT-41) passes key `turn`. FR-SHELL-130–134.
- **Test IDs:** DM `camp-sounds`, `camp-sound` (+ `data-value` = key), `camp-sound-select` (`data-value` = pick or `default`), `camp-sound-test` (`data-outcome` after a click), `camp-sound-upload`, `camp-sound-file`, `camp-sound-error`; players' Sound section `ui-sound-test-key` (`data-value`), `ui-sound-test` (`data-outcome`).
- **Fixtures:** units `audio-chime` (generated 0.4 s PCM WAV, 17,684 B; `ingestStep` gains a bytes form `{bytes, filename}`; manifest type `audio-asset`) and `camp-alert-sounds` (request, pause → Chime) join `session-sim-12`; bundles `alert-sounds` (`mid-combat` + both), `alert-sounds-e2e`. Upload-limit files `e2e/support/audio.ts` (`bun e2e/support/audio.ts <dir>` writes `big.wav`, `long.wav`, `fake.mp3`).
- **CON-44:** no new snapshot record; `audio-asset` 0 B (lazy); the picks' +150 B and the 50-sound load are measured by `con44-tt.test.ts` (§135).

| Crossing hint | after the move's ack, round ≥ 1, from the order before and after (client only): crossed the turn row upwards "Goblin now acts before Mira: no turn for it this round", downwards "… acts after Mira: it acts again this round", the moved block holds the turn "Mira's turn goes on; next: Goblin 2" (Next's row, skipping as Next does). Silent info toast | `cbt-init-move-hint` | FR-CBT-165 |

## 139. Sprint 8: one window placer (WF1)

From [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-95–119, [ADR-0060](../architecture/adr/0060-one-window-placer.md). **Built by `fix/placer-core`.** No intent, event or record change: the shell is client-side (0 B).

| Seam | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Placer (`play-shell/placer.ts`) | `settle(windows, trigger, ctx) → Plan {windows, memory, placed, pcClear, putAway, log, yields}`, pure. Triggers `load` (`drawn`: after the first token draw), `open` (`fresh`, `by`), `reopen`, `appear`, `settle`, `viewport`, `push`, `return`, `drag-end`, `reset`. The manager's `plan(ws, action, ctx)` / `reduce` call it for every action; close / minimise / focus move nothing (a close of a zone's or squeeze's cause returns what it displaced). `autoPlace`, `pushWindows`, `followDefaults`, `clearAround`, `followViewport` are gone | FR-SHELL-95 |
| Ladder | L1 target size → L2 useful + the protected squeeze (Game log, then DM Initiative / player sheet, then player Initiative) → L2b map yields (players, `fitted`, `ctx.dryFit` ≥ 75 % of `ctx.lastFullCell`, ≥ 24 px) → L3 put away the LRU launcher window when the look-ahead helps → L4 below useful → L5 least PC area (`data-pc-clear` absent); each rung at 12 px, retried at 4 px; a window displaced for a transient cause that would land at L5 is put away instead | FR-SHELL-97, -98, -101, -108 |
| Zones | protected windows without `userSpot` take their named default rect (`fitted` camera: always, the map refits; `moved`: iff PC-clear, else the ladder among the protected windows, a still-valid earlier spot kept); launcher windows over them, user spots included, are displaced (`window:<kind>`) and return when it closes or ends | FR-SHELL-99, -107 |
| `ManagerContext` additions | `memory`, `camera` (`[data-testid=map-canvas]` `data-camera`), `dryFit` + `lastFullCell` (the canvas's base port: WF3 wires it), `settledViewport`, `autoMinimised` | FR-SHELL-96 |
| Controller (`shell-store.ts`) | `setBlocked(b)`: while a `blocksInput` overlay is up, actions queue (one per window and action kind, last kept) and run in order when it goes; options `available`, `camera`, `dryFit`, `lastFullCell`, `onYield` | FR-SHELL-112 |
| Page hooks (`ShellContext`, `PlayFrame`) | `viewport` settles 200 ms after the last resize; `load` again once the map's load fit drew tokens; a kind going unavailable dispatches `return`; chrome keep-outs showing / changing push, going return | FR-SHELL-95, -102, -113 |
| Registries | `overlays.ts` `{id, clients, layer, blocksInput?(store), Component}` (layer above the alert flash, below the dice layer); `top-bar-items.ts` `{id, clients, width (px, or per viewport since TT4), order, Component}` (before the identity chip); `chrome-keep-outs.ts` `{id, clients, rect(vp, store) \| null, fitObstacle}` (hard keep-out; `chromeFitObstacles` for the fit); all append-only, empty | FR-SHELL-112, -113 |

- **Test IDs / hooks:** `ui-window` `data-placed` + `zone`, `displaced`, `grown`, `squeezed`; `data-auto-minimised-cause` (`toolbar:…`, `viewport:…`, `window:…`, `open:…`); `ui-window-body`; `ui-play-page` `data-settle-log` = "trigger:kind>rung;…" (test mode); `ui-overlay-layer` (`data-blocking`), `[data-overlay-id]`; `ui-topbar-item` + `data-record-id`.
- **Harness:** `fixtures/units/play-shell/replay-cells.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-114) → `replay-cells.generated.ts`; `src/client/{dm,player}/features/play-shell/replay.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-115, -116; known failures in `replay.failing.json`, run as `test.failing`).
- **CON-44:** 0 B.
