No new intent, event, record type or signal. Data side: [data model §110](../data-model/README.md#110-sprint-8-stats-tier-b-core-sb0).

**Wire changes**

| Item | Who | Change | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Outcome line payload (`cbt.attack`, `cbt.hp`) | DM all; players public lines (+ a companion owner's audience lines), as the line | + `fell?: "kill" \| "down"` (14 B), stamped at apply beside `callout` (`cbt.applyCard` per target incl. late damage, `cbt.applyHp` damage): `hpBefore > 0 → 0` on a non-companion monster = `kill`, on a PC or companion = `down` (`fellOf`, `@shared/stats/callout`). Line text, `hp_change` and events unchanged; old rows parse | FR-CBT-152, FR-STATS-26 |
| `stats.query {scope: "fight", encounterId?}` | DM, player | ack `fight` + `highlights` (`DmHighlights` / `PlayerHighlights`, keys = FR-STATS-36's ids: `mvp`, `biggest-hit`, `biggest-overkill`, `most-healing` `{value, winners ≤ 8, more?, detail?}`; `kills`, `downed` `{events ≤ 20 [{fell, from, to, callout}], more?}`; `nat20`, `nat1`; `rounds`; `duration` ms; DM + `standing`, winners' / events' `hidden`, a callout event's `overkill`). Optional in the schema (tier-A acks parse); the server always sends it. Player default (BL-69): the started fight even before a public fact, label "This fight" (also by id) | FR-STATS-31, -32, -35, -52 |
| `stats.query {scope: "luck", n? \| campaign: true}` *(new scope)* | DM, player | `n` in no listable session → `not_found` | `{luck: {scope: "session" \| "campaign", fromN?, pending?: true, rows ≤ 70 [{key, name, playerId, rolls, d20s, avg, nat20, nat1}]}}`; DM + row `dm-dice` ("DM dice": the DM, unowned monsters, unowned tokens); an owned creature's roll (pet, summon, companion, ADR-0056) counts nowhere (integrator ruling, SB0 gate pick 6); `PlayerLuckRow` refuses it | FR-STATS-37–41, -45 |
| `stats.query {scope: "trophies", characterId}` *(new scope)* | DM any; player own (another's → `forbidden`; unknown → `not_found`) | rate as FR-STATS-16; memo per player | `{trophies: {characterId, pending?: true, lifetime?: {avgD20, nat20, nat1, fights, dealt, taken, healing, kills, downed}, records?: {biggest-hit, biggest-overkill, best-fight-damage, best-fight-kills: {value, to?, fight} \| null}}}` (fight = the viewer's label) | FR-STATS-42, -43, -45 |
| Notification kind `stats-fight-summary` *(new; `notifications/kinds.ts`)* | audience `all` (a counted public fact) or `dm` (only DM-only facts); none when nothing counts | info, no action; payload `{encounterId}`; text "Fight summary"; toast class info (FR-NOTE-52: named in the kind's comment until WF4 adds the kind-def field) | FR-NOTE-68, FR-STATS-33 |

- **End hook** (FR-CBT-153): `rule-hooks.ts` line `stats.fight-summary` (appended last, `ended` only; `stats/summary.ts`): one indexed existence query on `hp_change_encounter` (`fightFactPresence`), then `notify` in `cbt.end`'s transaction after the CBTX hooks; a throw rolls End back. `lifecycle.ts` unchanged. Fixture loads run the same hook (the `stats` bundles' ended "Old Crypt" has its summary row).
- **Pending** (FR-STATS-46): a campaign answer while more than 5 closed sessions have no partial: `pending: true`, empty rows; never memoised; the client re-asks in 1 s.
- **Read helpers:** `dice-roll-log/stats-read.ts`: `rollFacts(db, viewer, campaignId, fromN?, toN?)` (range optional = the whole campaign in one pass; rows + `n`, `encounterId`, `manual`, `d20` faces kept and dropped, `hasD20`; one SQL pass over entries × `json_each(rollIds)` × roll PK), `nSpanOf` (binary search of a time span on `(campaign_id, n)`), `lastEntryAt`; `combat-tracker/stats-read.ts`: `combatFacts` ranges read their lines' encounters then `hp_change_encounter` (no campaign scan), `fightFactPresence`, `fightActorIds`, `standingOf`, `fightsOf` + `endedAt`.
- **Shared:** `@shared/stats/rules-b` (Highlights, luck and trophy tallies, mergeable), `@shared/stats/feed` (`feedText`: FR-STATS-27's wording for SB1).
- **Fixtures:** units `sb-fight-facts` (Bandits in the running Ambush; `stats-b` only), `sb-past-bandits` (gate ruling: the same facts as an **ended** "Bandit Camp" before the Ambush starts, via bundle `stats-b-past`, included first by session-sim-12, so the sim's fights are untouched), `sb-luck-rolls` (rolls only; joins session-sim-12), `sb-luck-reroll` (gems on; `stats-b` only); bundles `stats-b` (= `stats` + `gems-on` + `sb-fight-facts`, `sb-luck-rolls`, `sb-luck-reroll`), `stats-b-e2e` (`e2e/stats-core-b.e2e.ts`), `stats-b-past`.
- **CON-44** (heavy harness, measured): `,"fell":"kill"` 15 B a line; 3 kill / down lines in the 100-line window → +45 B per viewer (DM 690,797 B of 1.5 MiB; players ≤ 289,129 B of 512 KiB); worst, every line in the window flagged, +1.5 KB per viewer. A summary row sits in the closed-notification window (no growth). Acks: none in the snapshot.

## 141. Sprint 8: one toast policy, Stats and companion card useful sizes (WF4)

From [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-50–53, [stats](../specs/stats.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-STATS-66, -67 and [character-sheet-full](../specs/character-sheet-full.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CHARX-83 (#333, #335; seams S3–S5, S13). **Built by `fix/wf-sizes-toasts`**: browser only. No record type, intent, event, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Data side: [data model §111](../data-model/README.md#111-sprint-8-toast-policy-wf4).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Toast class (`@shared/notifications/toast-class.ts`) | `card` (sticky while pending), `alert` (6 s), `info` (6 s), `callout` (4 s; not a notification). `toastClassOf(declared, state)`: a pending row → card, else the kind's `alert`, else info | FR-NOTE-50, -52 |
| Kind defs | Server `NotificationKindDef.toastClass?` and client `NotificationView.toastClass?` (`card` \| `alert` \| `info`); every line of `kinds.ts` and both `notification-views.ts` names it (`RegisteredKindDef` / `RegisteredView`); an unknown class fails at load. `damage-card`, `cbtx-concentration`, `charx-draft`, `cbt-summon-concentration`, `roll-request` = card; `test`, `loot-received`, `charx-approved`, `mod-trigger`, `handout-shared`, `stats-awards` = info (SB's `stats-fight-summary` = info) | FR-NOTE-52 |
| One stack (`ToastRuntime.tsx`, `toasts.ts`) | `note-toasts` holds, in DOM order from home: `note-toast-pending-summary` (DM), cards, alerts, info (each oldest first), `note-toast-more` "+N more" (opens `note.history`), `note-callout`. At most 3 cards / alerts / info (the newest of each class; info held back first, then alerts); the summary and the callout don't count; the P3 E3 cap still lowers it while the stack doesn't fit. Players: bottom-right, the column runs upwards (`flex-col-reverse`); the DM: top-centre home band. `note-toast` gains `data-class` | FR-NOTE-50 |
| `showCallout({id, content, ms?})` (`toasts.ts`) | The one callout per page: a new one replaces it; `ms` default 4,000; the timer pauses while the stack is hovered; no row, bell or history; dropped while no runtime is mounted. Shown as `note-callout` (`data-callout-id`, close `note-callout-close`). The overkill toast (`stats-overkill-toast`, id `stats-overkill`) moved here from sonner; SB's kill feed uses it | FR-NOTE-50, -52 |
| Alerts (`alert-toasts.tsx`, `ShellToaster.tsx`) | While the runtime is mounted it claims the region (`claimToastStack`): sonner's Toaster isn't rendered and every `toast()` (alert Toasts, errors, confirmations) shows in the stack as the **alert** class, mirrored from sonner's store (`useSonner`): its own `duration`, else 6 s (infinite = until closed); the stack's timer ends it and dismisses it in sonner's store; `data-testid` = the toast's `testId` (alert Toasts keep `ui-alert-toast`), else `ui-toast`; `data-class="alert"`, `data-type`; action `ui-toast-action`, close `ui-toast-close`. Before the shell (nothing claims the region) sonner's Toaster shows them as before. Layer: the stack at z 900 (`TOAST_STACK_Z`), above dialogs (z-50) and the alert flash (z-100), below the overlay layer (1000: a `blocksInput` overlay such as the pause card) and the present popup | FR-NOTE-50 |
| `note.history` rows | Compact (time, badges and small buttons on one line); the test-mode send form after the lists. Useful-size criterion "pending + 3 rows" at 360 × 412 | FR-SHELL-100 |
| Companion card | Attack rows `py-1`, the last without its rule; no bottom padding: the Owlbear (2 attacks) fits 320 × 360 unscrolled | FR-CHARX-83 |
| "Pushed" from the plan (`keep-out.ts` `pushedByPlan`, `stack-inputs.ts`) | A window counts as pushed iff the placer's `placed` is `pushed`, or `displaced` with a `toolbar:` cause in the displacement memory; a pushed window's title bar + 40 px and its reported first row block the DM stack (FR-NOTE-44), whether or not a tool bar shows. *Sprint 9 ruling B ([§154](#154-sprint-9-toast-stack-kill-feed-stats-retry-load-clock-fx1)):* every other window's title bar + 40 px block it too while some spot clears them all | FR-NOTE-53 |
| Walkthrough fixes (WF4) | Alerts always show: they take the room first, the newest card / info still shows beside them (P3 E3), the rest in "+N more". The stack is portalled to `document.body` (z 900 holds against dialogs). An open modal dialog (`role="dialog"` / `alertdialog`, `data-state="open"`, not `aria-modal="false"`) is a hard keep-out for the DM stack, and its room is the larger band above or below it. A window under the home spot: a spot clear of every window body first (down, FR-NOTE-42), else beside a window over the home column (left, then right), else the runtime folds toasts into "+N more" while the spot covers a body (`dmStackSpot().covers`). History row badges `py-0 leading-[14px]` (DM rows as tall as the players') | FR-NOTE-42, -50 |
| Re-placement (`stack-inputs.ts`) | The DM stack's spot re-computes on every change of: open windows as rendered (grown against the **current** hard keep-outs), Initiative, hard keep-outs, first-row reports, stack height, viewport; the hover holds only the token re-read (P3 E1) | FR-NOTE-51 |
| `stats.board` | `usefulSize` 400 × 360 (criterion: 5 rows names whole, or 2 award cards, or the first 4 Highlights lines). Award grid: `@min-[356px]` 2 columns, `@min-[576px]` 3 (= window body 380 / 600 px), else 1; each card stacks title, value, winners, detail; names, values and details wrap (no truncation); Fight names wrap | FR-STATS-66, -67 |
| Fixtures / e2e | No unit; `wf-toasts.e2e.ts` on `toast-home-e2e`; `useful-size.e2e.ts` (`wf-frame-e2e`): `stats.board`, `charx.companion`, `note.history` cases on, plus `FR-CHARX-83 card useful criterion` (Owlbear, Drake at 320 × 360) and `FR-STATS-66 … FR-STATS-67 award grid fits` (both clients) | NOTE §13 |

## 142. Sprint 8: tool-bar pushes through the ladder, more push triggers, rail inset (WF2)

From [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-104–106, -109 ([ADR-0060](../architecture/adr/0060-one-window-placer.md)). **Built by `fix/wf-push-rail`.** No intent, event or record change (0 B).

| Seam | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `push` trigger (`placer.ts`) | Every shown window over the bar + 12 is displaced, protected ones first (registry order), then launcher windows by descending z; the ones still waiting are no obstacle. Each goes through the ladder (no L2b yield, no squeeze, puts no other window away) ranked nearest its pre-push rect (the memory's first one), cause `toolbar:<id>` (the hard region at that rect; `map.toolbar` if none, and the rect counts as hard). A launcher window with no spot ≥ useful is put away itself until `return`; a protected one never: L4, else L5 (flagged). `data-placed="pushed"`. FR-SHELL-55's push right and `belowBar` are gone | FR-SHELL-104 |
| `drag-end` | A move / resize end whose rendered rect overlaps a shown hard keep-out other than the toast box (a tool bar, a chrome keep-out) pushes that window; the memory keeps the dropped rect + `userSpot`, so `return` puts it back there. Over a PC: kept (user spot) | FR-SHELL-105, -106 |
| Re-select (`ToolRail.tsx`) | Pressing the active tool (the toggle group reports "") bumps `ToolPanel`'s `reselect`, which pushes with the current bar rect; a tool swap pushes as before (new `id`) | FR-SHELL-105 |
| Rail inset (`geometry.ts`, `placement.ts`) | `RAIL_CLEAR` = 68 (now in `geometry.ts`, re-exported by `placement.ts`). `clampRect`: stored x ≥ 0 renders at x ≥ 68 (flush right if wider than the room); x < 0 (parked) unchanged. `resizeRect`'s west edge stops at 68. Auto placement's `minX` = 68 always | FR-SHELL-109 |
| Top bar (`PlayFrame.tsx`) | `ui-topbar-left` (flex-1, `min-w-0`, children `truncate`) and `ui-topbar-right` (`shrink-0`): the left text ellipsises before the right cluster | FR-SHELL-109 |

- **Test hooks:** `data-settle-log` gains `push:<kind>>put-away` and `drag-end:<kind>>L…`; `data-auto-minimised-cause` = `toolbar:<id>` for a pushed window put away.
- **Harness:** push-path cells flipped (DM 14, players 6); push steps' I3 oracle keeps the launcher windows (a push puts none away, OQ-SHELL-61 A).

## 143. Sprint 8: map refits after the placer (WF3)

From [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-93–98 and [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-108's port (BL-65, sim-11 h / r03; #333, #335: OQ-SHELL-64 B, OQ-MAP-63 A). **Built by `fix/wf-refits`**: client only, no record type, intent, event, signal, frame or Konva layer (CON-44: 0 B). Amends §131's load / open fit, resize refit and fit-past-toasts rows and §139's `ManagerContext` row ("WF3 wires it": wired). Data side: [data model §113](../data-model/README.md#113-sprint-8-map-refits-after-the-placer-wf3).

| Seam | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| One obstacle source (`free-area.ts` `fitRooms(client, windowRects, regions, vp, shown) → {room, stripRoom}`) | `room` = the rendered windows of the placer's plan + this client's toast obstacles (`fitObstacles`), with its candidate rectangles; `stripRoom` = the DM's room without the shown stack (players: `room`). `useFreeArea()` adds `room`, `stripRoom`, `roomsWith(rects)`, `grown`, `settling`, `settledAt` | FR-MAP-93 |
| One PC test (`camera.ts` `fitTokensOf(tokens)`) | `pc` = `isPlacementPc` (#318): a player character's token; pets and summons are content tokens (kept under `all`, not required under `pcs`) | FR-MAP-93 |
| PC margin (`FIT_PC_MARGIN` = 4) | `allClear(…, margin)`, `panAtScale`, `fitVisible`, `pcsPan` keep each PC footprint + 4 px clear of windows: `fitVisible` searches its ladder with the margin only (an every-token-clear camera with a PC flush against a window is not `all`; walkthrough WF3 F9); `pcsPan` ranks it after the top-row rule; the area check uses the footprint itself | FR-MAP-93 |
| Bounds (`fitBounds(reason, lastFullCell, client) → {maxCell?, minCell?, leastHidden}`) | Full fits (`open`, `load`, `fit`, `fit-past-toasts`, `resize`; `isFullFit`) set the last full fit's cell and are unbounded; `reveal`, `appearance`, `window` take a cell ≤ that cell, zooming out to the floor when that clears tokens; a yield (`window`) also ≥ 0.75 × it (`REFIT_MIN_SHARE`, FR-SHELL-108). With no all-clear pan, the PCs-first ladder (`pcsFirst`): cells from min(48, step 1) down to the floor in 4-px steps, FR-MAP-81's pan at each; rank every PC clear, + 4 px, then (`leastHidden`: the players' fits) the least token area under windows or off the area (map px²), then the larger cell. The DM's fits skip the area rank (the largest PC-clear cell, BL-41). `pcsPan(…, scale)` takes its cell; it never exceeds step 1's | FR-MAP-94 |
| One fit path (`fitFor(reason, inputs, lastFullCell) → SceneFit & {reason}`) | `inputs` = `{client, tokens, grid, map, view, room, stripRoom}`; every reason runs `fitPastToasts`: first unbounded (the camera a load would take, so a reveal and a load with the same inputs agree: walkthrough F10), and only if that zooms in past the last full fit (or a yield falls below 75 % of it) again with `fitBounds`; `reason` = `fit-past-toasts` when that won, else the input | FR-MAP-95 |
| Settled refits (`createSettledRefit(refit, ready, frames)`, `placerSettled(state, reason, view)`) | The load / open fit, the yield refit and the resize refit run on the first frame the placer has settled: layout applied, no rendered window waiting for its `settle`, every open growing window measured (`grownAll`); a resize also needs `settledAt` = the viewport (the last `viewport` settle). At most `SETTLED_FIT_MAX_WAITS` 60 frames, then it fits anyway; a token / window / size change before it fires moves it a frame (≤ 10). The appearance refit waits the same way (`appear` settled), then FR-MAP-90's keep test (PCs + 4 px) | FR-MAP-96, -98 |
| Base port (`free-area.ts` `setFitPort(port \| null)`, `fitPort()`) | The mounted `MapCanvas` registers `{dryFit(windowRects) → DryFitResult \| null, lastFullCell(), yieldRefit()}`. `dryFitScene(inputs, lastFullCell)` → `{camera, cellPx, verdict, clear}`, not applied: the `window` fit with the candidate as one more obstacle; `clear` = every PC footprint + 4 px clear (the tokens the dry fit kept are clear by its verdict). `yieldRefit` = camera event `window` (refits only while `fitted`, stays `fitted`), after the settle. `ShellContext` passes the port to the controller as `dryFit` / `lastFullCell` / `onYield` (no canvas mounted → no yield) | FR-MAP-97, FR-SHELL-108 |
| `map-canvas` attrs | `data-fit-reason` + `window` | FR-MAP-97 |
| Layout tests | `windows.golden.json` (player) regenerated (auto spots move a few px with the PCs' 4-px margin); `fit-visible.test.ts` DM cells 35 / 37 / 42 / 42 px, + Scenes 29 / 29 / 38 / 42; a03 `pcs` at 44 px | FR-SHELL-62, FR-MAP-59 |
| Placer (walkthrough F9, WF2 notes; `placer.ts`) | L3's look-ahead counts the map's yield too: with every launcher window put away, a spot ≥ useful the map can yield to → put away the least recently used one, then the yield, before L4. The yield tries the target size first, then down to useful. A window put away for a transient cause that can't come back yet keeps its memory (a later settle retries). At `load` a protected window stored squeezed in its zone stays squeezed (its cause = the launcher window in its full zone, remembered), so a reload keeps the layout and the camera | FR-SHELL-97, -101, -102, -108 |
| Harness (`replay.ts` `runScript(registry, viewer, vp, state, steps)`) | Scripted repros on the replay world: `{open}`, `{close}`, `end`, `start`, `reload`, `fit`, with I1–I6 after each step | FR-SHELL-116 |

- **Harness:** `replay.ts` fits through `fitFor` with the reason per step (`load`, `resize`, `reveal`, `appearance`, `window`) and the canvas's `dryFitScene`; `replay.failing.json` down to DM 8, players 11 cells (main before this branch: 24 / 122).
- **Tests:** `refits.test.ts` (FR-MAP-93, -95, -96, -98), `src/client/player/features/scenes-map/wf-refits.test.ts` (FR-MAP-94, -97; the F10 repro), `src/client/player/features/play-shell/wf3-placer.test.ts` (F9, Party alone), `src/client/dm/features/play-shell/wf3-reload.test.ts` (the DM reload); E2E `e2e/wf-refits.e2e.ts` on `wf-frame-e2e`.
- **CON-44:** 0 B.

## 144. Sprint 8: saved macros and DM roll requests — intents, prompt views, Requests window (TT1+TT2)

From [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-64–92, [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICE-79–84, [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-58–62, -64 and [character-sheet-full](../specs/character-sheet-full.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CHARX-87–90 (#334, #335; [ADR-0061](../architecture/adr/0061-roll-requests.md)). **Built by `build/tt-dice`** on TT0's records and events ([§135](#135-sprint-8-table-tools-core--macros-roll-requests-audio-assets-pause-and-sound-fields-tt0)). Data side: [data model §114](../data-model/README.md#114-sprint-8-saved-macros-and-roll-requests-tt1tt2).

**Intents** (WS; zod in `@shared/dice-extras/macros`, `requests`; none takes `public` or an audience)

| Intent | Who | Errors (one code per case) | Result | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dicex.saveMacro {id?, name, expression, category, creatureId, dmOnly?}` | DM; player | name 1–40 trimmed, no control chars; expression FR-DICE-01 (stored canonical) → `invalid`. Player: `dmOnly` → `forbidden`; `creatureId` another's PC → `forbidden`, a monster / unknown → `not_found`; `id` not theirs (`playerId` ≠ them) → `not_found`, the DM's row (`createdBy` null) → `forbidden` "The DM's macros are read-only". DM: unknown creature → `not_found`. New / moved to another list: 25th in (`playerId`, `creatureId`) or 201st in the campaign → `conflict`. Same values → ack, no event | `{macroId}` | FR-DICEX-64–66 |
| `dicex.deleteMacro {id}` / `dicex.moveMacro {id, index 0–23}` | DM; player (own rows they made) | as `saveMacro`'s `id` rules; `index` past the end → last; same place → ack, no event | `{macroId}` | FR-DICEX-66 |
| `dicex.request {roll, characterIds 1–12 (distinct), dc? 1–40, dcShown, mode, results, label?}` | DM (player → `forbidden`) | a target not an owned, not-hidden (#141: its active combatant `hidden` in the started fight) PC of the campaign → `invalid`; 6th open request → `conflict`; a bad custom expression → `invalid` | `{requestId}` | FR-DICEX-74–77 |
| `dicex.answerRequest {requestId, characterId, mode, manual?, manualParts?}` | the target's player; DM | unknown request / target, or a player not the target's → `not_found`; request closed or target not `open` → `conflict`; manual count / parts as `char.roll` | `{rollId, entryId}` | FR-DICEX-78 |
| `dicex.declineRequest {requestId, characterId}` | the target's player; DM | as `answerRequest` | `{requestId}` | FR-DICEX-79 |
| `dicex.closeRequest {requestId}` / `dicex.rollForMissing {requestId}` | DM | unknown → `not_found`; closed → `conflict` | `{requestId}` / `{entryIds}` | FR-DICEX-80, -81 |

**Rules**

- **Macro lists:** a macro's list = (`playerId`, `creatureId`); `order` 0…n-1, re-numbered on move, delete and a move to another list. Player rows: `playerId` = `createdBy` = them. DM rows: on an owned PC `playerId` = its owner unless `dmOnly`; else null; a DM edit of a player's general row keeps its `playerId`. A macro roll is a plain `dice.roll` (`label` = name, its category, `actor {entityId}` = its creature); nothing marks it (FR-DICE-81).
- **Send:** one `roll-request` prompt per target (`notify`: audience the PC's owner, action "Roll" owned by them; payload `dc` only iff `dcShown`; text "DM: ‹label› · ‹PC›" (+ " · DC n"; the PC named so a player with two PCs can tell the prompts apart, integrator ruling after the part-2 walkthrough)), then the row (`seq`, CON-20). Event order: `NotificationCreated` × n, then `RollRequested` (the row lists the prompts' ids).
- **Answer** (built by the server; `sheetRoll`, exported from `character-sheet/commands.ts`, is `char.roll`'s body with `{audience?, label?, answer?}` and no event; `char.roll` unchanged): skill / save / check → `sheetRoll` (sheet specs, overrides, MOD hooks and parts); custom → `dice.evaluate` as the PC, category `other`, no subject (no sheet bonus, no roll hook). Label = the request's; every item `answer: true`. Audience: `public` → `{public: true}`; `hidden` → `{public: false, audiencePlayerId: the target's player}`. Target `rolled` (`total` = base + MOD parts, `natural`, `success` = total ≥ dc or null, `at`); the request closes when no target is `open`. Then `RollRequestAnswered {requestId, characterId, entryId}` (changes: entry + request), then `NotificationResolved` (`{outcome: rolled, total}`, closed by the roller).
- **Decline:** target `declined`; `RollRequestDeclined`, then `NotificationResolved {outcome: declined}`. **Close:** open targets `missed`; `RollRequestClosed {requestId}`, then each open prompt resolved **by rule** (`{outcome: missed}`, no closer). **Roll for missing:** each open target rolled as the DM like an answer (mode = the request's); hidden → `audiencePlayerId` = the PC's current owner, DM only (`public: false`) if none (then the resolution carries no total); `RollRequestClosed {requestId, rolled: entryIds}`, then the prompts resolved by rule (`{outcome: rolled, total}`).
- **Owner change** (entities' definition-changed hook line `dice-extras.owner-change`, `dice-extras/owner-hook.ts`, inside the update's transaction): the PC's macros on the old owner's list move to the new owner's (rows the old owner made become the new owner's; appended to the new list); its open targets → `missed`, their prompts resolved by rule (`{outcome: missed}`); a request with no open target closes. The changes ride on `EntityUpdated` (hooks never emit): the old owner gets the macro deletes and the resolved prompt, the new owner nothing of the request.
- **`gem.spend`** on an answer entry (`isAnswerEntry`) → `conflict` "A requested roll can't be rerolled" (gems' `reroll.ts`, one line; FR-DICEX-78, OQ-GEM-20 A).
- **Crash seams:** `PP_TEST_CRASH=dicex.request:before-commit` / `:after-commit`, `dicex.answerRequest:…` (each command one transaction).

**Client**

- **Macros** (`dice-extras/macro-logic.ts`, `MacroButton.tsx`; list and editor `MacrosPanel.tsx` in a lazy chunk): the tray header's Macros popover (`dicex-tray-macros`, by the gear): groups "‹actor›" (the tray's), other creatures by name, "General" (+ the DM's "General · ‹player›"); a gone creature's rows under "General", not rollable; disabled with the reason when not live or not the viewer's creature. "Save as macro" in the tray's Options row (free requests) opens the editor prefilled (expression, category, label, actor). The sheet's Macros tab: `sheet-sections.ts` line `dicex.macros` (tab "Macros", `order: 90`, characters + monsters; DM always, a player on own PCs; no lock gate).
- **Prompt** (`PromptViews.ts` in both `notification-views.ts`; bodies in `PromptBody.tsx`, a lazy chunk the dice runtime loads at idle; until then a row shows its text): player: Roll (a `RollButton` over `characterRolls`' expression, the MOD preview subject, mode preset from the payload; Alt → tray `adjust`, expression locked) → `dicex.answerRequest`; Decline; outcomes "Rolled 17" / "Declined" / "Closed". No `onAction` (no toast action button). DM: "‹PC› · ‹label›", `cueTokens: () => []`; player-owned actions never toast the DM.
- **Sound:** the dice runtime plays `useAlertSound()("request")` once per live `NotificationCreated` frame carrying a pending `roll-request` prompt owned by this player (never a snapshot).
- **Restore** (FR-DICEX-91, FR-NOTE-60; after WF4): optional view flag `restoreToast?: true` (`notifications/views.ts`), set only by the player's `roll-request` view. On a connect (the first snapshot of a load or reconnect), `syncToasts` (`toasts.ts`, its `restores` input; `ToastRuntime.tsx` passes the views' flag) toasts each pending row of such a kind the client owns as a card before the summary is counted, so they stay out of "N pending"; no cue, no sound (a connect starts neither); a row already toasted is not doubled. Seam for the pause catch-up (FR-DICEX-90): `pendingPrompts(store)` (`dice-extras/request-sound.ts`) = this player's pending prompts.
- **Requests window** `dicex.requests` (DM; `dm/features/dice-extras/requests-window.ts`, FR-SHELL-112 (1)): launcher "Requests" (order 46, after Dice), 360 × 460, useful 360 × 400 ("composer folded + a 6-row card"), min 300 × 320, `top-right`; badge = open requests (`dicex-launcher-badge`), the minimised "new" dot when a target leaves `open`; size-table row, DM golden list (goldens regenerated), harness set D3 (known-failure list regenerated: +2 / −4 D3 cells, the new ones the placer's drag-end under the tool bar and the Game log over a PC), useful-size e2e case. The body opens on the cards (composer folded) while a request is open. Body (`RequestsWindow.tsx`, lazy): composer (roll Select: skills / saves / checks / Custom…; PC chips + "All PCs" = owned, not hidden; DC + Show DC; mode; eye preset from the category's public setting; label; Send) and cards (open first): per target Waiting / total ✓ ✗ / Declined / No roll, nat 20 / 1, total → `focusLogEntry`; Roll for missing; Close (asks once). Roll bar (DM, Options row): "Request…" opens it (FR-DICE-84).
- **Test IDs** per [dice-extras §7](../specs/dice-extras.md#7-uiux-notes) sprint 8, plus `dicex-macro-group`, `dicex-macro-dm-only-badge`, `dicex-prompt-outcome`, `dicex-request-composer`, `dicex-request-fold`, `dicex-request-error`; `dicex-macro-roll` and `dicex-request-target` carry `data-reason` when disabled.
- **Bundle:** dice-extras initial 14.0 KB gz (cap 15 KB): the prompt views, the request sound, `request-names.ts` (zod-free constants, re-exported by `requests.ts`) and the Macros tab stub; the macro list / editor (2.5 KB gz) and the prompt bodies are lazy.
- **Fixtures:** units `dicex-macros` (joins `session-sim-12`) and `dicex-request-open` (not in it); bundles `table-tools` (= `session-sim-11` + `dicex-macros`, `audio-chime`, `camp-alert-sounds`, `dicex-request-open`) and `table-tools-e2e` (`e2e/tt-macros.e2e.ts`, `e2e/tt-requests.e2e.ts`).
- **CON-44:** no new record type; TT0's `con44-tt.test.ts` holds the caps (§135).

## 146. Sprint 8: stats tier-B UI — kill feed, Highlights, End summary open, Luck tab, Trophies tab (SB1 + SB2)

From [stats](../specs/stats.md#61-tier-b-sprint-8) FR-STATS-26–30, -34, -36, -41, -44, -48, -50, -52, -53, [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-68 (views), -69 and [character-sheet-full](../specs/character-sheet-full.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CHARX-97–99 (#334, #335; seams S4, S5, S11). **Built by `build/sb-stats-ui`**: browser only. No intent, event, record type, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Reads §140's acks and lines. Data side: [data model §116](../data-model/README.md#116-sprint-8-stats-tier-b-ui-sb1--sb2).

| Piece | Contract | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Kill feed (`stats/feed.ts`, `runtime.tsx`) | **Replaces the overkill toast** (§133's `stats-overkill-toast` row; FR-NOTE-69). From live event frames only: each `cbt.attack` / `cbt.hp` damage line upsert with `fell` or `callout`, not undone, not superseded, each line once per page. Words = `feedText` (FR-STATS-27) from the line's names: "Thorn finished Goblin 1", "Goblin 1 falls" (no source), "Goblin 2 dropped Brakka", "Brakka is down", a callout prefixed "Overkill! "; a callout on a target already down keeps "Overkill! Thorn → Goblin 1: 30 damage". The DM adds ", overkill N" (`dmPayload.overkill`) and " (hidden)" (non-public line). A two-target apply arrives as one frame per target: lines within **150 ms** of the first share **one** toast (≤ 3 lines `stats-feed-line`, then "and N more"). `showCallout({id: "stats-feed", ms: 4000})`, content `stats-feed-toast`; one feed per page (BL-71). Pause (S10): not yet (TT4) | FR-STATS-26–29, FR-NOTE-69 |
| End summary open (`runtime.tsx`, `open.ts`) | A live `NotificationCreated` with a `stats-fight-summary` row → `openStatsFight(encounterId)`: view `{tab: "fight", fightKey, highlightsOpen: true}`, then `open("stats.board", "main", {by: "feature"})` (the placer's ladder places it; it may put a launcher window away). Once per notification id per page. The DM tab that sent `cbt.end` (frame `cause.intentId`) with Initiative open waits for FR-CBT-60's minimise (≤ 2 s). Off with "Open the fight summary at End". `stats-awards` opens likewise once per id (FR-STATS-53) | FR-STATS-34, -53 |
| `stats-fight-summary` view (both `notification-views.ts`, info) | Icon `Swords`; "Fight summary" (+ ": ‹encounter name›" `stats-summary-name` from the DM's store) and **Open** `stats-summary-open` (= the open above, preference not consulted) | FR-NOTE-68 |
| Preferences section `stats.prefs` "Stats", order 40 (both `pref-sections.ts`) | Switches `stats-pref-feed` "Kill feed" and `stats-pref-summary` "Open the fight summary at End"; per browser `pp.stats.v1` `{feed, openSummary}` (zod, default both on; invalid → defaults; storage blocked → page memory); never sent | FR-STATS-30, -34 |
| Highlights (`Highlights.tsx`) | Fight tab, above the table: Collapsible `stats-highlights` (`data-state` open / closed; toggle `stats-highlights-toggle`), open on an ended fight, closed on a started one (the summary opens it; a picker change resets). Items `stats-highlight` + `data-record-id` = MVP `mvp`, `biggest-hit`, `biggest-overkill`, `most-healing`, `kills`, `downed`, `nat20`, `nat1`, `rounds`, `duration`, DM `standing`; empty items (and an all-empty block) not drawn. Winners `stats-highlight-winner` in player colours ("Mira + Owlbear"), details `stats-highlight-detail` ("Thorn → Goblin 1, 30"), kill / down lines `stats-highlight-event` (feed words, ≤ 20 then "+N"), sides `stats-highlight-side`, hidden mark `stats-highlight-hidden` (DM). Two columns from a 400-px window | FR-STATS-31, -36, -48 |
| Luck tab (`Luck.tsx`) | Tabs Fight · Session · **Luck**. `stats-luck` (`data-state` loading / ok / error / pending / offline), scope Select `stats-luck-scope` (options `stats-luck-scope-option`: sessions by key, `campaign`), `stats-luck-table` of `stats-luck-row` (`data-record-id` = character id / `dm-dice`): Name, Rolls, d20s, Avg (1 decimal, "—"), Nat 20, Nat 1, in ack order. Query `{scope: "luck"}` (newest), `{n}` or `{campaign: true}`; re-query on a live entry with a roll (trailing 1 s). `pending` → skeleton, re-query in 1 s. "No rolls yet" | FR-STATS-39–41, -46, -50 |
| Trophies tab (`stats/trophies/`, `sheet-sections.ts`) | Section `stats.trophies`, slot `tab` "Trophies", `order: 100`, characters, both clients, always: full sheets only (DM, owner). Query `{scope: "trophies", characterId}` while the tab shows; re-query on a live line whose actor or target is the character or one of its combatants / companions; `pending` → re-query in 1 s. `stats-trophies` (+ character id, `data-state`), `stats-trophy` (+ key, `data-value` = the shown value; Lifetime `avgD20` … `downed`, Records `biggest-hit` … `best-fight-kills`), `stats-trophy-detail` ("→ Ogre · Fight 3 · Tue 20 Oct"), DM `stats-trophies-dm-note`. "—" for 0; "No fights yet" | FR-CHARX-97–99, FR-STATS-42–44 |
| Fight refresh (BL-69) | The default Fight tab also re-queries on an `encounter` change (players get the started encounter before any public fact; rounds tick); a picked fight on its own encounter's change. Ack: a player's `fights` list is headed by the started fight as `{key, label: "This fight", status: "started"}` until it has a public fact (then its "Fight N · …" label), so the picker can show and re-pick it | FR-STATS-52 |
| Shared schema | `fight.highlights` **required** in `PlayerFightResult` / `DmFightResult` (acks aren't stored; the server always sends it) | FR-STATS-31 |
| Fixtures / e2e | No unit (SB0's). Bundle alias `stats-b-ui-e2e` (= `stats-b`) for `e2e/stats-ui-b.e2e.ts`: one toast per two-target apply on all three clients, Luck · Campaign (DM dice only for the DM), player-1's Trophies tab, End at 1280×720 opens Stats with Highlights (DM after Initiative minimises; player-1 with the companion card open). `stats-window.e2e.ts` and `stats-core-b.e2e.ts` read `stats-feed-toast` | STATS §13.1 |

## 147. Sprint 8: current-turn token highlight — turn ring (CB2)

From [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-108–112 and [combat-tracker](../specs/combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CBT-166 (#334, #335: OQ-MAP-67 A, OQ-MAP-68 A; seams S1). **Built by `build/cb-turn-ring`**: client only, derived from `turnCombatantId` / `playerTurnCombatantId` and `combatant.tokenId`, already in every snapshot. No record type, field, intent, event, signal, frame or Konva layer (CON-44: 0 B). Amends §26's token overlays row. Data side: [data model §117](../data-model/README.md#117-sprint-8-current-turn-token-highlight-cb2).

| Seam | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| Token overlay result (`token-overlays.ts`, type only; registry order unchanged) | + `turn {id, hidden?}`; merge (`decorations.ts`): first non-null wins | FR-MAP-108 |
| Mark (`layers/TokensLayer.tsx` `TurnRingMark`, geometry `token-decor.ts` `turnRingGeometry`) | One Konva `Shape` per ringed token, inside its `map-token` group right after the hit area, under the body (moves with a drag and drag previews; dims with the group). `name` = `id`, `id` = token id, attr `hidden` `"true"` \| `"false"`, `listening` false. A ring hugging the shape (circle / square, FR-MAP-26) just outside the selection / target outline: 3 px `accent.success` (`--success`) over FR-MAP-72's casing + a 10 px soft glow; `hidden` → dashed, no glow; cells < 48 px → 2 px, no glow. Static (no animation frame); memoised on its inputs | FR-MAP-109, -111 |
| Combat's `cbt` line (`combat-tracker/token-overlay.ts` `actsThisTurn`) | `turn` on a token bound to the viewer's turn combatant (DM `turnCombatantId`, players `playerTurnCombatantId`) and its `in-owner-turn` companions without a rank of their own (the owner's turn); constant marks `TURN_MARK` / `TURN_MARK_HIDDEN` (`hidden` = the combatant's `hidden` or `ownerHidden`, DM records only; a fog-hidden combatant's `hidden` copy is set, FR-FOG-14). None in round 0, without a started encounter, after End, on a removed or token-less turn | FR-CBT-166, FR-MAP-110 |
| Players | Never a hidden, fog-hidden or `ownerHidden` combatant's ring: its token and its turn id never reach them (FR-CBT-31, ADR-0039) | FR-MAP-110 |
| Canvas query (`e2e/support/canvas.ts` `turnRings(page)`) | `cbt-token-turn` nodes: `id`, `hidden`, `dashed`, `glow`, `listening`, parent group + index, centre and footprint centre | FR-MAP-112 |

- **Tests:** `scenes-map/turn-ring.test.tsx` (`FR-MAP-108 turn mark`: merge, drawn before the body in the token group via a server render, drag preview, `hidden` → dashed, `listening` false, geometry; `FR-MAP-109 small cells`), `combat-tracker/turn-ring.test.ts` (`FR-CBT-166 turn ring per viewer` × DM / P1 / P2: visible, hidden, fog-hidden, `ownerHidden`, in-owner-turn companions, removed, token-less, round 0, after End), server `combat-tracker/turn-ring-sync.test.ts` over real sockets on `cb-turn-ring-e2e` (a whole round of Next incl. the healed fog-hidden Goblin; reconnect; End; leak scans per player); E2E `e2e/cb-turn-ring.e2e.ts` on `cb-turn-ring-e2e`.
- **CON-44:** 0 B.

## 148. Sprint 8: pause screen — `camp.pause` / `camp.resume`, the card, the DM control (TT4)

From [campaign-management](../specs/campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-CAMP-46, -47, [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-125–129, -134–136, [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-90 and [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-63 (#334, #335). **Built by `build/tt-pause`** on TT0's field and events ([§135](#135-sprint-8-table-tools-core--macros-roll-requests-audio-assets-pause-and-sound-fields-tt0)) and WF1's registries ([§139](#139-sprint-8-one-window-placer-wf1)). Data side: [data model §118](../data-model/README.md#118-sprint-8-pause-screen-tt4).

| Intent (DM, WS) | Contract | Event | FR |
|---|---|---|---|
| `camp.pause {message?, imageAssetId?, backInMin?}` | `message`: control chars → `invalid`, trimmed, ≤ 200, `""` → none; `imageAssetId` via `assets.require(…, {kind: "image"})` (a sound → `invalid`, unknown → `not_found`); `backInMin` ∈ {5, 10, 15, 30} → `backAt` = `ctx.clock` + N min, else `invalid`; extra keys → `invalid`. A field **left out keeps** its value while paused (none on a new pause), `null` clears it. Paused already → update, `startedAt` kept. Same values → ack, no event. Player → `forbidden` | `CampaignPaused {campaignId}` + `campaign` upsert (with `pause`) to every connection; logged, not undoable, no resync | FR-CAMP-47 |
| `camp.resume {}` | running → ack, no event | `CampaignResumed {campaignId}` + `campaign` upsert (no `pause` key) to every connection; logged, not undoable | FR-CAMP-47 |

- **Visibility:** the `campaign` row as before (full to every connection of the campaign); `pause` (and so the image ID) is on the wire only while paused; a snapshot after resume carries neither. Player intents are accepted as before while paused (input block is client only, OQ-SHELL-70 A). Two DM tabs: last wins. Crash `PP_TEST_CRASH=camp.pause:before-commit` → running; `:after-commit` → paused after restart.
- **Player client** (`src/client/common/features/pause/`): overlay entry `pause` (`overlays.ts`, players, `layer` 10, `blocksInput` = paused): the opaque full-viewport card ("Paused", message, image `display` ≤ 60 vh, "Back in m:ss" / "Back soon" from `backAt` on the client clock, mute toggle, "Reconnecting…" while not live), shown from the snapshot (reload, reconnect, restart, campaign switch). Runtime: paused → the alert controller's `setSuppressed(true)` (no `alert()`, no sound but `pause`) and `pauseGate` closed; no throw, popup or hold for new `roll` entries (`ThrowLayer.tsx`); window opens queue in the placer (WF1). A live `CampaignPaused` that starts a pause plays `pause`; a live `CampaignResumed` catches up: the turn alert (FR-CBT-41's predicate on the started encounter) if it is their PC's turn, else `request` when a roll-request prompt for them arrived during the pause and still waits (TT2's `pendingPrompts`, `createdAt` ≥ the pause's `startedAt`), else `resume` (one sound). Prompts sent while paused toast as cards under the card, silent (FR-DICEX-90). A snapshot plays nothing.
- **Feature API:** `usePaused()`, `isPausedFor(store)`, `pauseGate.deferUntilResume(key, fn)` (for SB's `stats.showAwards`, WF3's reveal refit); `AlertController.setSuppressed(on)`.
- **DM client:** top-bar item `pause` (`top-bar-items.ts`, lazy chunk; slot 128 px from 1280 wide, 40 below via the item's per-viewport `width`): button 96 px running / 128 px paused / 40 px icon-only (label in a Tooltip), disabled unless live; Popover: message, image (asset picker), back in None / 5 / 10 / 15 / 30; running → Pause; paused → Update (back in left out unless changed), Resume, Preview (the form as the card in a non-modal Dialog, no overlay; Escape / ✕). Draft `pp.pause.v1.<campaignId>` in `localStorage`.
- **Test IDs:** `ui-pause` (`data-state` paused / reconnecting), `ui-pause-card`, `ui-pause-{message,image,countdown,mute,reconnecting}`; DM `ui-pause-open` (`data-state` running / paused), `ui-pause-form`, `ui-pause-message-input`, `ui-pause-image-pick`, `ui-pause-image-clear`, `ui-pause-back-in` (+ `ui-pause-back-in-item` `data-value`), `ui-pause-{submit,update,resume,preview,error}`, `ui-pause-preview-dialog`, `ui-pause-preview-close`.
- **Toast stack** (WF4's, `notifications/toasts.ts`): while paused a new info-class row isn't toasted (history only; cards still toast and wait under the card) and `showCallout` drops (not replayed).
- **Stats** (`stats/runtime.tsx`, `onStatsFrame`): while paused no kill-feed line is pushed (dropped, not replayed); the `stats-awards` open (FR-SHELL-94) and the End summary open (FR-STATS-34) go through `pauseGate.deferUntilResume` (keys `stats.awards`, `stats.summary`): once each at resume.
- **Reveal refit** (`MapCanvas.tsx`, players): `pauseGate.deferUntilResume("map.reveal", …)`: under the card it runs once at resume.
- **Fixtures:** unit `camp-paused` ("Pizza break, back soon", `town`, 10 min; not in `session-sim-12`); bundles `table-tools-paused` (= `table-tools` + `camp-paused`), `table-tools-paused-e2e` (`e2e/tt-pause.e2e.ts`).
- **CON-44:** no new record; the paused card's +488 B and the events' sizes are TT0's (`con44-tt.test.ts`).

## 149. Sprint 9: reminders + strongholds core — records, intents, Stores holder (RS0)

From [reminders](../specs/reminders.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-REM-01–16, -27–31, [strongholds](../specs/strongholds.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-HOLD-01–21, -35–40 and [party-loot](../specs/party-loot.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-LOOT-30–39 (#338, #339; review-a rulings 1–3, 6–10, 21–23; [ADR-0065](../architecture/adr/0065-reminder-stronghold-records.md), [ADR-0066](../architecture/adr/0066-stores-holder.md)). **Built by `fix/rs-core`** (server only; clients RS1–RS3). Data side: [data model §119](../data-model/README.md#119-sprint-9-reminders--strongholds-core-rs0). No new registry (§26 unchanged).

**Record types** (scope `campaign`)

| Type | Visibility | Snapshot | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `reminder {id, campaignId, seq, characterId \| null, text, source, rolls [{id, label, expression, category}], strongholdId \| null, facilityId \| null, dmOnly, ownerPlayerId \| null, linkHidden, createdByPlayerId \| null, createdAt, updatedAt}` *(new)* | DM full. Player hidden if `dmOnly`, `linkHidden` or another player's character (`ownerPlayerId` ≠ them); else redacted: no `seq`, `updatedAt`, `dmOnly`, `linkHidden`, `ownerPlayerId` (order by `createdAt`, then id) | **lazy `all`** | FR-REM-01, -13 |
| `stronghold {id, campaignId, seq, name, dmOnly, defenders, defendersDmOnly, notes, notesDmOnly, facilities [...], staff [...], createdAt, updatedAt}` *(new)* | DM full. Player hidden if `dmOnly`; else redacted inside: DM-only facilities / staff removed, `defenders` / `notes` omitted when DM-only, pointers at a removed facility → null, every flag, `seq` and `updatedAt` removed | **lazy `all`** | FR-HOLD-01, -08 |
| `stronghold-index {id, campaignId, name, dmOnly?: true}` *(new, derived)* | DM full; player hidden while `dmOnly`, else `{id, campaignId, name}` | every row (≤ 8) | FR-HOLD-01 |
| `stores-coin {id = strongholdId, campaignId, strongholdId, cp…pp, hidden?: true}` *(new, derived)* | DM full; player hidden while `hidden` | one row per existing stronghold with ≥ 1 entry (≤ 8) | FR-LOOT-31 |
| `loot-entry` | ends gain `{kind: stores, strongholdId}`; DM-only keys `storesHidden?: true`, `itemHidden?: true`. **Player: every row redacted** (no `seq`, no DM keys); a hidden Stores end → wire-only `{kind: outside}`; hidden if `itemHidden` or no end is party-side (only `world` / `outside` left) | window unchanged | FR-LOOT-30, -32, -36 |
| `party-item` | + `strongholdId?` (a Stores row), DM-only keys `storesHidden?: true`, `dmOnly?: true`; player hidden if either | lazy `all` unchanged (one load = party + every Stores) | FR-LOOT-36 |

**Intents** (none optimistic)

| Intent | Who | Rules | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| `rem.save {id?, target, text, source?, rolls?, dmOnly?, strongholdId?, facilityId?}` | DM, player | strict zod (text 1–500, source 0–60, ≤ 4 rolls); caps 200 / 30 (`conflict`; a player's message has no number); player: party / own character, edit only own-created rows still on party / own PC, never the DM keys (`forbidden`), a save keeps the stored ones; links DM only (`not_found` / `invalid`); same values → `ack` | `{reminderId}` |
| `rem.delete {id}` | DM, player | as `rem.save`; a row the player can't see → `not_found` | `{reminderId}` |
| `hold.create {name}` | DM | name 1–60 unique ci (`conflict`); ≤ 8 | `{strongholdId}` |
| `hold.update {strongholdId, name?, dmOnly?, defenders?, defendersDmOnly?, notes?, notesDmOnly?}` | DM | patch ≥ 1 field; same → `ack`; a `dmOnly` flip rewrites `linkHidden`, Stores items' and every Stores entry's `stores_hidden` (changes: entries **inside the loot window only**) | — |
| `hold.saveFacility {strongholdId, facility}` / `hold.saveStaff {strongholdId, staff}` | DM | append (no id; ≤ 24 / ≤ 40) or replace (`not_found`); `replacesId` / `facilityId` of the same stronghold (self → `invalid`) | `{facilityId}` / `{staffId}` |
| `hold.moveRow {strongholdId, list, id, index}` | DM | index clamped; same place → `ack` | — |
| `hold.removeStaff`, `hold.removeFacility` | DM | removing a facility deletes its linked reminders and nulls pointers, one transaction | — |
| `hold.delete {strongholdId}` | DM | deletes linked reminders; Stores items → party (merge; full → `conflict` "Party inventory is full (60) — move or remove items first", nothing written); coin balance → 0 by move entries ≤ 999 999 per coin (negative coins paid by the pot), text "Stronghold deleted" or empty for a hidden Stores; then the row | — |
| `loot.move` | DM, player | ends may be `stores`; `world` pairs with `character` or `stores`; player with a visible Stores: Stores ↔ pot, Stores → own PC, own PC → Stores; hidden / gone Stores → `not_found` "Stores are gone" | `{entryId}` |
| `party.addItem {…, holder?: {kind: stores, strongholdId}, dmOnly?}` / `party.editItem {…, dmOnly?}` / `party.removeItem` / `party.moveItem` (ends + `stores`) | as before; Stores rights as `loot.move` | per-holder caps (`Stores are full (60)`) and keys; no `loot-received` for a Stores add; a player's take of a DM-only Stores item → `not_found` | as before |

Internal (fixture loader only, no WS intent): `hold.saveFacilityByName`, `hold.saveStaffByName`, `rem.saveByName` take a facility **by name** and run the commands above unchanged.

**Domain events** (`src/shared/events/reminders.ts`, `strongholds.ts`; all logged, none undoable; all **`quietWhenUnchanged`**: a change no player can see sends players no frame)

| Event | Changes | Player sees |
|---|---|---|
| `ReminderSaved {reminderId, created}` / `ReminderDeleted {reminderIds}` | `reminder` | per row (FR-REM-13) |
| `StrongholdCreated {strongholdId}` | `stronghold`, `stronghold-index` | nothing (new = DM-only) |
| `StrongholdUpdated {strongholdId, part: header \| facility \| staff \| order, facilityId?, staffId?}` | `stronghold`; rename / flip + `stronghold-index`; flip + reminders, Stores items, `stores-coin`, window entries; facility `dmOnly` + reminders | the redacted change, or no frame |
| `FacilityRemoved {strongholdId, facilityId, reminderIds}` | `stronghold`, reminder deletes | per row |
| `StrongholdDeleted {strongholdId, reminderIds, entryIds}` | `stronghold` / `stronghold-index` / `stores-coin` → null, reminder deletes, `party-item`, entries, `party-pot` | per row |
| `CoinMoved`, `PartyItemAdded`, `PartyItemRemoved`, `ItemMoved` | + `stores-coin` per Stores end | per row |
| entities' `EntityUpdated` (owner change) | + reminder `ownerPlayerId` upserts | old owner deletes, new owner upserts |

- **Zod:** `@shared/reminders` (`schemas.ts`, `text.ts`), `@shared/strongholds`, `@shared/party-loot` (`Endpoint` + `stores`, `WireEndpoint` + `outside`, `StoresCoinRecord`, `PlayerLootEntryRecord`, `playerMayMove`, `visibility.ts`), `@shared/party-board` (`StoresHolder`, `ItemEnd` + `stores`, `PartyItemRecord` keys, `planHolderMerge`).
- **Hook line:** `reminders.owner-copy` in `definitionChangedHooks` (FR-REM-05).
- **Fixtures:** bundles `reminders-strongholds`, `rs-e2e`, `session-sim-13` (RS group; other sprint-9 tracks append).
- **CON-44** (`src/server/features/strongholds/con44.test.ts`, heavy harness at the caps: 200 reminders, 8 × 24 × 40 strongholds, 8 Stores with coin, the whole loot window with Stores ends): DM 691,615 → 694,491 B (+2,876 B; 878,373 B left of 1.5 MiB); `stronghold-index` 1,364 B, `stores-coin` 1,831 B per frame; players +786 B worst (289,358 B of 512 KiB); `reminder` / `stronghold` 0 B in every snapshot; DM `sync.load` 364,036 B (reminders) / 377,798 B (strongholds). `StrongholdUpdated` at the caps: DM 46,645 B, player 45,579 B (≤ 48 KB); a 500-entry show flip sends 100 entries (37,687 B DM in the test's rows).

## 150. Sprint 9: dice + gems lines (FX2)

From [dice-roll-log](../specs/dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICE-91–93, [gems](../specs/gems.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-GEM-41–44, [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-74 and [dice-extras](../specs/dice-extras.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-DICEX-94–95 (#338, #339; review-b rulings 10–12). **Built by `fix/fx-dice-lines`**. Data side: [data model §120](../data-model/README.md#120-sprint-9-dice--gems-lines-fx2).

| Intent / helper | Change | FR |
|---|---|---|
| `gem.adjust {characterId, gem, delta}` | `delta` −10…+10, ≠ 0 (was ±5; gems' own zod, `GEM_DELTA_MAX`); 11 → `invalid`. A net award writes one line "DM awards Brakka 6 Fortune gems (6 held)" | FR-GEM-43 |
| `dice.reroll(env, {entryId, rollId, manual?, others?})` | `others(item, {items, rollIds, at}) → {items, rollIds}` (the old entry's items, roll ids and rerolled slot), called once the new roll row is written, before the entry: `items` aligned with the old payload (an item replaces, `null` drops it and its `dmPayload.items` names; the rerolled slot is the new item), `rollIds` the new entry's in full. Absent → copied as before | FR-DICE-91 |
| `updateNotification(env, id, patch, {emit: false}) → RecordChange` | no `NotificationUpdated`; the caller puts the change in its own event's `changes`. Default unchanged | FR-NOTE-74 |
| `cards.replaceToHit → {critFlipped, change, payload}`, `cards.applyMaxDice → {rollIds, change}` | write the card with `{emit: false}`; `cards.cardLineItems(env, p, item, old)` = the attack line's items as the card shows them, roll ids swapped (to-hit, maxed part) or left out (a dropped item's, with its part and offered rolls) (FR-DICE-91's `others`) | FR-GEM-41, -42 |

**Events and visibility** (amends [§22](#22-combat-domain-events)'s event order and §130 / §134's `GemSpent` rows)

| Event | Changes (order) | Player sees |
|---|---|---|
| `GemSpent` (Combat gem) | `character-gems`, the `gem.spend` line, **the card's upsert**; no `NotificationUpdated` after | the count and the public spend line; the card only its audience (owner + DM, or the DM alone) |
| `GemSpent` (Inspiration reroll) | `character-gems`, the spend line, the new `roll` entry (its items per the card, FR-GEM-41), the old entry's supersede upsert, **a pending card's upsert**; no `NotificationUpdated` after | as before; the card only its audience |

- **One frame per spend** (FR-SYNC-17, gate I15): each connection gets one `event` frame (cause `GemSpent`) per spend. Known I15 exceptions, recorded not widened (review-b ruling 11): `damage.ts` (×2) and `hit.ts` still emit their own `NotificationUpdated` after the owner's event; `cards.ts`' two helpers are called only by `gem.spend` and now fold (the ruling's `cards.ts:112` is `replaceToHit`).
- **Reroll line follows the card** (BL-62): the new line holds the new to-hit + the card's current damage (maxed dice as the card shows them), or the to-hit only after a crit flip; never the old pre-max part. `rollIds` name only final rolls (FR-DICE-67).
- **Client:**
  - Log lines (FR-DICE-92, -93): " + " joins kept dice across dropped ones ("4d6kh3: 5 3̶ + 6 + 6 = 17"). A die chip's number is HTML text over the shape (an `inline-block` `<span data-face>` pulled back over the `<svg>` by a negative margin, centred by padding so the chip stays 18 px with no scroll overflow; the `<svg>` stays `aria-hidden`, no `<text>`); chips, their `dice-log-die` wrappers (`leading-none`) and the line's badges are `inline-block`, so `innerText` and a selection copy read on one line. A whole d100 draws its two faces from `data-face` (CSS) and carries its value as one text node. New test ID `dice-log-entry-text` (the line's text).
  - Gems (FR-GEM-43, -44): the DM's ± clicks per character + gem sum client-side and send one `gem.adjust` 1.5 s after the last click (±10 at once; net 0 → nothing; popover close → now; not live → dropped). The count shows the pending sum at once, muted (`gem-count` / `gem-adjust-count` `data-pending`). The log's reroll confirm reads "Spend Inspiration?".
  - Roll popups (FR-DICEX-94): the 6 s after rest always run; at expiry a popup closes unless held (a `pointermove` with movement on it since it showed and `:hover` now, or focus inside), re-checked 0.3 s after a leave / blur (a card that slides from under a following pointer stays held) and every 1 s; the hold flags clear when the card mounts or unmounts; ✕ always closes.
  - Macro editor (FR-DICEX-95): a field's error shows only after it was touched (changed or blurred) or Save was pressed; Save stays disabled while invalid; a new macro (Save as macro's preset) opens with focus in Name.
- **Fixtures:** bundle `fx-dice-e2e` (= `gems-reroll-e2e`) for `e2e/fx-dice.e2e.ts`; no new unit.
- **CON-44:** 0 B (no new record or field); one frame fewer per Combat / reroll spend with a card.

## 151. Sprint 9: window memory, frame and canvas follow-ups (PM1)

From [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-144–155, -158, -159, -162–167 and [scenes-map](../specs/scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-MAP-113–115 ([ADR-0064](../architecture/adr/0064-window-memory.md), amends [ADR-0060](../architecture/adr/0060-one-window-placer.md); #338, #339). **Built by `fix/window-memory`.** Client only: no intent, event, record, signal or stored field (CON-44: 0 B). Amends §139's zone row ("user spots included" → untouched only), §142's `drag-end` row (now render-only, below) and §143's settled-refit row (FR-MAP-113). This section holds the reopen algorithm and the harness invariants the spec points to (review-b ruling 22).

**Reopen algorithm** (FR-SHELL-146–150; pure, in `settle` and the render). Inputs: the stored `WindowState` `w`, the viewport `vp`, the shown hard keep-outs `K` (tool bar, chrome keep-outs; never the toast box), the windows `ws`.

1. `isRemembered(w)` = `w.userSpot || w.userSize` (a move end sets `userSpot`, a resize end `userSize`; a click, focus, minimise or close sets nothing; only Reset clears them). Not remembered → the ladder as built.
2. The stored rect is unchanged; nothing else in `ws` changes; no put-away, no memory entry, no yield, no refit (`data-settle-log` `<trigger>:<kind>>remembered`, `data-placed="stored"`).
3. Drawn `r = renderedRect(w, def.minSize, vp)` (FR-SHELL-29 fit, FR-SHELL-12 clamp, rail inset). Never written.
4. Render-only push: while `r` overlaps a `k ∈ K` + 12 px, `r` moves by the least move clear of it, below else right (ties below), clamped (`pushedRect`); `data-placed="pushed"`. Recomputed every render from `K`; never written (a reload or a closed tool loses nothing).
5. z: every open (launcher, toggle, restore, `appear`, a feature open) and every pointer-down fronts it (`maxZ + 1`, `normaliseZ`); a reload keeps the stored order; placer writes never change z.

| Seam | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| Geometry (`geometry.ts`) | `isRemembered(w)`; `pushedRect(r, bars, vp)` (step 4); `placedRect(w, min, vp, bars)` = step 3, then step 4 for a remembered window; `windowRect(…, caps, bars)` / `windowRects(items, vp, hard, bars)` take the bars, so `ShellWindow`, the placer's obstacles and `free-area`'s `renderedWindowRects(…, hard, bars)` (fits, free area, toast keep-out) all see the drawn rect | FR-SHELL-147, -150, FR-MAP-115 |
| Placer (`placer.ts`) | `pushBars(keepOuts)` = hard keep-outs minus `toasts`. A remembered window is never written (`setWin` refuses it): `open` / `reopen` / `appear` / `settle` run no rung for it; `load` / `viewport` keep it; `push` and `drag-end` skip it (a drop under a bar stores the drop rect, drawn pushed, FR-SHELL-167); L3 and the look-ahead put away untouched launcher windows only; the L2 squeeze takes untouched protected windows only; a zone (an untouched protected window) ignores remembered windows and displaces only untouched launcher windows; a remembered protected window displaces nothing. `renderedPlaced(w, def, vp, bars, placed)` = `data-placed` as drawn | FR-SHELL-145, -149–152 |
| BL-87 (`placer.ts`) | A launcher window put away for an open (cause `open:<kind>`, never returns, FR-SHELL-152) hands the squeezes it caused to the opened window (`window:<opened>`), so they end when that one does (root cause: the squeeze waited for a cause that never came back). A squeezed untouched protected window whose cause ended goes back to its zone iff the zone is clear of untouched windows and hard keep-outs (remembered windows ignored), else waits for the untouched window in it (the memory's cause becomes that window). `growBack` (`return`, viewport up) also grows a shell-shrunk untouched protected window back to its zone. The zone tests (taking it, going back) count the other windows as drawn with it at its zone, so a growing neighbour (Initiative) that grew into the room the squeeze freed is capped again and never blocks the return (walkthrough M9). A cause the user drags (now remembered) ends its squeeze at that `drag-end` (FR-SHELL-149). `Plan.refit?: "window"` when the zone went back over a PC with a `fitted` camera | FR-SHELL-159, FR-MAP-114 |
| Stored entry (`persistable(windows, memory)`, `shell-store.ts`) | A transient displacement is never written: a protected window squeezed for a launcher window (`window:`) and a window a tool bar pushed or put away (`toolbar:`), still where that put it, are written as they were before it (the page shows the displaced rect from memory). At `load` a squeeze is re-derived while an untouched launcher window still sits in the full zone and clears the squeezed rect (its cause again, remembered; `Restored.memory` keeps it); an entry stored squeezed by an older build with no such window loads at its zone. A tool bar doesn't survive a reload, so a window it pushed or put away is back where it was (walkthrough "other" 1) | FR-SHELL-102, -159 |
| BL-82 (`placement.ts`, `placer.ts`) | `placeMemo(tokens)`: one settle's token grids (nearest footprints, their rows and full-width columns, the spot cap's drop order) and PC boxes, keyed by (position, size, gap, heights), shared by every search of the settle; `roomFor(def, vp, input)`: exact "a clear spot of the smallest candidate size exists" (the corners against the padded blockers): the ladder skips a search whose answer is only used when clear (accepted, a yield). Same spots as before (goldens unchanged by it); `placeStats` = searches and grids built, printed by `placement.bench.ts` | FR-SHELL-158 |
| Manager (`manager.ts`) | `appear` fronts the window. `absorbEntries(registry, windows, vp)` at `restoreLayout`: an absorbed kind's entry (open or minimised) while the absorbing kind (same instance) is closed or missing gives it its `state` and `z`; geometry: the absorbing window's own rect if remembered, else (absorbed remembered) the absorbed x / y at the absorbing window's size with `userSpot`, else the `load` settle places it; absorbed entries are dropped (`migrated`: written); `Restored.absorbed` | FR-SHELL-148, -155 |
| Registry (`registry.ts`) | `absorbs?: readonly string[]`, `onAbsorb?(fromKind, you: You \| null)`: run once by the controller after the load that absorbed | FR-SHELL-155 |
| Controller (`shell-store.ts`) | Options `you()` (for `onAbsorb`), `onRefit()` (a `Plan.refit`: `ShellContext` → the canvas port's `yieldRefit`, the `window` refit); state `viewportSettles` (count of `viewport` settles) | FR-SHELL-155, FR-MAP-113, -114 |
| Exports for SP (seams S3) | `useResetLayout(): () => void` (`ShellContext.tsx`): FR-SHELL-153's reset, the same action as the launcher's "⋯" and Preferences → Layout (incl. the Fit). `isTypingTarget(target)` (`keys.ts`): true for a text-like `input`, `textarea`, `select`, contenteditable (or inside one) | FR-SHELL-165 |
| Canvas (`camera.ts`, `MapCanvas.tsx`) | `placerSettled(state, reason, view, since?)`: a resize refit also waits for `state.settles ≥ since`, `since` = the settle count at its resize event + 1 (the settle of this resize); `createSettledRefit(refit, ready, frames, hold?)`: while `hold()` (that settle not landed) it waits past `SETTLED_FIT_MAX_WAITS` up to `SETTLED_FIT_HOLD_MAX` (600 frames). Root cause of BL-86: after 60 frames the refit fit anyway on the previous size's windows, render-fit (1280×720: the 48-px floor, `pcs`). `useFreeArea()` adds `settles` | FR-MAP-113 |
| Brute oracle (`layout-checks.ts`) | Candidate x starts at `max(workArea.left, 68)` (BL-76); `layoutRun`'s protected pass: the moved window is remembered and stays under the protected one (on top) | FR-SHELL-166, -151 |

- **Harness** (`replay.ts`, path `memory` per set × camera × size, FR-SHELL-162): (1) focus and drag each open launcher window to a scripted spot (over a PC, over another window, at the right edge, partly off-screen; the first one resized); (2) close and reopen each; (3) reload; (4) resize down and back; (5) the tool bar shown, a reload while shown, the bar gone; (6) End, Start; (7) Reset; (8) one untouched open. Invariants: I1–I3 exempt remembered windows; I2 holds between untouched windows and keep-outs; I4: only untouched launcher windows are put away, LRU first; **I9** a remembered window's stored rect never changes except by Reset; **I10** its drawn rect = steps 3–4 (= the stored rect at the size it was set, no bar shown); **I11** after every open, focus or drag the touched window has the highest z; **I12** no remembered window is put away, squeezed or displaced; **I13** after Reset no entry has `userSpot` / `userSize` / `fixedH` and the layout = the named default. `runScript` gains `{pan: {x, y}}` (the camera `moved`). `replay.failing.json`: DM 8 → 8 (0 / 24 memory cells fail), players 21 → 22 (1 / 24: `P2 moved @1280×720 memory`, an approved named exemption: it inherits the shared open phase's I2, as every P2 moved @1280×720 cell).
- **Goldens:** `windows.golden.json` regenerated (DM: one step moved, by the squeeze hand-off); new `windows.memory.golden.json` per client (FR-SHELL-163): the scripted spots set at 1600×900 → the stored rect and the drawn rect at the four sizes.
- **Test hooks:** `ui-window` `data-remembered="true"`; `data-placed` `stored` / `pushed` for remembered windows; test mode `data-z` (z rank among mounted windows, 1 = bottom); `data-settle-log` `<trigger>:<kind>>remembered`.
- **Tests:** `play-shell/window-memory.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-144–155, -166), `reset-layout.test.tsx` and `keys.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-165), `placement.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-158), DM `play-shell/window-memory.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-159, FR-MAP-114, -115), `tool-bar-push.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-167), DM `scenes-map/resize-round-trip.test.ts` (FR-MAP-113); bench `FR-SHELL-119 settle` with the FR-SHELL-158 profile; E2E `e2e/window-memory.e2e.ts` on `pm-memory-e2e`.
- **Fixtures:** no unit; bundles `pm-memory` (walkthrough) and `pm-memory-e2e`, both `session-sim-12` under their own name.
- **CON-44:** 0 B.

## 152. Sprint 9: command palette and keyboard shortcuts (SP1)

From [play-shell](../specs/play-shell.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-SHELL-180–199 ([ADR-0067](../architecture/adr/0067-command-registry.md); #338, #339; seams S3, S9, S14; review-a rulings 11–15). **Built by `build/sp-palette`.** Client only: no intent, event, record, signal or stored server field (CON-44: 0 B). Commands send only intents the UI already sends (`cbt.nextTurn`, `cbt.prevTurn`, `camp.resume`), checked by the server as today; compendium rows come from `GET /api/compendium/search` (FR-CMP-10) with the page's own credential and visibility (FR-CMP-14).

| Seam | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| Registry (`play-shell/commands.ts`, append-only) | `COMMANDS` and `PROVIDERS` (types in `palette/types.ts`). `keys` only from play-shell §7's table (`SHORTCUT_TABLE`); a test fails on a duplicate per client, a key outside the table or a reserved key (`RESERVED_KEYS`: Space, Escape, Ctrl+D, Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Shift+Z, Ctrl+Y, Enter + modifiers, Tab, arrows, Backspace, Delete). `feature?` (ADR-0058): left out of this client's list while off (palette, sheet and keys). `when` → `true` / a reason string (shown disabled) / `false` (hidden); a throwing `when` hides. A window key (`window: kind`) shows its chip on the generated Windows row and is hidden while the kind is not available (R before SP2's `cmp.rules`) | FR-SHELL-184, -189, -191, -199 |
| `CommandCtx` (`palette/context.ts`) | `{client, store, live, registry, windows, available, shell: {openWindow, focusWindow, toggleWindow, resetLayout}, send, openPalette(prefix?), openShortcuts()}`; window actions dispatch `open` / `focus` / `toggle` with `by: "user"` (window memory and MRU apply; "Go to" on an open window only fronts it, S14); `resetLayout` = `useResetLayout()` (FR-SHELL-165); `send` = the connection's `sendIntent`; a reject toasts (`toastIntentError`) and resolves undefined | FR-SHELL-184, -185 |
| Overlay (`overlays.ts` entry `palette`, layer 5, no `blocksInput`) | `PaletteOverlay` mounts the one dispatcher (a capture-phase `keydown` listener on `window`, registered once, before any pause guard) and draws the palette (`Palette.tsx`) and the sheet (`ShortcutsSheet.tsx`) as Radix modal Dialogs inside the overlay layer (`data-palette`); a blocking overlay closes both; focus goes back to the element that had it. Not a window: no layout entry, no keep-out, no placer obstacle | FR-SHELL-180, -181 |
| Dispatcher (`palette/dispatch.ts` `decide`) | Ignores `repeat` / `isComposing`. A registry key → `paused` while a `blocksInput` overlay is up; a single key (no Ctrl / Alt / ⌘) → `typing` on a typing target (`isTypingTarget`) or inside a dialog / menu / listbox, `off` while single keys are off; a chord → `disabled` while a modal dialog / alert dialog other than the palette's is open (`modalDialogOpen`: an `alertdialog`, or an open `dialog` while Radix holds `body` pointer events); then `when` → `ran` (`preventDefault` + run) or `disabled`. Letters by `e.key` case-insensitively with Ctrl / Alt / Meta and Shift exact; punctuation by `e.key`, Shift ignored; `Mod` = Ctrl, ⌘ on macOS | FR-SHELL-190 |
| Ranking (`palette/rank.ts`) | Local rows (commands, generated windows, provider `items`) prepared once per store / layout change (`foldName(title)`, `textWordsOf(keywords)`); per keystroke `searchTier` (`@shared/search/match`); rows by tier, shorter title, title; groups by best tier, then group order; ≤ 8 a group, ≤ 40 in all. `>` commands only, `@` characters + creatures only; empty → Recent then Commands. Server rows (`search`, debounced 150 ms, only without a prefix and with a query; offline → no request) go after the local groups, Rules then Compendium (`kind = rule` → Rules), ≤ 8 each, server order kept | FR-SHELL-182, -183, -188 |
| Launch commands (`palette/launch.tsx`) | Both: Search and commands (Mod+K, toggles; no row), Keyboard shortcuts (`?`), Roll dice (`/`: opens `dice.log`, focuses `dice-roll-expression` once drawn, ≤ 1 s), Reset layout, Preferences, window keys L I D C B R P S. DM: Next / Previous turn (N / Shift+N: the Initiative buttons' payload `{encounterId, expectedRound, expectedTurnId}` and their skip / not-restored toasts; disabled "No fight started"), Pause… (clicks the top-bar `ui-pause-open`), Resume (`camp.resume`), Show awards (Stats view `{tab: session}` + open). Sending commands disabled "Not connected" unless `live`. C: a player's one character → that sheet (toggle); several, or the DM → the palette on `@`. Party status (PM2), Session recap (SP3) and Rules rows through `openRules` (SP2) are later lines | FR-SHELL-186, -187, -189 |
| Top bar (`top-bar-items.ts` entry `palette`, 40 px, order 90) | `PaletteTopBarButton` (lucide `Search`, Tooltip "Search and commands (Ctrl+K)"), right before the identity chip | FR-SHELL-195 |
| Preferences (`shell.keys` "Keyboard", order 70, both `pref-sections.ts`) | Switch `ui-prefs-keys-single` → `pp.keys.v1`; link "All shortcuts" opens the sheet | FR-SHELL-193 |

- **Test hooks:** `ui-palette` (`data-state` = `local` / `searching` / `ready` / `error` / `offline`), `ui-palette-input`, `ui-palette-group` (`data-value`), `ui-palette-item` (`data-record-id`, `data-value` = group, `data-kind` = compendium kind, `data-disabled`), `ui-palette-empty`, `ui-palette-retry`, `ui-palette-open`, `ui-shortcuts`, `ui-shortcuts-row` (`data-record-id`, `data-state` on / off / disabled), `ui-shortcuts-close`, `ui-prefs-keys-single`, `ui-prefs-keys-sheet`; test mode `body[data-test-last-shortcut]` = `<command id>:<ran | typing | paused | off | disabled>`.
- **Tests:** `palette/commands.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-184–188, -191, -194), `palette/palette.test.ts` (FR-SHELL-182, -183), `palette/shortcuts.test.tsx` (FR-SHELL-180, -181, -186, -189, -190, -192, -193, -195, -196); bench `palette/palette.bench.ts` (FR-SHELL-198: 5,000 rows p95 3.1 ms per keystroke, 1,100 rows 0.4 ms, prepare 11 ms); E2E `e2e/sp-palette.e2e.ts` on `sp-palette-e2e`.
- **Fixtures:** no unit; bundles `sp-palette` (the walkthrough until `sp-s9` lands) and `sp-palette-e2e`, both `session-sim-12` under their own name. `session-sim-13`: no line.

No intent, event, record type, signal or wire field (CON-44: 0 B). Data side: [data model §122](../data-model/README.md#122-sprint-9-party-manager-pm2).

PM2: `status` "Party status" 10, `handouts` 20, `inventory` 30, `loot` 40; RS: `reminders` 50, `strongholds` 60 | FR-PARTY-67 |

| Party status tab (`StatusTab.tsx` from `Summary.tsx`; rows `summary-logic.ts` `partySummary`) | PC rows as §145 (unchanged rules); each PC in a `party-companion-group` (+ `data-record-id` = PC id) followed by its companions in the companion row form (`Companions.tsx` `CompanionLine`): pets per FR-ENT-37 (DM and owner every pet; another player's only while that player holds a visible combatant or token for it) and owned combatants of the started encounter (not hidden / `ownerHidden`); DM `party-companion-hidden` (eye-off) on a pet hidden from players (its combatant hidden / `ownerHidden`, or out of a fight only hidden tokens on the active scene); owner + DM numbers and Card, others the band; DM menu on pets only (summons none). Owner PC not listed → `party-companion-other` "Other companions". DM header `party-companion-new` (New pet…). New: `party-tab` `data-value="status"`, `party-status` (tab body), `party-companion-other`, `party-companion-hidden`; `party-companion-menu` gains `data-locked` (in a fight).

## 154. Sprint 9: toast stack, kill feed, stats retry, load clock (FX1)

From [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-70–73, [stats](../specs/stats.md#62-sprint-9-fix-pack) FR-STATS-68–70 and [fixtures-adapters](../specs/fixtures-adapters.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-FIX-29 ([ADR-0068](../architecture/adr/0068-fixture-load-clock.md); #338, #339; review-b rulings 4, 13, 14, 18; integrator ruling B on FR-NOTE-53). **Built by `fix/fx-toasts-feed`**: no record type, intent, event or wire field.

| Seam | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| DM stack lefts (`keep-out.ts` `dmStackLefts`) | Home `(vw − 320) / 2` and the shift left of Initiative or of a window with `blocks` (this client's open `note.history`), only when left of home and ≥ `MIN_SHIFT_LEFT` 80 (rail 68 + 12). WF4's "beside a window over the home column" lefts are retired | FR-NOTE-70 |
| DM stack spot (`dmStackSpot`) | Clear = off Initiative and `blocks` windows (+ 12 px), off every window's bar strip, off hard keep-outs. Strips, pass 1: every window's title bar + 40 px (a pushed one: also its reported first row; ruling B). No clear spot in pass 1 → pass 2 with the sprint-8 strips (title bars; pushed windows' title + 40 + row). Ranking: a spot over no window body wins (least token cover, then the earlier spot); none → the clear spot with the smallest top. `covers` > 0 → the runtime folds toasts ("+N more"); never beside a window. Tops add `blocks` windows' bottom + 12 | FR-NOTE-70, -71, -72, -53 |
| Stack inputs (`stack-inputs.ts`) | `windows[i].blocks = true` for `note.history`; `useHistoryOpen()` = this client's `note.history` open (not minimised) and available. Remembered windows (window memory) are ordinary rendered windows here | FR-NOTE-72 |
| Fold (`toasts.ts` `visibleToasts(s, max, folded)`, `unfold`) | Folded: the summary and every card class toast neither show nor count; alerts, info and the callout do. On open → closed / minimised the runtime calls `unfold`: the summary is set again for pending rows without a toast; cards still on the stack show as they were (silent: no cue, no sound). A card the user closed stays closed; one resolved while folded is pruned as before. Both clients | FR-NOTE-72 |
| Callout node (`showCallout`, `Callout.seq`) | A page-wide counter per show; `note-callout` keyed by it, `data-callout-seq` = it; a replacement is a new node with its own 4 s | FR-NOTE-73 |
| Kill feed (`stats/feed.ts`) | A line toasts the first time this page sees it carry `fell` / `callout`, as a new line or an upsert of one already held (amends FR-STATS-28); undo / supersede upserts never. Repro (real sockets, `stats-b`): the DM's direct `hp` line and the card Apply's `cbt.attack` line both reach the DM as new lines with `fell`; the second callout reused the first's node (fixed by FR-NOTE-73) | FR-STATS-68 |
| Stats query (`stats/refresh.ts`) | A `conflict` answer: last result kept, no error state, one ask `CONFLICT_RETRY_MS` 1000 later with the latest input; a second `conflict` in a row → error ("Couldn't load stats" + Retry). A new input gets its own retry. Server unchanged (per viewer, not coalesced) | FR-STATS-69 |
| Empty fight copy (`stats/logic.ts` `noDamageYet(rows, highlights)`) | "No damage yet this fight" only when every row is 0 and Highlights hold no kill, down or Biggest overkill | FR-STATS-70 |
| Fixture load clock (`loader.ts` `LoaderOptions.now`, `loadAnchor`) | `scripts/fixtures.ts` passes `now: Date.now()` for `load` / `reset-load`; every fixture's seeded clock = `fixedClock(floor(now / 60 000) × 60 000)`, printed as a notice ("Fixture clock: …"). No `now` (`loadBundle`, `scripts/e2e.ts`) → `FIXTURE_INSTANT` | FR-FIX-29 |

- **Test hooks:** `note-callout` `data-callout-seq`; `note-toasts` `data-left` / `data-top` = the drawn style = the FR-SHELL-59 report.
- **Tests:** `keep-out.test.ts` (FR-NOTE-70 incl. the 500-layout property, -71, -72, `FR-NOTE-53 every window's first row keeps the stack off (ruling B)`), `toast-runtime.test.tsx` (FR-NOTE-70 drawn = computed, -72 keep clear + fold, -73), `toast-policy.test.ts` (FR-NOTE-72 model), `stats/feed.test.tsx` + `server/features/stats/feed-sync.test.tsx` (FR-STATS-68, real frames), `refresh.test.ts` (FR-STATS-69), `logic.test.ts` + `render.test.tsx` (FR-STATS-70), `fixtures/fixtures.test.ts` + `server/features/stats/load-clock.test.ts` (FR-FIX-29); e2e `fx-toasts.e2e.ts`.
- **Fixtures:** no unit; bundle `fx-toasts-e2e` (= `session-sim-12`).
- **CON-44:** 0 B.
