## 158. Sprint 9: rules quick reference (SP2)

From [compendium](../specs/compendium.md#61-sprint-9-rules-quick-reference-track-sp) FR-CMP-59–75 (#338, #339; review-a rulings 17–19; seams S3, S4, S14). **Built by `build/sp-rules`.** REST only: no intent, event, record type or signal (CON-44: 0 B). Data side: [data model §126](../data-model/README.md#126-sprint-9-rules-quick-reference-sp2).

| Route / shape | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| Kind `rule` | `COMPENDIUM_KINDS` + `rule`: facets `{chapter ≤ 40, section ≤ 60, order int 0–999}` (strict), body `{text}`; `sourceKey` `rule/<section-slug>/<card-slug>`; `line` "‹section› · ‹chapter›" (no section: the chapter). `ImportCounts` (in `CompendiumImported`) gains `rule`; `CompendiumSource.counts` is a partial record (a source imported before a kind existed has no count for it) | FR-CMP-59 |
| `GET /api/compendium/search` | Filters `chapter`, `section` with `kind=rule` (amends FR-CMP-11, §58). Empty `q` without `kind` skips `rule` (amends FR-CMP-36). Without `kind`, rule cards match on the **name** only (tiers 0–3), so a monster or item word never pulls in rules text that mentions it; `kind=rule` matches their text too (FR-CMP-33 as written) | FR-CMP-62 |
| `GET /api/compendium/rules` | DM / player (FR-CMP-13 auth). `{pages: [{id, title, cards: [{id, name, section}]}], attribution: SourceAttribution \| null}`: the six curated pages (`@shared/compendium/rules-pages.ts`) + `house`; Pinned is the client's. A page key resolves to the best-ranked visible entry with that `sourceKey` (FR-CMP-38); a key no entry has is skipped. Conditions' `section` = "Conditions". `attribution` = the bundled source row, null when not imported | FR-CMP-64, -65 |
| `GET /api/compendium/rules/:pageId` | `{id, cards: [{id, name, section, text, house, shared}]}` in page order; `house`: the campaign's homebrew `rule` entries the viewer may read (players: `shared` only), by folded name; `shared` false only in the DM's body. Unknown page (incl. `pinned`) → `404 not_found` | FR-CMP-65, -67 |
| Both rules routes | `Cache-Control: private, no-cache` (FR-CMP-12), `Vary: Authorization`. `ETag` = opaque hash of (bundled source `version`, viewer role, the campaign's homebrew-rule version = `count:newest updatedAt`, SHA-256 of the body): any house-rule create, edit, unshare or delete changes it, also on a fixed clock (review-a ruling 17); DM and player ETags differ. `If-None-Match` → `304` | FR-CMP-65 |
| `cmp.putEntry` (homebrew `rule`) | Writes `chapter` "House rules", `order` 0 whatever was sent; `section` free text ≤ 60 (may be empty) | FR-CMP-67 |

- **Adapter** (`adapter/rules.ts`, `ADAPTER_VERSION` 3 → 4, so the DM sees **Update**): Open5e `sections` (endpoint added to `OPEN5E_ENDPOINTS`) split at `##` / `###` (intro card named after the section; deeper headings stay inside; a name repeated in its section gets " (‹parent heading›)"; a heading with no text of its own is no card); text through `toMarkdownLite`, Open5e's `---` between words → "—". A section outside `RULE_SECTION_SLUGS` is skipped (counted). Bundled: 21 sections → **160 cards** (161 headings; "Types of Actions" has no text).
- **Recorder** (`sources/srd-5.1/record.ts [endpoint …]`, by hand, the only network use): `sections` keeps `wotc-srd` records whose slug is in `RULES_SECTIONS` (20, the pages') + `RULES_EXTRA` (`between-adventures`). `sections.json` 157 KB; `licence.test.ts` covers it.
- **Client:**
  - Window `cmp.rules` "Rules" ([§24](#24-combat-windows) row): both clients, launcher lucide `ScrollText` order 66 (after Compendium), default closed; 440 × 600 (short viewports 440 × 412; FR-CMP-63's 480 cut to FR-SHELL-61's width, see §24), min 340 × 320, `usefulSize` 420 × 360 ("page Tabs + 3 card headings"); fits 1280×720, no product overlap at 1600×900; in `GOLDEN_KINDS` (goldens regenerated). Replay sets D3 and P1 (untouched, S4), on PM2's lists: DM 8 → 10 (D3 fitted 1440×900 / 1600×900 resize), players 38 → 36 (1 new: P1 fitted 1600×900 resize; 3 fixed); the new ones are BL-93's resize round trip (approved: the player one a named exemption, folded into BL-93). Launcher labels (FR-SHELL-161, product launchers only, 6 px a label character; the re-test measured the player bar at 1010 px): DM from 1434 px (icons only at 1280), players from 1044 px (labels at 1280).
  - Body (`@ui/features/compendium/rules/`): page Tabs (wrapping) from a 420-px window width, a Select below; search Input → `kind=rule` + `kind=condition` (200 ms), merged, name tiers first; cards = Collapsibles (text rendered only when open; search results fetch `GET /entries/:id`); star → Pinned (`pp.rules.pins.v1.<campaignId>`, ≤ 30, 404 → dropped); footer "Rules text: SRD 5.1 · CC BY 4.0 · About" (About adds "Rules text from the SRD 5.1, split into cards at its headings and reformatted; wording unchanged."). The last good answer per page is kept for the page load (offline: readable; others "Offline").
  - `openRules(openWindow, entryId?)` / `useOpenRules()` (`rules/open.ts`): `openWindow("cmp.rules", undefined, {by: "user"})` and, with an id, its page (open, scrolled to) or alone in the search view. Open in Rules (`cmp-rule-open`) on rule entry views. Palette (`rules/palette.ts`, appended to `commands.ts`, ADR-0067): command `cmp.rules.open` "Open Rules" (both clients; keywords hold no card words, so "cover" reaches the Rules row) and provider `rules` (group Rules: compendium search `kind=rule`, text matches too, ≤ 8, server order; a row → `openRules(entryId)`). SP1's compendium provider leaves `rule` entries out (no duplicate rows).
  - Refetch (FR-CMP-23): every DM homebrew put / delete (`dm/features/compendium/homebrew-rest.ts`, from any window) and an import bump the Rules window's nonce, so it refetches at once.
  - DM: **New house rule** (`cmp-rules-new-house`) on House rules opens the homebrew form with kind `rule` (`FormMode {kind: "new", preset}`, `HomebrewDialog onSaved`); the form gains kind House rule with a Section field.
  - Test IDs (FR-CMP-74): `cmp-rules-window` (+ `data-value` page), `cmp-rules-page` (+ `data-value`), `cmp-rules-page-select`, `cmp-rules-search`, `cmp-rules-card` (+ `data-record-id`, `data-state` open / closed), `cmp-rules-pin` (+ `data-record-id`, `data-state` on / off), `cmp-rules-about`, `cmp-about-rules`, `cmp-rules-empty`, `cmp-rules-loading`, `cmp-rules-error` (+ `data-value` offline / error), `cmp-rules-retry`, `cmp-rules-back`, `cmp-rules-new-house`, `cmp-rule-open`.
- **Fixtures:** units `cmp-rules-mini` (mini sections: Actions in Combat, Attacking, Cover, Damage and Healing, Movement → 47 cards; re-imports `cmp-srd-mini` beside them at version `mini`, so only rule rows are written), `cmp-rule-house` ("Flanking" shared, "Critical fumbles" DM only); bundles `sp-rules` (= `session-sim-12` + both), `sp-rules-e2e` (`e2e/sp-rules.e2e.ts`); `session-sim-13` + `cmp-rules-mini`; `compendium-full` gains the bundled sections (1,310 entries).
- **CON-44:** 0 B (`FR-CMP-71`: the full import's 160 cards + two 4,000-char house rules, every viewer, heavy harness).

## 159. Sprint 9: session recap — `recap.query`, `recap.share`, `session-recap` kind (SP3)

From [session-recap](../specs/session-recap.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-RECAP-01–29 and [stats](../specs/stats.md#62-sprint-9-recap-reads-track-sp) FR-STATS-78–80 (#338, #339; review-a rulings 4, 5, 16, 20; seams S6–S8, S10). **Built by `build/sp-recap`**. Data side: [data model §127](../data-model/README.md#127-sprint-9-session-recap-sp3). No record type, event, signal or snapshot field.

**Intents** (none optimistic)

| Intent | Who | Rules | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| `recap.query {n?}` | DM, player | read-only (no write, event, log row or `seq`; answered in this connection's frame order); `n` in a session the viewer can list (stats' rule, absent = the newest; else `not_found`); **per actor ≤ 2 computed answers / s**: an over-limit query with a memo gets it (coalesced), with none → `conflict` "Too many recap queries, try again in a moment" (the client re-asks once after 1 s); never counts against `stats.query`'s budget | `{recap: {fromN, toN, from, to, label, fightsMore?, shared {note, omit, at} \| null, fights[≤ 12], party[], loot {rows[≤ 60], more?, totals {coins, items[]}}, rolls {rows[≤ 12], more?}, awards[9]} \| null}` per role (below) |
| `recap.share {fromN, note?, omit?}` | DM (player → `forbidden`) | `fromN` a session key (else `not_found`); `note` cleaned (control characters removed, trimmed), clipped to 280 code points; `omit` ⊆ {`party`, `loot`, `rolls`, `awards`} (else `invalid`); one transaction: a `session-recap` notification + its log row (crash seam `recap.share:before-commit` / `:after-commit`) | `{notificationId}` |

- **Per role** (`@shared/session-recap/schemas`: `DmRecap`, `PlayerRecap`, strict): players get public facts only, every player the same recap; fight `label` = stats' neutral "Fight N · ‹date›"; kills / downs never name a hidden side; no `hidden`, `standing`, `overkill`, HP, AC, `dm-dice` or hidden roll; the sections the newest share of the session omits are **absent**. The DM: encounter names, `hidden` marks (fell lists, rolls), overkill amounts, `standing` per fight, DM dice nat 20s / 1s; every section always (`shared.omit` marks the ones players don't see).
- **Sections:** fight `{key, label, status, rounds, duration (ms; null = running), mvp, kills, downed, standing (DM)}` (stats' Highlights over the whole fight); party row `{characterId, name, playerId, dealt, kills, downed, healed, nat20, nat1, avg}` (companions rolled into the owner, by Dealt); loot row `{id, kind: coins \| item, holder: party \| {character, characterId, name}, text, coins (signed: + gained, − spent), qty? (signed)}`: `world ↔ party / character` ledger rows with `from ≤ createdAt <` the next session's `from` (the newest: up to now); party ↔ character moves and **any row with a Stores end** left out (for the DM too); same for every viewer; roll row `{id (listing entry), name, natural: 20 \| 1, label, hidden (DM)}`; awards = stats' session awards.
- **Memo** (server memory, never persisted, LRU 32): key (session `fromN`, role, enabled features), all players one entry; fresh while (fact version, loot high-water) match. Fact version: bumped after a commit changing an `hp-change`, `combatant`, `encounter`, `entity`, `campaign`, a token's owner, an existing `game-log-entry` (undo, supersede) or adding a line with rolls (a line with no roll never bumps it). Loot high-water: max `seq` + max `editedAt` of the session's recap rows. Header and `shared` are read per query outside it.

| Notification kind | Rules | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `session-recap` *(new; `notifications/kinds.ts`)* | audience `all`, info, no action, `payloadMaxBytes` 2,048; payload `{fromN, toN, from, to, label, note?, omit}` (players' clipped times, "Tue 20 Oct", as `stats-awards`); text "Session recap: ‹label›"; made only by `recap.share`; the newest row of a session wins on view | FR-RECAP-20–23 |

- **Client** (both): window `recap.session` "Recap" (no launcher; 440 × 560, 1280×720 440 × 412, min 340 × 320, useful 400 × 360; replay sets D3 / P1 untouched). A live `session-recap` notification opens it `by: "feature"` on that session (players; paused: at resume; the DM only toasts) and re-queries an open Recap on that session at once; history **Open** the same; palette command `recap.open` "Session recap" (`by: "user"`, the newest session). Live refresh: a `game-log-entry` / `loot-entry` upsert re-queries the newest session 2 s later; a `conflict` is asked again once after 1 s, quietly. DM: Share dialog (`recap-share` → `recap-share-dialog`), Copy as Markdown; players: Copy as Markdown.
- **Session picker** (RC5): stats' `{scope: "sessions"}` list, plus the shown session while the list predates it; a recap answer the list doesn't cover (a missing session, a grown `toN`) asks the list again; a pick is kept by its `fromN` (never "the newest").
- **Stats seam** (FR-STATS-78, -79): `stats/compute.ts` exports `sessionFights(r, s, facts?, fights?)` and `sessionFactsOf(r, s)`; the Session tab's `stats-session-recap` "Recap" (both roles; DM after Show awards) opens `recap.session` `by: "user"` on the picked session, shown only while the Recap runtime (the window's companion) is mounted. Bench `recap.bench.ts` in `bun run bench` on `stats/testing.ts`'s `history`.
- **Fixtures:** units `recap-loot-haul` (`sp-s9` only), `recap-share-note`; bundles `sp-s9`, `sp-s9-shared`, `sp-s9-e2e`, `sp-s9-shared-e2e`.
- **CON-44** (`src/server/features/session-recap/con44.test.ts`, heavy harness): one max share (280 × 4-byte note, all sections omitted) +1,020 B per viewer; worst, every closed row of the notification window a max share: DM 793,503 B (+101,901), players ≤ 390,462 B (+101,901), both under the ceilings. Acks (FR-RECAP-18, bench at 100 sessions): DM ≈ 7.3 KB, player ≈ 6.5 KB; compute DM 23 ms / player 31 ms; memo hit < 1 ms.

## 160. Sprint 10: the DM toast stack keeps off window content and modal dialogs (H3)

From [notifications](../specs/notifications.md#6-functional-requirements) FR-NOTE-75–77 (BL-101, DM #341; walkthrough H3 re-test; amends §154's stack spot and FR-NOTE-53 ruling B). **Built by `fix/h3-toasts`**: client only; no record type, intent, event or wire field.

| Seam | Contract | FR |
|---|---|---|
| Window content (`stack-inputs.ts` `contentEnd`, `StackWindow.used`) | While the DM stack shows, each open window's `used` = its rendered top + how far below its section top the body's drawn boxes end (leaf elements and `[data-record-id]` rows, document order, clipped to the body; the walk stops at the first box reaching the body's bottom). Nothing drawn → the title bar. Re-read on every stack input change and, once a frame, on a `childList` change inside any `ui-window-body`. Absent `used` → the whole window counts as content | FR-NOTE-75 |
| DM stack spot (`keep-out.ts` `dmStackSpot`) | Strips per window, pass by pass (Initiative, `blocks` windows + 12 px and hard keep-outs block in every pass): (1) title bar to `used`; (2) `max(title + 40, first-row report)` for **every** window; (3) ruling B (title + 40; pushed: (2)); (4) title bars (pushed: (2)). The first pass with a clear spot decides, ranked per FR-NOTE-71; pass 4 keeps §154's fallback. `covers` = area over windows' content parts (title bar to `used`) | FR-NOTE-75 |
| Slide, fold, fallback (`dmStackSpot(…, dialogs, pcs)`, `besideSpot`, `MAX_SLIDE`; `toasts.ts` `visibleToasts`) | Order: (1) column, pass 1; (2) slide: lefts beside each window / Initiative / hard keep-out (± 12 px), ≥ `MIN_SHIFT_LEFT`, inside the viewport, \|left − home\| ≤ `MAX_SLIDE` 160; the spot overlaps no window, hard keep-out, `pcs` (PC token footprints + `PC_MARGIN` 4, from the store's PC tokens via `tokenScreenBoxes`) or card target, and Initiative / `blocks` + 12; nearest home (\|Δleft\| + Δtop); (3) column, passes 2–4 together, ranked [controls under it, lowest pass whose own tops hold it, body, top or token area]. Fold: `covers` > 0 lowers the runtime's cap to 0 while the summary shows (`visibleToasts(s, 0)` → summary + alerts + "+N more"), else to 1 | FR-NOTE-77 |
| First rows, controls (`stack-inputs.ts` `firstRowEnd`, `ROW_MAX_H` 120) | One walk per open body (subtrees with a box outside the body skipped): `head` = rendered top + the first `[data-record-id]` box ≤ 120 px tall inside the body (none → title + 40); `controls` = visible `button, input, select, textarea, a[href]` and `role` button / tab / checkbox / switch / combobox / slider boxes ≥ 4 × 4, clipped to the body, offset onto the rendered rect. `head` replaces the frame's first-row report for measured windows | FR-NOTE-77 |
| Modal dialogs (`dmStackSpot(…, dialogs)`, `dialog-rects.ts`) | The runtime passes open modal dialogs' rects apart (no longer merged into `hard`). Any present → placed against the dialogs alone, each as a `blocks` window (shift left of it ≥ 80, below + 12, above − 12); windows, Initiative, hard keep-outs and tokens are ignored (under the overlay). `dialogRoom` = the full column when the shift fits, else the larger band above or below (T4) | FR-NOTE-76 |

- **Tests:** `keep-out.test.ts` (`FR-NOTE-75 keep off window bodies' first rows`: sim-13 h07, h08, h09, f04; `FR-NOTE-76 an open modal dialog counts like Initiative`: b07; `FR-NOTE-77 …`: re-test 1b, 2, 4; amended FR-NOTE-53, -42 / -70 property and FR-NOTE-50 dialog room), `stack-inputs.test.ts` (`FR-NOTE-75 content end`, `FR-NOTE-77 first rows`), `toast-policy.test.ts` (`FR-NOTE-77 fold to the summary`); e2e `h3-toasts.e2e.ts` (1280×720, 1440×900, test cards the DM sends itself).
- **Fixtures:** no unit; bundle `h3-toasts-e2e` (= `session-sim-13`).
- **CON-44:** 0 B.
